### [1] [STRUCTSURVEY: Structured Agentic Retrieval for Automated Survey Paper Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01243)
*Paolo Pedinotti,Enrico Santus*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: STRUCTSURVEY是一个分层多智能体框架，通过动态构建基于图谱的实体、关系和主题分类表示，将结构推理从生成阶段转移到检索阶段，从而提升自动文献综述生成的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着科学出版物数量激增，跟踪和综合研究进展变得日益困难。现有LLM支持的自动综述生成方法检索非结构化数据，需要在生成时让模型从原始文本中推断概念、方法和分类关系，导致生成质量受限。

Method: 提出STRUCTSURVEY分层多智能体框架，在检索阶段动态构建实体、关系和主题分类的图结构表示，将结构推理从生成阶段转移到检索阶段。在ACL会议论文的引用基准数据集上进行评估。

Result: 相比仅使用嵌入检索的基线方法，STRUCTSURVEY在ROUGE-1召回率上平均提升+2.9，ROUGE-2召回率平均提升+1.0，且未降低精确率。LLM-as-a-Judge评估中，在逻辑结构、深度和综合能力方面均有提升。

Conclusion: 显式的结构检索能够生成更接近人工撰写的组织和推理方式的文献综述，验证了将结构推理前置到检索阶段的有效性。

Abstract: The rapid growth of scientific publications makes it increasingly difficult to track and synthesize research progress. While Large Language Models (LLMs) can support automated survey generation, existing methods retrieve unstructured data and require models to infer conceptual, methodological, and taxonomic relations from raw text at generation time. We introduce STRUCTSURVEY, a hierarchical multi-agent framework that shifts structural reasoning from generation to retrieval by dynamically constructing graph-based representations of entities, relations, and topical taxonomies. We evaluate STRUCTSURVEY on a new reference-grounded benchmark of ACL survey papers for reproducible long-form scientific summarization. Compared with embedding-only retrieval baselines, STRUCTSURVEY improves ROUGE-1 recall by +2.9 and ROUGE-2 recall by +1.0 on average, without reducing precision. It also improves LLM-as-a-Judge ratings for logical structure, depth, and synthesis, showing that explicit structural retrieval yields surveys closer to human-written organization and reasoning.

</details>


### [2] [Retrieval-Augmented Generation to Support Railways Engineering Tasks: A Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01244)
*Andrea Gerardo Russo,Federico Ruggeri,Ivan Tomarchio,Davide Bombini,Nicolò Donati,Gianmarco Pappacoda,Paolo Torroni,Giuseppe-Emiliano La Cara*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文详细介绍了一个面向铁路领域复杂技术法规咨询的检索增强生成（RAG）系统从设计到部署的案例研究，展示了在受监管行业中平衡技术能力与领域专业知识的人本化实施路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 技术法规的数量和复杂性日益增长，给受监管行业的专业人员带来了巨大挑战，亟需一种能够准确检索复杂文档并确保法规合规性的高效解决方案。

Method: 采用检索增强生成（RAG）系统架构，以人类为中心的设计方法，结合LLM技术能力与领域专业知识，从设计到部署进行全流程案例研究。

Result: 成功构建了面向铁路领域复杂技术法规咨询的RAG系统，为受监管行业提供了可借鉴的工业经验，实现了技术文档咨询的准确性和合规性。

Conclusion: 该RAG系统不仅适用于铁路领域，其经验对任何需要法规合规性和复杂文档准确检索的技术领域都具有重要价值，为各类受监管行业实施基于LLM的技术文档咨询提供了人本化的参考框架。

Abstract: The growing number and complexity of technical regulations represent an important challenge for all professionals in regulated industries. This paper describes a case study, from design to deployment, of building a Retrieval-Augmented Generation system for the consultation of complex technical regulations in the railway domain. Although developed for the railway sector, this testimony of an industrial experience is of particular value for technical domains where regulatory compliance and accurate information retrieval from complex documentation are essential requirements. It also constitutes a human-centered approach for implementing LLM-powered technical documentation consultation across various regulated industries, balancing technological capabilities with domain expertise.

</details>


### [3] [Bi-NAS: Towards Effective and Personalized Explanation for Recommender Systems via Bi-Level Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01387)
*Longfeng Wu,Yao Zhou,Tong Zeng,Zhimin Peng,Bhanu Pratap Singh Rawat,Lecheng Zheng,Giovanni Seni,Dawei Zhou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出Bi-NAS框架，通过神经架构搜索优化推荐系统的解释生成，结合大语言模型零样本提示，在四个数据集上同时提升了推荐准确性和解释效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统的解释对用户参与度、信任和决策至关重要，但现有方法在不同场景下评估解释有效性存在挑战，且缺乏对解释生成过程的系统性优化。

Method: 提出Bi-NAS框架，在层内和层间设计空间中同时优化交叉注意力机制和特征交互函数；集成大语言模型通过零样本提示生成个性化解释；对齐用户特征偏好与物品质量分数。

Result: 在四个真实数据集上，Bi-NAS不仅提升了推荐准确性，还显著提高了推荐系统解释的有效性，为用户提供了清晰可靠的推荐洞察。

Conclusion: Bi-NAS通过双层架构搜索和LLM增强，有效解决了推荐系统解释生成和评估的难题，实现了推荐准确性与解释效果的同步提升。

Abstract: Recommender systems are vital in helping users navigate vast amounts of information, offering personalized suggestions and effective explanations for these recommendations. While previous efforts have attempted to provide such explanations, evaluating their effectiveness across various scenarios remains a challenge. Enhancing these explanations is essential for improving user engagement, trust, and decision-making. To facilitate effective explanations within the recommender system, we propose a Bi-level Neural Architecture Search (Bi-NAS) framework to optimize explanations. This approach simultaneously refines cross-attention mechanisms and feature interaction functions by exploring both intra-layer and inter-layer design spaces. Furthermore, we integrate Large Language Models (LLMs) to enhance explanation generation, leveraging zero-shot prompting to produce more effective and personalized justifications. By aligning user feature preferences with item quality scores, our approach ensures that explanations reflect both user intent and item attributes, improving transparency and reasoning depth. Extensive evaluations on four real-world datasets demonstrate that Bi-NAS not only boosts recommendation accuracy but also significantly improves the effectiveness of explanations for recommender systems, providing users with clear and reliable insights into the suggestions they receive.

</details>


### [4] [CoPersona: Collaborative Persona Graphs for Robust LLM Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01485)
*Yangtian Zhang,Leyao Wang,Hiren Madhu,Ngoc Bui,Walter Roznyatovskiy,Rex Ying*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: CoPersona提出了一种基于图的协作个性化框架，通过从行为相似用户中借调信号来补全稀疏用户画像，利用多面表示分解和多层人格图建模用户间面级对齐，并采用双分支架构结合非参数同伴检索与参数图推理，在多个领域和模型规模上实现了稳健的LLM个性化改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的LLM个性化面临用户历史数据稀疏且偏斜的问题，大多数用户仅有少量交互记录，导致弱观测的用户属性难以推断，测试时请求转向未被充分支持的方面时个性化效果脆弱。

Method: CoPersona将交互历史分解为多个面级表示，通过多层人格图显式建模用户间的面级对齐；采用双分支架构，结合非参数同伴检索与参数化图推理，在推理时有效利用同伴信息。

Result: 在多个领域和不同模型规模上的实验表明，CoPersona相较于强基线方法取得了一致且显著的改进。

Conclusion: CoPersona通过图协作方式有效解决了稀疏用户画像下的鲁棒个性化问题，验证了跨用户信号借调对于LLM个性化的有效性。

Abstract: Real-world LLM personalization is often constrained by sparse and skewed user histories: most users provide only a handful of interactions, while even frequent users' logs capture an incomplete and biased view of their preferences. As a result, weakly observed user attributes are difficult to infer, leading to brittle personalization when test-time requests shift toward under-supported facets. Motivated by this limitation, we present CoPersona, a graph-based collaborative personalization framework that completes sparse user profiles by borrowing signals from behaviorally similar peers. However, directly transferring signals is difficult because uneven facet coverage introduces bias into interaction histories, obscuring user similarity in the unstructured global space. To address this issue, CoPersona decomposes interaction histories into multiple facet-level representations and explicitly models peer-to-peer, facet-level alignment through a multiplex persona graph. To effectively leverage peer information at inference time, we employ a dual-branch architecture that combines non-parametric peer retrieval with parametric graph reasoning. Experiments across multiple domains and model scales demonstrate consistent improvements over strong baselines, validating CoPersona as an effective approach for robust LLM personalization.

</details>


### [5] [IntentTune: Using user demand and personalization to resolve "unknown" query intents for e-commerce search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01530)
*Rachith Aiyappa,Ishita Khan,Chester Palen-Michel,Jayanth Yetukuri,Samarth Agrawal,Mehran Elyasi,Shuang Zhou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: IntentTune是一个解决电商搜索中模糊查询意图的框架，利用用户个性化行为信号（搜索历史、浏览活动等）来推断潜在意图（性别、年龄组等），实验表明用户特定信号优于群体级统计信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商搜索中大量查询（如"watch"、"shirt"）缺乏明确属性（性别、年龄等），导致查询意图检测困难，影响下游检索相关性。

Method: 提出IntentTune框架，利用两种信号源：(1)用户特定行为信号（搜索历史、浏览活动、画像属性）；(2)群体级需求模式。通过真实电商数据实验比较两者效果。

Result: 群体级需求模式不足以可靠推断模糊查询意图；用户特定行为信号（尤其历史搜索查询）在推断性别、年龄组、产品类别和尺寸意图方面优于群体级统计和静态画像信息。

Conclusion: 用户个性化的行为信号对解决电商搜索中查询意图模糊问题更为有效，为实际搜索系统优化提供了可落地方案。

Abstract: Understanding user intent is fundamental to delivering relevant search results in e-commerce. However, substantial fraction of real-world queries are under-specified (e.g., "watch" or "shirt"), lacking explicit attributes such as gender or age group. This ambiguity poses a significant challenge for query intent detection models in e-commerce search systems, which must accurately infer latent user intent (e.g., age, gender) to support effective downstream retrieval. We introduce IntentTune, a framework for resolving ambiguous or under-specified query intents by leveraging either (1) user-specific behavioral signals including search history, browsing activity, and profile attributes or (2) population-level demand patterns aggregated across all users. Through experiments on real-world e-commerce data, we first demonstrate that population-level demand patterns alone are insufficient to reliably infer intent in under-specified queries. We then demonstrate that user-specific behavioral signals -- particularly prior search queries -- outperform both population-level statistics and static profile information for inferring gender, age group, product category, and size intent from underspecified queries.

</details>


### [6] [Evaluating Chunking Strategies for Retrieval-Augmented Generation on Academic Texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01852)
*Valentin J. J. Kreileder,Johannes Reisinger,Andreas Fischer*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本研究评估了基于聚类的语义分块方法在RAG系统中对长篇幅结构化学术论文的检索和答案质量影响，发现该方法未优于简单的固定大小或递归分块策略，且RAGAs faithfulness评估在该场景下可靠性有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索聚类语义分块是否能提升RAG系统对长篇幅结构化文档（如学术论文）的检索和问答质量，以改进现有分块策略的不足。

Method: 对比聚类语义分块与固定大小分块、递归分块三种策略，在长结构化学术论文上使用RAGAs框架评估检索和答案质量。

Result: 聚类语义分块未优于简单分块策略；RAGAs的faithfulness指标在该场景下可靠性有限；固定长度与文档特定问题的性能差异大，可能与文档格式化和预处理有关。

Conclusion: 在当前配置下，聚类语义分块未能带来性能提升，RAGAs评估框架的faithfulness指标在长结构化文档场景中可靠性有待改进。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems use the question-answering capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs) to access information outside their parameters. We evaluate if cluster-based semantic chunking improves retrieval and answer quality compared to fixed-size and recursive chunking evaluating on long, structured academic theses using the Retrieval Augmented Generation Assessment (RAGAs) framework. RAGAs based faithfulness shows limited reliability in this setup. Performance on fixed versus document specific questions varied substantially, likely related to the formatting of documents and preprocessing. Under the tested configuration, cluster-based chunking did not outperform simpler strategies.

</details>


### [7] [Planning over Matrix-Factorization MDPs for Candidate Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02115)
*Mikhail Trapeznikov,Maksim Utushkin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文将Top-K检索建模为MDP，利用隐式ALS后验的闭式fold-in更新作为状态转移，通过少量规划（尤其是单步前瞻）在固定协同过滤嵌入上提升检索效果，无需重训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答一个窄问题：在推荐系统中，考虑fold-in导致的用户状态动态变化进行规划，何时是有价值的？标准矩阵分解检索忽略这一动态，仅构建单一用户向量并返回静态评分最高的K个独立物品。

Method: 将Top-K检索建模为MDP：状态为隐式ALS后验(A⁻¹,u)，动作为物品，转移为闭式秩1 fold-in更新；轨迹奖励结合相关相似度与后验对齐项。在固定嵌入下比较静态检索、单步规划和horizon-K MCTS。

Result: 在五个数据集上，动态感知规划在leave-last-n分割下优于静态检索；在MovieLens-1M和VK-LSVD切片上的全局时间分割下增益保持。单步前瞻即可捕获大部分增益。增益依赖于使用余弦相似度而非内积相似度。

Conclusion: 轻量级规划层将静态Top-K评分转化为短决策过程，在不重训练、不改变表示的前提下提升了固定协同过滤嵌入的检索效果。

Abstract: For a recommender service, we view the customer journey as a chain of item recommendations: a useful item changes the user's state and therefore what should be retrieved next. Standard matrix-factorization retrieval ignores this -- it builds one user vector and returns the top-$K$ items by a static score, treating them as independent. We ask a narrow question: when is it worth planning over the user-state dynamics that fold-in induces? To answer it we propose casting top-$K$ retrieval as an MDP over the implicit-ALS posterior $(A^{-1},u)$, where an action is an item and the transition is a closed-form rank-one fold-in, and the trajectory reward combines a relevance similarity with a posterior-alignment term. Under the same fixed embeddings we compare static retrieval, one-step planning, and horizon-$K$ MCTS across five datasets and two protocols: a per-user leave-last-$n$ split and a stricter global time split. Dynamics-aware planning tends to overcome static retrieval on all datasets under leave-last-$n$, and the gains hold on MovieLens-1M and the VK-LSVD slices under the global time split. A single step of lookahead already captures most of the gain, so the lightweight planning layer turns static top-$K$ scoring into a short decision and improves retrieval over fixed collaborative-filtering embeddings, with no retraining and no change to the representation. These gains depend on measuring relevance with cosine rather than inner-product similarity, which is otherwise entangled with item popularity.

</details>


### [8] [Bringing Agentic Search to Earth Observation Data Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02387)
*Minghan Yu,Youran Sun,Chugang Yi,Yixin Wen,Haizhao Yang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向地球科学领域的智能搜索系统，利用知识图谱和大语言模型，将自然语言研究查询映射到匹配的数据集和工具。通过构建NASA-EO-Bench基准（47k查询-数据集对），微调神经评分器，并结合BM25分数融合，Recall@10和MRR提升超5倍。在此基础上加入零样本智能重排序阶段，MRR再提升28%，证明LLM推理与监督检索互补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NASA及其数据中心持有数千个地球科学数据集和工具，但即使领域专家也难以快速准确地找到所需资源。现有搜索方式难以满足自然语言研究查询的需求。

Method: 1) 从NASA EO-KG知识图谱衍生出NASA-EO-Bench基准（47k查询-数据集对，含21k基于任务的查询）；2) 微调神经评分器；3) 通过分数融合将神经评分器与BM25结合；4) 添加零样本智能重排序阶段（基于LLM推理）。

Result: 神经评分器优于余弦相似度和BM25基线；与BM25分数融合后，Recall@10和MRR提升超5倍；零样本智能重排序在N=200子集上MRR再提升28%。

Conclusion: 在大语言模型时代，知识图谱的潜在价值可通过智能搜索得到显著放大；LLM推理与监督检索方法具有互补性。

Abstract: NASA and its data centers hold thousands of geoscience datasets and tools like Worldview, Giovanni, the Science Discovery Engine, and Harmony. Finding the right one is hard even for domain experts. We present an agentic search system, deployed as a public service for the geoscience community, that takes a natural-language research query and returns the matching datasets and tools. We demonstrate that, in the era of large language models, the latent value of knowledge graphs (KGs) can be substantially amplified through agentic search. From the NASA Earth Observation Knowledge Graph (NASA EO-KG) we derive NASA-EO-Bench, an open benchmark of 47k query-dataset pairs (21k task-based queries). A neural scorer fine-tuned on NASA-EO-Bench beats cosine and BM25 baselines. Further combining it with BM25 via score fusion raises both Recall@10 (R@10) and MRR by over 5x. On top of this supervised pipeline, we add a zero-shot agentic reranking stage that, without any additional training, lifts MRR by 28% on a stratified N=200 subset, showing that LLM reasoning is complementary to supervised retrieval.

</details>

### [9] [Benchmarking Code Improvement with Progressive, Adaptive, and Interactive Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01360)
*Cuong Chi Le,Aashish Yadavally,Minh Le-Anh,Tien N. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: PAIR-Bench 是一个用于评估大语言模型代码改进能力的渐进式自适应基准，通过结构化反馈协议（progressive hinting）和两阶段控制机制，从修复目标、泛化能力、行为保持及所需辅助程度等多维度细粒度评估模型的代码优化轨迹，弥补了传统二元功能性正确性评估的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的代码生成和程序修复评估仅使用二元功能性正确性（通过/不通过测试套件），忽略了模型在改进代码过程中的部分进展、反馈利用、回归问题以及优化轨迹，无法细粒度衡量模型的代码改进能力。

Method: 提出 PAIR-Bench 基准，采用 progressive hinting（渐进式提示）结构化反馈协议，包含两个控制维度：1）Failure-region control（失败区域控制）——通过将隐藏的失败测试分组为失败场景来确定反馈目标；2）Hint-depth control（提示深度控制）——控制修复相关信息揭示的程度，从粗略症状到实现级指导。

Result: 通过评估修复轨迹而非仅最终通过/失败结果，PAIR-Bench 提供了对 LLM 代码改进能力更细粒度的评估，能够测量模型是否修复了目标失败、是否能在提示信息之外进行泛化、是否保持已正确的行为，以及需要多大程度的辅助。

Conclusion: PAIR-Bench 通过渐进式反馈协议和多维度评估指标，克服了传统二元评估的局限性，为 LLM 代码改进能力提供了更全面、更精细的评估方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are typically evaluated on code generation and program repair using binary functional correctness: a generated program or patch either passes or fails a test suite. This protocol is simple but coarse, as it ignores partial progress, feedback use, regressions, and the refinement trajectory through which models often improve code. We introduce PAIR-Bench, a progressive and adaptive benchmark for evaluating code improvement: transforming an incorrect or incomplete program into a more correct one through feedback-guided refinement. PAIR-Bench uses progressive hinting, a structured feedback protocol with two controls. Failure-region control determines what the feedback targets by grouping hidden failing tests into failure scenarios, while hint-depth control determines how much repair-relevant information is revealed, from coarse symptoms to implementation-level guidance. This design enables PAIR-Bench to measure whether a model repairs targeted failures, generalizes beyond the hint, preserves already-correct behavior, and how much assistance it requires. By evaluating repair trajectories progressive metrics rather than only final pass/fail outcomes, PAIR-Bench provides a finer-grained assessment of LLM code-improvement capability.

</details>


### [10] [A global predicted-fMRI drive signal from TRIBE does not predict YouTube replay heatmaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01400)
*Barada Sahu,Shivesh Pandey*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文测试了深度多模态脑编码模型（TRIBE，2025年Algonauts挑战赛冠军）预测的神经信号能否预测视频观看行为。结果发现，模型预测的皮层响应与YouTube“最常回看”热力图之间的相关性几乎为零（偏相关+0.058，p=0.23），且不优于简单的响度和运动基线。该零结果在多种皮层网络读取方式和自相关保留置换检验下均成立。音乐视频中报告的适度相关性被归因于体裁特定的伪影，不具有泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证深度多模态脑编码模型预测的神经信号是否能够预测自然视频观看过程中的行为参与度（即观众回看行为）。

Method: 使用2025年Algonauts脑编码挑战赛冠军模型TRIBE（Llama-3.2 + V-JEPA2 + Wav2Vec-BERT）处理48个YouTube视频，将预测的皮层响应通过全局场功率降维为每秒的参与度曲线，并与YouTube的“最常回看”热力图进行相关分析。使用了位置控制偏相关、自相关保留置换检验等多种统计方法，并对比了响度和运动基线。

Result: 模型预测信号与回看行为之间的偏相关为+0.058（95% CI [-0.04, 0.15]；t(47)=1.21, p=0.23），与零无显著差异，且不优于简单的响度（+0.04）和运动基线。音乐视频中的中等相关性被证实为体裁特定的开头/起始回放伪影，不具泛化性。零结果在六种皮层网络读取方式和自相关保留置换检验下均保持一致。

Conclusion: 当前最先进的深度多模态脑编码模型虽然能高精度预测fMRI对自然视频的响应，但其预测的神经信号并不能有效预测视频观看者的行为参与度（回看行为）。这表明神经活动预测与行为预测之间存在差距，脑编码模型的优化目标可能需要进一步与行为预测对齐。

Abstract: Deep multimodal brain-encoding models now predict fMRI responses to naturalistic video with high accuracy. Whether their predicted neural signals also forecast behavioral engagement is unknown. We run TRIBE, the winning model of the 2025 Algonauts brain-encoding challenge (Llama-3.2 + V-JEPA2 + Wav2Vec-BERT), on 48 YouTube videos and reduce its predicted cortical response to a per-second engagement curve, the global field power. Correlated against each video's "most replayed" heatmap, a passively-collected proxy for which moments viewers return to, the curve shows no evidence of predicting re-watch behavior. The pooled position-controlled partial correlation is +0.058 (95% CI [-0.04, 0.15]; one-sample t(47)=1.21, p=0.23), indistinguishable from zero and not significantly above simple loudness and motion baselines (loudness +0.04, paired p=0.74). The raw correlation is also near zero; the moderate values reported for music videos reflect a genre-specific intro/onset-replay artifact rather than content prediction, and do not generalize. The null holds across six cortical-network readouts and under an autocorrelation-preserving permutation test. We release the code, the video-ID manifest, and an acquisition method that works despite YouTube's SABR-only streaming.

</details>


### [11] [GPUAlert: A Zero-Instrumentation Process-Boundary Monitor for Diagnosing GPU Training-Job Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01409)
*Parv Agarwal,Asif Ekbal*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: GPUAlert 是一个命令行包装器，可在不修改训练脚本的情况下监控 GPU 训练任务，通过进程边界拦截实现失败原因分类、持久化日志和结构化邮件通知，在474个真实GPU训练日志上达到0.997 macro-F1的失败分类准确率，额外开销仅约3ms/任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU训练任务在大规模生产集群上故障率极高（约40%），但运维人员通常需要数小时后重新连接才能发现故障。现有方案如实验追踪器需要修改训练脚本并维护云连接，调度器的邮件钩子仅能提供单行状态且无日志，缺乏一个无需修改训练代码、能自动捕获失败原因和日志的轻量级工具。

Method: GPUAlert 是一个进程边界上的命令行包装器，围绕三个可靠性原语设计：(1) 启动前日志保证——在子进程启动前建立持久化日志目标；(2) 通知器隔离——使包装器的退出码仅取决于子进程状态，与邮件发送成功与否无关；(3) 非静默工件预算——限制附件大小时不会静默丢弃输出。此外构建了一个包含474个GPU训练日志、覆盖15种失败类别的标注语料库和可复现评估框架。

Result: 在12种硬件可复现的失败类别上，有序规则分类器达到0.997 macro-F1，而无序关键字匹配为0.830，退出码检查仅为0.133。包装器开销恒定约3ms/任务；启动前保证能在shell重定向无输出的情况下保留日志；在所有15种失败模式下，即使SMTP中继不可达，包装器也能返回与子进程一致的退出码。

Conclusion: GPUAlert 提供了一种零侵入、高可靠的GPU训练故障监控方案，其核心贡献在于三个进程隔离层面的可靠性原语，以及高精度的失败分类能力。该工具在开销极小的情况下显著优于传统退出码检查和简单关键字匹配方法。

Abstract: GPU training jobs fail often, roughly two in five on large production clusters, yet the operator typically learns of a failure only by reconnecting hours later. Experiment trackers require editing the training script and maintaining a cloud connection; the scheduler's mail hook delivers a single status line with no cause and no logs. GPUAlert is a command-line wrapper that monitors any training command at the process boundary, and with no change to that command, emails a structured notification on completion carrying a classified failure cause, durable logs, and output artifacts. The tool is organized around three reliability primitives: a pre-launch log guarantee that establishes the durable destination before the child process can crash, notifier isolation that makes the wrapper's exit code a pure function of the child's status regardless of whether the email succeeds, and a non-silent artifact budget that bounds attachment size without ever dropping output silently. We release a labelled corpus of 474 GPU training logs across 15 failure classes and a reproducible evaluation harness. On the twelve hardware-reproduced classes, the ordered-rule classifier reaches 0.997 macro-F1, against 0.830 for unordered keyword matching and 0.133 for exit-code inspection. Wrapper overhead is a constant approximately 3ms per job; the pre-launch guarantee preserves a log where a shell redirect yields nothing; and across all 15 failure modes the wrapper returns the child's exit code unchanged even when the SMTP relay is unreachable.

</details>


### [12] [Adoption and Impact of Command-Line AI Coding Agents: A Study of Microsoft's Early 2026 Rollout of Claude Code and GitHub Copilot CLI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01418)
*Emerson Murphy-Hill,Jenna Butler,Alexandra Savelieva*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 对微软2026年初数万名工程师采用CLI编码代理（如Claude Code和Copilot CLI）的研究发现：首次使用主要通过社交网络传播；留存率与编码活跃度相关而非人口统计学特征；采用者合并PR数量提升约24%，且效果在四个月内持续。组织应将可见的同伴使用作为推广策略核心。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织在规模化推广代理型CLI工具时需要了解谁会尝试、谁会持续使用、以及工具输出是否值得成本。Token消耗可达每年数百万美元，误判采用率、留存率或影响会导致高成本投入却无法提升工程效率。

Method: 对微软2026年初数万名工程师进行实证研究，分析CLI编码代理（Claude Code和Copilot CLI）的采用、留存和产出影响。以合并PR数量作为产出代理指标，采用纵向数据分析首次使用传播、留存关联因素及因果效应。

Result: 首次使用主要通过社交网络传播；留存与编码活跃度相关而非人口统计学特征；采用者合并PR数量提升约24%，且效果在四个月窗口期内持续。

Conclusion: CLI编码代理既非均匀采用也非短暂新奇效应；组织应将可见的同伴使用作为推广策略的核心要素。

Abstract: Organizations rolling out agentic command line tools like Anthropic's Claude Code and GitHub's Copilot CLI need to know who will try them, who will keep using them, and whether the tools produce enough output to justify their cost. At organizational scale, token spend can run into millions of dollars annually, so misreading adoption, retention, or impact can make a rollout expensive without changing engineering velocity. Studying tens of thousands of engineers at Microsoft over its early-2026 rollout, we find that first use spread primarily through social networks, retention was associated more with engineers' coding activity than with demographics, and adopters merged roughly 24% more pull requests than they would have otherwise. We use merged pull requests as our proxy for output -- acknowledging that a merged PR is not the same as the value it delivers -- and the lift persists across our four-month window. These results suggest that CLI coding agents are neither uniformly adopted nor mere novelty effects and that organizations should treat visible peer use as central to rollout strategy.

</details>


### [13] [Risk Architecture for AI-Native Engineering Teams: An Organizational Framework for Agentic System Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01421)
*Laxmipriya Ganesh Iyer*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文指出传统软件风险管理框架（基于确定性、离散审计事件、清晰归属）在智能体AI系统中失效，贡献了七维团队画像、六类故障模式（含新发现的依赖边界确定性失配）及合成框架充分性评估方法，发现从纯软件工程到AI原生团队，风险覆盖率单调下降且出现高后果未覆盖故障，最严重风险存在于AI原生团队的输出与确定性依赖的边界处。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有软件风险管理框架隐含假设确定性行为、离散可审计变更事件和清晰的组件-所有者映射，而构建和运维智能体AI系统的团队同时违反了这三条假设：输出是概率性的、系统自主执行多步动作、风险面在部署之间悄然变化。同时，现有AI风险文献要么从上层（政策框架如NIST AI RMF、ISO/IEC 42001）处理，要么从下层（威胁分类如OWASP智能体AI指南）处理，但没有覆盖工程管理者（EM）实际操作的层级：角色、决策权和升级结构。因此需要填补这一空白。

Method: 本文贡献了三个核心方法产出：(i) 七维团队画像，区分纯软件工程团队、混合团队和AI原生团队；(ii) 六类故障模式分类体系，包含新提出的依赖边界确定性失配（dependency-boundary determinism mismatch）；(iii) 合成框架充分性评估方法论，量化每个画像的风险架构在定义场景集上的检测、遏制和升级能力。评估以框架充分性（而非人类行为）为研究对象，得出推导性而非观测性的覆盖率声明。

Result: 覆盖率从纯软件工程到AI原生操作单调下降（中位数意义上）且在AI原生阶段出现突增的未覆盖高后果故障数量。退化集中在特定的故障模式类别中，最严重且覆盖率最低的故障并非出现在AI原生团队内部，而是在其组织边界处——即概率性输出被假定确定性的依赖方消费时产生。

Conclusion: 现有风险管理框架在智能体AI系统场景下存在系统性不足，特别是AI原生团队面临的风险覆盖退化严重，且最危险的风险位于组织接口处（概率输出与确定性依赖的不匹配），而非团队内部。这为工程管理者提供了明确的框架改进方向。

Abstract: Engineering management research has produced mature frameworks for software risk: ownership by feature, escalation by severity, and assurance by test coverage. These frameworks implicitly assume deterministic behavior, discrete and auditable change events, and clear component-to-owner mappings. Teams that build and operate agentic AI systems violate all three assumptions at once: outputs are probabilistic, systems take autonomous multi-step actions, and the risk surface mutates silently between deployments. Existing AI risk literature addresses this from above (policy frameworks such as the NIST AI RMF and ISO/IEC 42001) or below (threat taxonomies such as OWASP's agentic AI guidance), but not at the layer where an engineering manager (EM) operates: roles, decision rights, and escalation structures. This paper contributes (i) a seven-dimension profile distinguishing pure software-engineering, hybrid, and AI-native teams; (ii) a six-cluster failure-mode taxonomy including a previously unarticulated cluster, dependency-boundary determinism mismatch; and (iii) a synthetic framework-adequacy methodology scoring how well each profile's risk architecture detects, contains, and escalates a defined scenario set. Because the object of study is framework adequacy rather than human behavior, the evaluation yields derived rather than observed coverage claims. Coverage degrades as teams move from pure software engineering to AI-native operation, monotonically in the median and abruptly in the count of uncovered, high-consequence failures appearing only at the AI-native step. The degradation concentrates in specific failure-mode categories, and the most severe, least-covered failures arise not inside AI-native teams but at the organizational boundary where their probabilistic outputs are consumed by determinism-assuming dependencies.

</details>


### [14] [From Anatomy to Smells: An Empirical Study of SKILL.md in Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01456)
*David Boram Hong,Aaron Imani,Iftekhar Ahmed*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了LLM智能体技能定义文件SKILL.md的编写实践，通过分析238个真实技能文件、29份文献综述和自动化检测，发现超过99%的文件存在"技能异味"，且这些异味在技能演化过程中很少消失，揭示了实际编写与推荐实践之间的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent Skills被广泛采用，但SKILL.md文件的编写实践和遵循情况尚不清楚，缺乏系统性研究来理解这一新兴软件工件的编写质量。

Method: 1) 对238个真实世界技能文件进行定性分析，归纳出13个高层级和44个低层级语义组件的分类体系；2) 对29个来源进行多声部文献综述，识别最佳编写实践并定义"技能异味"；3) 开发自动化检测工具并对真实技能进行检测分析。

Result: 超过99%的SKILL.md文件包含至少一个技能异味；技能异味一旦引入，在技能演化过程中很少消失。

Conclusion: 实际编写实践与推荐实践之间存在显著差距，需要开发自动化技术来修复技能异味，并提高开发者对这一新兴质量问题的认识。

Abstract: Agent Skills provide on-demand domain knowledge to LLM agents without requiring model retraining. Each Agent Skill is defined by a mandatory SKILL.md file containing metadata and an unstructured Markdown body whose contents are left entirely to the skill author. Despite the rapid adoption of Agent Skills, little is known about how these files are authored or whether existing authoring guidelines are followed in practice. In this paper, we present the first systematic study of SKILL.md files as a software artifact. We qualitatively analyze 238 real-world skills and derive a taxonomy of 13 higher-level and 44 lower-level semantic components. We then conduct a multivocal literature review of 29 sources to identify best practices for authoring SKILL.md files and introduce skill smells as violations of these practices. Finally, we develop an automated detector and apply it to real-world skills, finding that over 99% of SKILL.md files contain at least one skill smell, and once introduced, skill smells rarely disappear as skills evolve. These findings reveal a substantial gap between recommended and actual authoring practices, motivating the development of automated techniques to remediate skill smells while increasing developer awareness of this emerging quality issue.

</details>


### [15] [Insights from GitHub Community on the Matter Standard: Developer Perspectives and Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01494)
*Muhammad Hassan,Carl Gunter,Susan Landau,Masooda Bashir*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过对Project CHIP GitHub仓库超过13,000个issue的主题建模和定性分析，发现开发者在实现和集成Matter物联网标准时面临测试、互操作性、开发、平台与网络四大类系统性问题，并为改进测试基础设施、跨供应商指导和文档提供了具体建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Matter旨在解决物联网长期存在的互操作性问题，但开发者在实际工作中如何体验该标准尚不清楚，因此需要系统性理解开发者贡献者遇到的问题。

Method: 从官方Project CHIP GitHub仓库收集超过13,000个issue，采用主题建模和定性分析方法进行归纳分类。

Result: 识别出四大类重复出现的问题领域：测试、互操作性、开发、平台与网络，并描述了它们在代码库和工具演进中的表现。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了系统性的技术与集成挑战，为Matter标准的成熟提供了改进测试基础设施、跨供应商指导和文档的具体机会。

Abstract: Matter seeks to resolve longstanding interoperability problems in the Internet of Things (IoT), yet little is known about how developers experience the standard in day to day work. This paper examines over 13,000 issues from the official Project CHIP GitHub repository to understand the kinds of problems contributors report when implementing and integrating Matter. Using topic modeling and qualitative analysis, we identify four recurring areas of concern, Testing, Interoperability, Development, and Platform and Network, and describe how they manifest in the evolution of the codebase and tooling. The findings reveal systematic technical and integration challenges and point to concrete opportunities to refine Matter's test infrastructure, cross vendor guidance, and documentation as the standard continues to mature.

</details>


### [16] [Kani: A Model Checker for Rust](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01504)
*Rémi Delmas,Zyad Hassan,Qinheping Hu,Rahul Kumar,Felipe R. Monteiro,Thanh Nguyen,Adrián Palacios,Celina Val,Michael Tautschnig,Justus Adam,Daniel Schwartz-Narbonne,Carolyn Zech*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Kani，一个用于Rust的开源模型检测器，它将有界模型检测从bug发现扩展到提供正确性保证，通过将Rust MIR编译到CBMC引擎来自动检查安全属性，并提供规约语言（函数契约、循环契约、量词、函数存根）以实现从有界到无界的验证。在工业Rust项目中验证了可行性，发现了6个未知bug，并在Rust标准库验证活动中实现了每次代码变更超过16,000个测试用例的规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rust的所有权类型系统虽然能防止安全代码中的内存错误，但某些重要属性（不安全操作的正确性、功能正确性、无运行时panic）仍然独立于编译器的检查范围，需要额外的验证手段。

Method: Kani将Rust的MIR（中级中间表示）编译到CBMC的位精确验证引擎中，自动检查全面的安全属性（无需用户注释），并提供函数契约、循环契约、量词和函数存根等规约语言，将有界模型检测扩展到无界验证。

Result: 在工业Rust项目中，契约将验证从panic自由升级到功能正确性，发现了6个先前未知的bug。在Rust标准库验证活动中，每次代码变更运行超过16,000个验证测试用例，展示了生产级CI规模下的可行性。

Conclusion: Kani成功地将有界模型检测从bug发现提升到提供正确性保证，通过规约语言实现了从有界到无界的扩展，并证明在工业级Rust项目和生产CI环境中具有实用性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Rust's ownership type system prevents memory errors in safe code, but certain desirable properties remain orthogonal to compilation: the soundness of unsafe operations (e.g., raw pointer dereferences), functional correctness, and absence of runtime panics. We present Kani, an open-source model checker for Rust that pushes bounded model checking beyond bug-finding to provide correctness guarantees for these properties. Kani compiles proof harnesses from Rust's Mid-level Intermediate Representation (MIR) into CBMC's bit-precise verification engine, automatically checking a comprehensive set of safety properties with no user annotation. To extend verification from bounded to unbounded, Kani provides a specification language comprising function contracts, loop contracts, quantifiers, and function stubbing. We demonstrate feasibility through case studies on industrial Rust projects, where contracts upgraded verification from panic-freedom to functional correctness, uncovering six previously unknown bugs. Kani operates at scale in production CI, with over 16,000 harnesses verified per code change in the Rust standard library verification campaign.

</details>


### [17] [A Capacity-Aware Parr Model for Agile Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01562)
*Pedro E. Colla*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种容量感知的帕尔模型重构，用于敏捷项目进度预测，将标准帕尔曲线中的潜在努力需求与固定或受限的团队容量相结合，预测总体进度、完成时间、容量赤字和容量松弛，并提供离散冲刺版本及校准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典软件工作量分布模型（如PNR族和Parr曲线）隐含了可自由调配人员的前提，在敏捷环境中团队容量固定、部分固定或受外部约束时直接使用受限，原始曲线可能规定组织无法分配的人员需求。

Method: 将帕尔模型重构为容量感知预测层：将归一化的帕尔型潜在努力需求与观测或计划的容量轨迹相结合，使用少量参数（总工作量K、帕尔形状参数、可匹配非零初始人员的原点常数c、容量轨迹），提供离散冲刺公式，从普通Scrum记录中校准，并采用滚动原点验证协议。

Result: 模型可在不假定资源限制下遵循相同内部活动路径的前提下，预测汇总进度、完成时间、容量赤字和容量松弛。

Conclusion: 贡献刻意窄于完整的项目动态因果理论，作为敏捷项目的容量感知预测层，在团队容量受限场景下比简单管理基线更有效。

Abstract: Classical software effort distribution models, including the PNR family and Parr alter native curve, were designed to describe the time distribution of development effort under an implied staffing pattern. Their direct use in agile environments is limited when team capacity is fixed, partially fixed, or externally constrained, the original curve may prescribe a staff demand that the organization cannot allocate. This paper proposes a compact refactoring of Parr model as a capacity-aware forecasting layer for agile projects. The contribution is deliberately narrower than a full causal theory of project dynamics. A normalized Parr shaped latent effort demand is combined with an observed or planned capacity trajectory. The resulting model forecasts aggregate progress, completion time, capacity deficit, and capacity slack without assuming that the same internal activity path is followed under resource restriction. The model uses a small parameter set such as total effort K, a Parr shape parameter, an origin constant c that can match nonzero initial staffing, and the capacity trajectory. A discrete sprint formulation is provided, together with a calibration method from ordinary Scrum records and a rolling origin validation protocol against simple management baselines.

</details>


### [18] [A Single Patch Is Not Enough: Deterministic Fusion of Repair Candidates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01597)
*Boyang Yang,Xiangliang Hu,Luyao Ren,Yanjun Chen,Bach Le,Tegawendé F. Bissyandé,Haoye Tian*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出PatchFusion，一种确定性原子证据融合方法，通过复用候选补丁间的编辑原子证据来选择和构造最终补丁，无需测试结果。在PatchFuseBench基准上，PatchFusion在SWE-bench Verified解决426/500个bug，SWE-bench Multilingual解决236/300个，Defects4J达到87/371个合理补丁，优于所有匹配的候选池选择器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM编码代理常用pass@k评估，但实际开发中只应用单个最终补丁。存在pass@k到pass@1的差距：候选补丁池可能包含正确补丁，但系统需决定哪个提交给开发者。现有方法主要对候选补丁排序、用测试过滤或查询LLM评判，但都没有确定性地复用共享编辑原子证据来同时选择和构造最终补丁。

Method: PatchFusion包含三步：1) 将全差异一致性融合为修复邻域；2) 选择可审计的代表性补丁；3) 应用证据约束融合(ECF)，保留重复编辑原子并剪枝不支持部分。方法确定性地复用跨候选补丁的编辑原子证据，无需测试结果。

Result: 在PatchFuseBench上，PatchFusion在SWE-bench Verified解决426/500个bug（96.2%的可达上限），SWE-bench Multilingual解决236/300个（89.7%的可达上限），Defects4J达到87/371个合理补丁。恢复41和27个单一源无法解决的bug。ECF消融实验显示增加+5/+6/+9个已解决bug，无回归现象。

Conclusion: 跨候选补丁证据比基于测试和LLM的选择器恢复更多正确补丁，成本低数个数量级。PatchFusion在三个基准上均优于所有匹配的候选池选择器。

Abstract: Modern LLM coding agents are commonly evaluated using pass@k, but developers typically apply a single final patch in real-world settings. This pass@k-to-pass@1 gap is a post-generation problem: a candidate patch pool may contain a correct patch, but the system must decide which one to suggest to developers. Existing post-generation approaches mainly rank whole candidates, filter them with tests, or query an LLM judge, but none deterministically reuse shared edit-atom evidence to both select and construct the final patch. Thus, we propose PatchFusion, a deterministic atomic evidence fusion approach for candidate patches that consults no test outcome at decision time. PatchFusion first fuses whole-diff agreement into a repair neighborhood, selects an auditable representative, and then applies evidence-constrained fusion (ECF) to retain repeated edit atoms and prune unsupported parts. To evaluate this setting, we build PatchFuseBench, a fixed-pool benchmark covering SWE-bench Verified, SWE-bench Multilingual, and Defects4J candidate patches.
  On PatchFuseBench, PatchFusion solves 426/500 bugs on SWE-bench Verified and 236/300 on SWE-bench Multilingual, and reaches 87/371 plausible patches on Defects4J, outperforming every matched candidate-pool selector on all three. PatchFusion recovers 41 and 27 bugs that no single source solves (30 and 18 more over the best single source). Ablation studies show that ECF adds +5/+6/+9 solved bugs by recovering in-pool repairs that selection misses, with no observed regression, and that PatchFusion's gains remain stable as candidate pools are resampled. On these complementary multi-source pools, cross-candidate evidence recovers more correct patches than the test-based and LLM-based selectors we evaluate, at orders-of-magnitude lower cost, reaching within 96.2% and 89.7% of the candidate-reachable ceiling on the two SWE-bench benchmarks.

</details>


### [19] [AgentFlow: Building Agent Dependency Graphs for Static Analysis of Agent Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01640)
*Shenao Wang,Xinyi Hou,Yanjie Zhao,Xiao Cheng,Haoyu Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出AgentFlow，首个用于从智能体程序中恢复和分析智能体依赖关系的静态分析框架。它通过构建智能体依赖图（ADG）来捕获框架无关的智能体组件及其依赖关系，支持智能体物料清单生成和提示-工具风险检测等治理与安全分析。在5个主流框架和5,399个真实程序中验证，其效果优于现有基于AST的静态分析工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体程序的行为不仅依赖传统控制流和数据流，还依赖一类新型的智能体依赖关系，这些关系常嵌入在框架语义（如构造器、工具装饰器、智能体交接声明）中，现有静态分析或依赖追踪工具难以恢复和分析这些依赖。

Method: 提出AgentFlow静态分析框架，构建智能体依赖图（ADG）——一种框架无关的图表示，将智能体、提示、模型、能力、记忆状态和控制策略表示为类型化节点，将组件依赖、控制流和数据流依赖表示为类型化边。基于ADG支持智能体物料清单（BOM）生成和提示-工具风险检测等分析。

Result: 在5个代表性智能体框架和5,399个真实世界智能体程序上评估，AgentFlow比现有基于AST的静态分析工具恢复出更丰富的智能体实体和依赖关系，生成更具依赖感知能力的智能体BOM，并在真实程序中发现了238个提示-工具风险。

Conclusion: ADG为理解、治理和保障新兴智能体软件提供了实用的基础。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly developed as source-code applications built on agent frameworks. These agent programs combine conventional host-language code with framework-defined semantics for models, prompts, tools, memory, and multi-agent orchestration logic. As a result, their behavior depends not only on traditional control and data flows, but also on a new class of agent dependencies. Such dependencies are often expressed as framework-induced semantics, such as agent constructors, tool decorators, and agent handoff declarations, making them difficult to recover with existing static analysis or dependency tracking tools. In this paper, we present AgentFlow, the first static analysis framework for recovering and analyzing agent dependencies from agent programs. AgentFlow constructs an Agent Dependency Graph (ADG), a framework-agnostic graph representation that represents agents, prompts, models, capabilities, memory states, and control policies as typed nodes, and captures their component-dependency, control-flow, and data-flow dependencies as typed edges. Built on ADGs, AgentFlow supports a range of analyses for agent governance and security, including Agent Bill of Materials (BOM) generation and prompt-to-tool risk detection. We implement AgentFlow for five representative agent frameworks and evaluate it on AgentZoo, a corpus of 5,399 real-world agent programs. Our evaluation shows that AgentFlow recovers richer agent entities and dependencies than existing AST-based agent static analysis tools, generates more dependency-aware Agent BOMs, and uncovers 238 taint-style prompt-to-tool risks in real-world agent programs. These results show that ADG provides a practical foundation for understanding, governing, and securing emerging agent software.

</details>


### [20] [When Agents Do Not Stop: Uncovering Infinite Agentic Loops in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01641)
*Xinyi Hou,Shenao Wang,Yanjie Zhao,Haoyu Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出静态分析工具IAL-Scan，用于检测LLM代理系统中由反馈路径未有效约束导致的无限代理循环（IAL）故障，在6,549个代码仓库中实现了91.9%的精确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理通过迭代执行（规划、工具使用、状态更新、代理协作）实现自动化，但反馈路径缺乏有效约束会导致无限代理循环（IAL），引发成本耗尽、模型拒绝服务、上下文增长及重复外部副作用等问题。

Method: IAL-Scan将异构代理代码抽象为框架无关的Agent IR，构建代理循环依赖图（ALDG）恢复显式和框架引入的反馈路径，并检查这些路径能否在无有效边界的情况下反复到达高成本或状态增长操作。

Result: 对6,549个LLM代理仓库进行扫描，报告74个潜在发现，人工确认47个项目中的68个IAL故障，精确率达91.9%。

Conclusion: IAL-Scan能够有效检测真实LLM代理项目中的无限循环故障，验证了静态分析方法在识别此类新型故障模式中的可行性。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly rely on iterative execution to solve tasks through planning, tool use, state updates, and agent collaboration. While this design enables flexible automation, it also creates a new class of failures: an agent may repeatedly execute model calls, tools, workflow transitions, or agent handoffs when the feedback path is not effectively bounded. We call this problem Infinite Agentic Loops (IALs). IALs are not ordinary programming loops; they arise from the interaction between agent logic, framework semantics, runtime observations, and termination mechanisms. Such failures can amplify a single request into long running model and tool execution, causing cost exhaustion, model denial of service, context growth, and repeated external side effects. We propose IAL-Scan, a static analysis tool for detecting IAL failures in real-world LLM agent projects. IAL-Scan abstracts heterogeneous agent code into a framework independent Agent IR, builds an Agentic Loop Dependence Graph (ALDG) to recover explicit and framework induced feedback paths, and checks whether these paths can repeatedly reach costly or state growing operations without an effective bound. We evaluate IAL-Scan on 6,549 LLM agent repositories. It reports 74 potential findings, among which manual review confirms 68 IAL failures across 47 projects, achieving 91.9% precision.

</details>


### [21] [Refploit: Facilitating Exploit Construction via Code-Agent Trajectory Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01760)
*Zirui Chen,Zhipeng Xue,Jiayuan Zhou,Xing Hu,Xin Xia,Xiaohu Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Refploit是一个基于LLM的轨迹恢复框架，通过差分执行验证和约束引导的轨迹恢复，从公开的漏洞利用参考中自动化复现Java库漏洞的可运行利用。在DeepSeek-V4-Flash下对143个漏洞的172个参考进行评估，复现率达80.2%，相对初始轨迹提升64.3%，优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前漏洞利用参考通常不完整、非结构化或仅描述部分复现步骤，现有代码代理生成的利用常常看似成功但实际未触发真正的漏洞逻辑（如用自实现函数替换漏洞API），需要一种方法来自动从公开参考中恢复可运行的漏洞利用。

Method: Refploit首先通过差分执行验证代理生成的利用是否有效；当利用无效时，分析复现进度，定位与复现进度相关的轨迹片段，推导约束条件以指导聚焦恢复。

Result: 在三个开源Java漏洞数据集上（143个漏洞、172个利用参考），使用DeepSeek-V4-Flash成功复现138个利用，复现率80.2%；相对初始生成轨迹提升64.3%；优于PoCGen和Codex with GPT-5.4等基线方法；在不同代码代理上表现一致，证明其通用性。

Conclusion: Refploit通过利用失败轨迹中的有效信息并规避误导方向，显著提升了漏洞利用自动化复现的成功率，具有良好的通用性和实际应用价值。

Abstract: Vulnerability exploits play a crucial role in assessing the downstream impact of Java library vulnerabilities. While some vulnerabilities are accompanied by disclosed exploit references, automatically reproducing such references into runnable exploits remains challenging because they are often incomplete, unstructured, or only describe partial reproduction steps. Recent code agents provide a promising way to automate this process, but our study shows that their generated exploits often appear successful without triggering the actual vulnerable logic, such as replacing vulnerable APIs with self-implemented functions. To address this, we propose Refploit, an LLM-based trajectory recovery framework for facilitating vulnerability reproduction from public exploit references. The key insight is that a failed agent trajectory is not entirely useless. It may have already completed some reproduction subtasks while also revealing misleading directions that should be avoided. Refploit first validates an agent-generated exploit through differential execution. When the exploit is ineffective, Refploit analyzes its reproduction progress, locates the trajectory segments associated with the reproduction progress, and derives constraints to guide focused recovery. We evaluate Refploit on three open-source Java vulnerability datasets, covering 172 exploit references for 143 vulnerabilities. Under DeepSeek-V4-Flash, Refploit successfully reproduces 138 exploits, achieving a reproduction rate of 80.2%. It achieves a 64.3% relative improvement over the initially generated trajectories and outperforms both the SOTA exploit-generation method PoCGen and advanced code agents such as Codex with GPT-5.4. We further adapt Refploit to another code agent and observe consistent improvements, demonstrating its generality.

</details>


### [22] [KRCA: An Efficient Root Cause Analysis System in Hyper-Scale Microservice Systems via Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01788)
*Jiamin Jiang,Jingfei Feng,Yu Luo,Qingliang Zhang,Yongqian Su,Wenwei Gu,Shenglin Zhang,Tianyu Cui,Yao Wu,Jielong Huang,Nan Qi,Dan Pei*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: KRCA是一个面向超大规模微服务系统的端到端根因分析系统，采用多阶段流水线（API级下钻→骨架因果图→记忆增强多智能体框架），在快手生产环境中部署超过6个月，将平均诊断时间减少77.3%，根因定位AC@1达0.88，故障分类AC@1达0.79，比最强基线高出至少31%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有根因分析方法难以适应超大规模微服务系统的极端动态性和庞大规模，亟需一种能兼顾诊断准确性与实时生产效率的端到端RCA系统。

Method: KRCA采用多阶段流水线：1) API级下钻隔离可疑服务；2) 从异常指标实例化基于骨架的因果图作为高召回结构先验；3) 利用记忆增强的多智能体框架验证因果关系并生成最终故障报告。通过结构化因果约束与多智能体推理相结合，平衡诊断准确性与实时生产效率。

Result: 根因服务定位AC@1为0.88，故障类型分类AC@1为0.79，均比最强基线高出至少31%的绝对增益；在快手生产环境部署超6个月，平均诊断时间减少77.3%。

Conclusion: KRCA通过结构化因果约束与多智能体推理的有机结合，在超大规模微服务系统中实现了高效准确的根因分析，并已在实际生产环境中验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Hyper-scale microservice systems have become the standard infrastructure for large-scale Internet companies. These systems consist of numerous loosely coupled microservices that evolve independently through continuous development and deployment. Such complexity makes failures unavoidable, necessitating efficient Root Cause Analysis (RCA) to help Site Reliability Engineers (SREs) quickly localize root cause services and classify failure types. However, existing RCA methods often struggle to adapt to the extreme dynamism and massive scale of these systems. In this paper, we present KRCA, an end-to-end RCA system designed for hyper-scale microservice systems. To manage the vast search space, KRCA employs a multi-stage pipeline that begins with an API-level drilldown to isolate suspicious services. It then instantiates a skeleton-based causal graph from anomalous metrics to serve as a high-recall structural prior, before utilizing a memory-augmented multi-agent framework to verify causality and generate the final failure report. By combining structured causal constraints with multi-agent reasoning, KRCA employs balances diagnostic accuracy with the efficiency requirements of real-time production use. Experimental results show that KRCA achieves AC@1 scores of 0.88 and 0.79 for root cause service localization and failure type classification, outperforming the strongest baseline by at lease 31% in absolute gains. KRCA has been deployed in Kuaishou's production environment for over six months, reducing the average diagnosis time by 77.3%.

</details>


### [23] [Archer: Towards Agentic Review for Compiler Optimizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01808)
*Yunbo Ni,Shaohua Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Archer，首个面向编译器优化的自动化代码审查工具，以LLVM为目标，通过义务约束和确定性验证守卫来审查PR。在70个开放PR和328个关闭PR上的评估发现，21%开放PR和11%关闭PR存在语义缺陷（如误编译），揭示了大型编译器项目中关键审查能力的严重缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代编译器频繁更新，但专家审查能力极其有限，导致集成延迟且语义缺陷潜入编译器代码库。现有通用代码审查智能体因编译器复杂性面临严峻挑战，亟需专用于编译器优化的自动化审查工具。

Method: 以LLVM为目标编译器，提出Archer工具。采用约束驱动的审查流程：前端通过obligations引导分析方向，后端通过确定性验证守卫（deterministic validation guard）仅接受有可执行证据支持的发现，从两端限制智能体审查过程。

Result: 在70个开放PR和328个关闭PR上评估，Archer发现21%的开放PR和11%的关闭PR存在语义缺陷（如误编译）。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了大型编译器项目中关键审查能力的严重缺口，证明了Archer作为额外审稿人的实用价值。

Abstract: Modern compilers are frequently updated, but expert review capacity is highly limited, leading to delayed integration and, in some cases, subtle semantic bugs entering the compiler codebase. Automating the code review process with modern general code review agents may be feasible, but it faces critical challenges due to compiler complexity. In this paper, we use LLVM as our target compiler and present Archer, the first automated agentic code review tool for compiler optimizations. Archer constrains the agentic review process from both ends by using obligations to guide analysis and a deterministic validation guard to admit only findings backed by executable evidence.
  We evaluated Archer on 70 open PRs and 328 closed PRs in LLVM from the last two months. The review results are shocking and concerning: Archer discovers that 21% of open PRs and 11% of closed PRs are buggy, i.e, introducing semantic bugs such as miscompilations in LLVM. Our findings expose a critical gap in the capacity for critical review in large compiler projects and demonstrate the practical value of Archer as an additional reviewer.

</details>


### [24] [Decoupling Code Complexity from Newcomer Participation: A Causal Study of AI Coding Agent Adoption in OSS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01810)
*Weiwei Xu,Xuanning Cui,Hengzhi Ye,Minghui Zhou*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过对1888个开源项目的因果分析，发现采用AI编码助手后，新手流入、入职率和留存率并未显著下降，尽管代码复杂度确实小幅增加（Python认知复杂度约+11%，循环复杂度约+3-4%），但新手参与度并未因此减少。AI辅助与人类参与之间的担忧性权衡并未出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源项目依赖稳定的新手流入，但AI编码助手（如Cursor、Claude Code）可能通过吸收简单任务和使代码更难阅读而挤出新手，引发行业担忧。本文旨在验证这一因果关系是否存在。

Method: 利用GitHub代码搜索识别出1888个首次提交AI代理配置文件的采用项目，通过双重差分法（DiD）与匹配的非采用控制组进行比较，主分析限定在603个具有真实采用前期的项目中，并采用多种趋势设定和度量指标进行稳健性检验。

Result: 新手流入在采用后无显著下降（点估计显示小幅增加至最保守设定下的轻微不显著下降），入职率和留存率不变，good-first-issue标签相关的新手任务未减少。代码复杂度确实上升：Python认知复杂度约+11%（约为先前估计的四分之一），全语言循环复杂度约+3-4%。但在复杂度上升的固定子集中，新手参与度并未下降。

Conclusion: 在成熟开源项目中，采用AI编码助手会使代码复杂度小幅增加，但不会挤出项目赖以维继的新手参与者。AI辅助与人类参与之间的担忧性权衡并未在现实数据中显现。

Abstract: Open-source projects depend on a steady inflow of newcomers. A growing concern is that AI coding agents (tools such as Cursor and Claude Code that write code from natural-language instructions) will crowd them out, by absorbing the simple tasks that beginners start with and by making code harder to read. We give this concern a causal answer. Using GitHub code search we identify 1,888 projects that adopted an agent, signaled by their first commit of a configuration file. We apply difference-in-differences against matched non-adopting controls, restricting the main analysis to the 603 adopters with a genuine pre-adoption period. We find no evidence of crowding-out: across estimators newcomer inflow shows no significant decline after adoption (point estimates run from a small increase to, under the most conservative trend specification, a slight and insignificant dip), onboarding and retention are unchanged, and a sparse, correlational beginner-task measure (good-first-issue labels, which we cannot test for parallel trends) shows no decline. The feared mechanism is real but decoupled: adoption raises per-function code complexity (about +11% on a cognitive metric for Python, a quarter of the prior estimate, and +3 to 4% in cyclomatic terms across all languages), yet in fixed-unit subsets where complexity rose (Python on the cognitive metric, and all languages on the cyclomatic metric), newcomer participation does not decline. These results suggest that, in established open-source projects, adopting an AI coding agent makes code modestly more complex but does not crowd out the human newcomers that a project depends on: the feared trade-off between AI assistance and human participation does not materialize.

</details>


### [25] [Understanding Software Defect Prediction: A Large-scale Empirical Study Across Uncertainty Quantification and Performance Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01842)
*Ranjun Peng,Xuan Xie,Rubing Huang,Wenbin He,Zhijie Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了软件缺陷预测中基于概率的不确定性量化（UQ）指标与分类器性能及校准之间的关系。通过在36个WPDP数据集和32个CPDP数据集上对16种分类器、5种UQ指标、6种性能指标和3种校准指标进行大规模实证分析，发现UQ高度依赖上下文：WPDP下UQ与假阳性率和AUC相关性更一致，而CPDP下多个相关性减弱甚至逆转。结论是UQ需针对具体性能目标评估，校准应使用多指标独立评估，跨项目转移的概率需重新验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件缺陷预测分类器输出的概率被广泛用于检查优先级排序、阈值调优和风险沟通。尽管基于概率的不确定性量化（UQ）可以表征预测置信度，但常见UQ指标是否可靠地指示分类器的性能和校准效果尚不明确，因此需要系统性地实证研究这一关系。

Method: 大规模实证研究：选取16种代表性分类器、5种UQ指标、6种性能指标、3种校准指标；在两种预测设置（WPDP使用36个基准数据集，CPDP使用32个特征兼容数据集）下分析UQ与性能/校准之间的关系。

Result: WPDP下UQ与假阳性率和AUC的相关性比与MCC、F1分数等指标更一致且稳定；这些相关性因分类器类别和数据集集合而异。性能与校准相关但不可互换，判别力强的分类器仍可能存在较大校准误差。CPDP下多个UQ-性能和UQ-校准相关性减弱或逆转，表明不确定性信号不能可靠跨项目迁移。

Conclusion: UQ应针对具体性能目标进行评估；校准应使用多指标独立评估；跨项目迁移的概率应在用于质量保证决策前重新验证。

Abstract: Software defect prediction (SDP) classifiers produce probabilities used for inspection prioritization, threshold tuning, and risk communication. Probability-based uncertainty quantification (UQ) characterizes prediction confidence, but whether common UQ metrics reliably indicate performance and calibration remains unclear. We conducted a large-scale empirical study of probability-based UQ for SDP. We evaluated five UQ metrics, six performance metrics, and three calibration metrics for 16 representative classifiers. We analyzed these relationships under two prediction settings: within-project defect prediction (WPDP), using 36 benchmark datasets, and cross-project defect prediction (CPDP), using 32 feature-compatible datasets. Results showed that UQ was highly context-dependent. Under WPDP, UQ correlated more consistently with false positive rate and AUC than with MCC, F1 score, and other metrics; these correlations also varied across classifier categories and dataset collections. Performance and calibration were related but not interchangeable; classifiers with strong discrimination could still exhibit large calibration error. Under CPDP, several UQ-performance and UQ-calibration correlations weakened or reversed, indicating that uncertainty signals do not reliably transfer across projects. Thus, UQ should be evaluated against specific performance objectives. Calibration should be assessed independently using multiple metrics. Transferred probabilities should be revalidated before guiding quality-assurance decisions.

</details>


### [26] [Technical Debt Friction for Maintenance Prioritization: An Industrial Multi-Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01850)
*Simeon Tverdal,Phu Nguyen,Arda Goknil,Antonio Martini,Markus Borg,Adam Tornhill*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过多项工业案例研究，提出技术债务摩擦（Technical Debt Friction）作为识别维护与重构优先级的概念，发现其能与代码健康度、热点、耦合度等分析互补，帮助从业者定位维护痛点并揭示演化模式，但有效性高度依赖上下文和辅助证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有软件分析方法（如代码健康度、热点、耦合度）虽能提供有价值信号，但未能充分捕捉实践中拖慢软件演化的变更负担（experienced burden of change）。因此需要一种新的优先级导向概念——技术债务摩擦，来识别技术债务对维护与演化影响最严重的地方。

Method: 采用多案例工业研究，在多个工业案例中与从业者进行结构化走查会议，使用包括代码健康度、热点、耦合度、重构目标和社会技术视图在内的分析工件，评估技术债务摩擦的实用性和解释力。

Result: 从业者普遍认为技术债务摩擦有助于推理维护负担，尤其在结合互补的技术与社会技术视图时效果更佳。文件层面，摩擦常与已知问题区域一致，并在多个案例中与后续获得维护关注的文件匹配，但其实际相关性高度依赖上下文。项目层面，摩擦分布可能揭示更广泛的维护与演化模式。

Conclusion: 技术债务摩擦作为一种决策支持概念具有潜力，但最有效的使用方式需要结合上下文知识和辅助证据。

Abstract: Software-intensive organizations need effective ways to identify where maintenance and refactoring efforts will yield the greatest practical benefit. Although software analytics such as code health, hotspots, and coupling provide valuable signals, they do not always capture the experienced burden of change that slows software evolution in practice. This paper presents a multi-case industrial study of technical debt friction as a prioritization-oriented concept for identifying where technical debt most strongly affects maintenance and evolution. We investigate how practitioners interpret the concept, whether friction-related analysis aligns with perceived maintenance pain points and refactoring needs, and what broader maintenance and evolution insights friction can provide beyond individual refactoring candidates. To this end, we conducted structured walkthrough sessions with practitioners across multiple industrial cases using analysis artifacts including code health, hotspots, coupling, refactoring targets, and socio-technical views. Our findings show that practitioners generally considered technical debt friction useful for reasoning about maintenance burden, especially when interpreted together with complementary technical and socio-technical views. At the file level, friction often aligned with known problematic areas and, in several cases, with files that later received maintenance attention, although its practical relevance depended strongly on context. In addition, our exploratory project-level analysis suggests that friction distributions may reveal broader maintenance and evolution patterns. These results indicate that technical debt friction is promising as a decision-support concept, but most effective when used with contextual knowledge and supporting evidence.

</details>


### [27] [Regression Accumulation in Multi-Turn LLM Programming Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01855)
*Yonghui,Huang,Lin Ma,Amjed Tahir,Qian Zhang,Liwen Xiao,Lysa Xiao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 在多轮LLM编程对话中，代码建议存在"回归累积"问题——后续轮次可能破坏前期已满足的需求。研究发现6个模型中有40%-73%的任务出现此问题，而"验证门控"（Verification Gate）策略能持续提升模型最终轮次质量（如DeepSeek-V3从75.8%提升至87.9%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM辅助软件开发中编程往往是迭代的，但多轮LLM编程对话中后续代码建议可能破坏前期已满足的需求（即回归累积），而现有评估多聚焦于单轮性能，可能高估模型在多轮场景下的可靠性。

Method: 从HumanEval+和MBPP+构建542个任务，扩展为8轮需求演化链；评估6个LLM共26,016个实例（542×6×8）；每轮测试当前代码是否仍通过前期基准测试；对384个失败案例进行四标注者独立标注，构建多轮回归错误分类体系。

Result: 所有6个模型均出现回归累积，40%-73%的任务在完整对话中丢失了先前正确行为；最终轮质量低于初始轮质量，尤其在添加输入验证或更广泛输入类型时；跨轮冲突（Cross-Turn Conflict）是主要失败类别；验证门控（Verification Gate）是唯一能持续提升所有模型的策略。

Conclusion: 强单轮性能可能高估多轮编程对话的可靠性；未来评估和工具设计应测试后续代码建议是否保留前期需求，并应纳入验证门控机制。

Abstract: In LLM-assisted software development, coding is often iterative. We study regression accumulation in multi-turn LLM programming conversations, where later code suggestions may break requirements introduced in earlier turns. Reliability therefore depends not only on satisfying the current request, but also on preserving previously satisfied behavior.
  We construct 542 tasks from HumanEval+ and MBPP+ and extend each task into an 8-turn requirement-evolution chain. We evaluate six LLMs on 26,016 turn instances (542 x 6 x 8). At each turn, we test whether the current code still passes earlier benchmark tests. We also analyze 384 failure cases from the failure population and build a taxonomy of multi-turn regression bugs through independent four-annotator labeling.
  Our results show that regression accumulation appears across all six models: 40% to 73% of tasks lose previously correct behavior over the full conversation. Final-turn quality is lower than initial-turn quality across models, especially when later turns add input validation or broader input types. Manual analysis shows that Cross-Turn Conflict, where later code conflicts with earlier requirements, is the main failure class. We further find that Verification Gate, which checks new code against prior tests and triggers rollback and retry, is the only strategy that consistently improves all models, raising final-turn quality from 75.8% to 87.9% on DeepSeek-V3 and from 31.6% to 47.3% on Llama-3.1-8B.
  These findings suggest that strong single-turn performance can overestimate reliability in multi-turn coding conversations. Future evaluation and tool design should test whether later code suggestions preserve earlier requirements and should include Verification Gate mechanisms.

</details>


### [28] [An Exploratory Study on LLM-Generated Code and Comments in Code Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01867)
*Yongyi Ji,Jiaji Wang,Yi Zhou,Fuxiang Chen,Hongji Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过检测器代理分析，调查了2021至2025年间公司和社区维护的仓库中LLM生成代码与注释的特征、差异及其与bug的关联。结果显示：被检测为LLM生成的代码随时间减少，频繁出现在测试用例中，且存在大量仓库内代码克隆；注释比例相对稳定，但语法正确句子的比例较低；公司维护仓库中LLM生成代码和注释的比例更高；仅少数人工标记的bug与LLM生成代码相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM在软件开发中被广泛使用（如大公司20-30%的代码由LLM生成），但业界对其实际应用效果存在质疑，例如调试生成代码耗时更多、生成注释不自然等问题。为回应这些疑虑，需要系统性地研究LLM生成代码和注释的特征、差异及其与bug的关联。

Method: 对2021年至2025年间活跃的公司维护和社区维护仓库进行大规模实验，使用多种检测工具和技术识别可能由LLM生成的代码和注释，进行代理分析，比较两者的特征、仓库类型间的差异以及与bug的关联性。

Result: 1) 被检测为LLM生成的代码随时间减少，且频繁出现在测试用例中；注释比例相对稳定。2) LLM生成代码存在大量仓库内代码克隆；LLM生成注释中语法正确句子的比例较低。3) 公司维护仓库中LLM生成代码和注释的比例更高。4) 仅少数人工标记的bug与LLM生成代码存在关联。

Conclusion: 研究表明，基于检测器的代理分析揭示了LLM生成代码和注释的若干特征（如代码克隆普遍、注释语法正确率低），并发现公司仓库中LLM生成内容占比更高，但LLM生成代码与bug的关联性较弱，这为理解LLM在软件开发中的实际影响提供了实证依据。

Abstract: The use of LLMs in software development has become increasingly widespread on tasks such as code generation and summarization. Reports from large technology companies showed that around 20% to 30% of their code are generated by LLMs. However, there remains skepticism about the practical usage of LLM-generated code and comments, such as concerns on more time for debugging the generated code and the unnaturalness of the generated comments. In this paper, we study the code and comments detected as likely to be generated by LLMs and their characteristics, the differences between company- and community-maintained repositories, and how likely bugs are associated with LLM-generated code. We conduct extensive experiments on active company- and community-maintained repositories from 2021 to 2025 using various tools and techniques that detect code and comments generated by LLMs. Based on our detector-based proxy analysis, the results suggest that code detected as likely to be generated by LLMs decreased over time and appeared frequently in test cases, while that of comments remains relatively stable. Proxy results further suggest that code detected as likely to be generated by LLMs shows substantial intra-repository code clones, whereas comments exhibit a relatively low proportion of grammatically correct sentences. In addition, the company-maintained repositories show a higher percentage of code and comments detected as likely to be generated by LLMs, and only a small percentage of the human-labelled bugs are detected as being likely associated with LLM-generated code.

</details>


### [29] [Understanding Build Reproducibility in the F-Droid Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01890)
*Denise Nanni,Julien Malka,Stefano Zacchiroli,Théo Zimmermann,Gabriele d'Angelo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次对F-Droid开源Android应用生态的构建复现性进行实证研究。通过分析历史日志发现，整体比特级复现率随时间稳步上升；但对已确认可复现的18,904个应用版本进行重构建时，仅83%成功，失败主因是依赖缺失（占76%）。成功重建的应用中94%仍保持比特级复现。结果表明，比特级复现性在可重建应用中基本保持，但重建能力本身随时间显著退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: F-Droid虽然在应用发布时系统性地从源码重建并测试比特级复现性，但未保证未来的复现持续性。随着软件生态系统演化，复现性可能退化，对软件保存和安全性造成负面影响。现有研究缺乏对F-Droid生态中构建复现性随时间变化情况的实证分析。

Method: 分析F-Droid的历史复现性日志，统计整体比特级复现率的时间变化趋势；对2018年9月至2026年2月间已确认比特级可复现的18,904个应用版本进行重构建实验，评估复现性随时间保持的情况，并分类分析重建失败的原因。

Result: 整体比特级复现率随新版本发布稳步上升；重构建成功率为83%，其中依赖缺失占失败原因的76%；成功重建的应用中94%仍保持比特级复现。

Conclusion: 比特级复现性在可重建的应用中基本得以保持，但重建能力本身随时间高度敏感地退化，依赖缺失是主要瓶颈。

Abstract: The security of open source applications benefits considerably from the possibility of rebuilding their source and verifying the output. F-Droid, a prominent distribution for open source Android applications, systematically rebuilds them from source and tests their bitwise reproducibility at app publishing time. However, F-Droid offers no guarantee that app reproducibility will continue to hold in the future. As software ecosystems evolve, reproducibility may degrade, with potential negative consequences for software preservation and security. We present the first empirical study of build reproducibility in the F-Droid app ecosystem. Analyzing historical reproducibility logs, we find that the overall bitwise reproducibility rate has been steadily increasing over time (as new versions of apps are published). We then evaluate how reproducibility holds in time for fixed app versions, by attempting to rebuild 18 904 app versions that F-Droid had previously confirmed bitwise reproducible, published between September 2018 and February 2026, achieving an 83% rebuild success rate, and identify missing dependencies as the dominant cause of failure, accounting for 76% of non-rebuildable cases. Among successfully rebuilt apps, 94% are also bitwise reproducible-i.e., they still yield bitwise identical artifacts upon rebuild. Together, these results show that while bitwise reproducibility largely holds for apps that can be rebuilt, rebuildability itself is highly sensitive to temporal decay.

</details>


### [30] [AI Writes Faster Than Humans Can Review: A Longitudinal Study of an Enterprise 2x Mandate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01904)
*Hao He,Shyam Agarwal,Yegor Denisov-Blanch,Pavel Azaletskiy,Sanmi Koyejo,Bogdan Vasilescu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过对一家中型AI驱动企业的案例研究，展示了2025年中期实施的"2x"强制AI编码工具使用 mandate 如何使每位工程师的合并拉取请求数量翻倍（达到基准线的2.09倍），并分析了AI采用、使用强度、代码审查结构变化等机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业越来越多地强制使用AI编码工具并报告生产力大幅提升，但关于此类强制措施如何长期展开的纵向证据稀缺，本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 基于802名开发者和196,212个拉取请求的面板数据（2024年1月至2026年4月），采用交错双重差分设计，结合描述性统计和因果推断方法。

Result: 每位工程师的吞吐量最终翻倍，达到基准线的2.09倍；生产力提升与AI采用和使用积累效应相关；代码审查结构发生变化，每位审查者的负载约翻倍，自动化审查超过人工审查；合并和回退率保持稳定。

Conclusion: 强制使用AI编码工具作为催化剂而非直接驱动因素，通过采用和使用渠道实现生产力提升，但因果归因需谨慎；生产力提升在各资历级别广泛分布，但集中在新代码上。

Abstract: Enterprises increasingly mandate AI coding tools and report large productivity gains, yet longitudinal evidence on how such a mandate unfolds is scarce. In this paper, we present a quantitative case study of a documented enterprise "2x" mandate at a mid-sized, AI-forward company that has been committed to doubling merged pull requests per engineer since mid-2025. In a panel of 802 developers and 196,212 pull requests (January 2024-April 2026), per-capita throughput eventually doubled, reaching 2.09x the pre-mandate baseline in April 2026, among the largest gains reported from a field deployment of AI coding tools to our knowledge. A staggered difference-in-differences design links the within-developer share of this gain to AI adoption and to a further gain that grows with accumulated use, with the mandate acting as a catalyst rather than a direct driver. Because adoption and usage intensity were not randomly assigned, we read this evidence as strongly implicating an adoption-and-use channel rather than as exact causal attribution. The gain is broadly shared across seniority yet concentrated in newer code and not separable across model generations. Adoption also restructured code review around automation: per-reviewer load roughly doubled and automated review overtook human review, while merge and revert rates held steady.

</details>


### [31] [Beyond Textual Repository Exploration: Dual-Modal Structural Reasoning for Agentic Issue Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01929)
*Jiayi Zhang,Kai Huang,Yang Liu,Chunyang Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出DUALVIEW，一个双模态结构脚手架框架，通过四种互补图视图（模块耦合图、函数调用图、类层次图、程序依赖图）为问题解决智能体提供可视化仓库结构推理能力，从而克服传统文本顺序导航在大规模长时序代码库中的局限性，在SWE-bench基准上显著提升各类智能体的修复性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体程序修复方法依赖顺序的、基于文本的代码导航，在面对大规模、长时序、具有复杂长距离依赖的仓库时，智能体需在交互步骤中反复从碎片化文本观察重建结构信息，导致探索漂移和不完整的定位。

Method: DUALVIEW构建四种互补图视图：模块耦合图(MCG)、函数调用图(FCG)、类层次图(CHG)、程序依赖图(PDG)，并通过可查询接口同时提供视觉和文本响应，使智能体能够直接在持久化的依赖关系视觉表示上进行推理。

Result: 在SWE-bench Pro和Verified上评估，DUALVIEW在不同智能体架构和模型家族上均一致提升问题解决性能。消融实验表明改进不仅来自文本结构信息，更来自仓库依赖的视觉外化表示。

Conclusion: 双模态结构脚手架（结合文本与视觉结构信息）能有效支持长时序仓库探索，克服文本顺序导航的固有局限。

Abstract: Recent advances in agentic program repair have significantly improved issue resolution by enabling iterative repository exploration. However, existing approaches predominantly rely on sequential, text-based code navigation, which fundamentally limits their ability to reason over large-scale long-horizon repositories with complex and long-range dependencies. As issue-resolution agents traverse repositories through fragmented textual observations, structural information such as module organization, call relationships, and dependency chains must be repeatedly reconstructed across interaction steps, often leading to exploration drift and incomplete localization. We present DUALVIEW, a dual-modal structural scaffolding framework that brings visual reasoning into repository exploration for issue-resolution agents. DUALVIEW represents repository structure through four complementary graph views: Module Coupling Graph (MCG), Function Call Graph (FCG), Class Hierarchy Graph (CHG), and Program Dependence Graph (PDG), and exposes them through a queryable interface with visual and textual responses. Rather than reconstructing repository structure from a sequence of textual observations, agents can directly reason over persistent visual representations of code dependencies, enabling more effective exploration and understanding of long-horizon codebases. We evaluate DUALVIEW on SWE-bench Pro and Verified. Results show that DUALVIEW consistently improves issue-resolution performance across different agent architectures and model families. Further ablation studies demonstrate that the gains arise not only from textual structural information but also from visual externalization of repository dependencies, which better supports long-horizon repository exploration.

</details>


### [32] [Underspecification does not imply Incoherence: The Risks of Semantic Collapse in Coding Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01953)
*Cedric Richter,Mike Papadakis*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文发现LLM在代码生成中并非因提示模糊而产生不一致的输出，反而会"坍缩"到单一但行为错误的实现上（称为"有害语义坍缩"），在MBPP、HumanEval、LiveCodeBench等基准测试中分别影响超10%、3%和32%的任务，且通过故意注入模糊性可使该比率提高5倍以上，挑战了以输出不一致性作为提示模糊性代理指标的常规假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战"提示模糊会导致LLM输出不一致代码"这一常见假设，揭示LLM实际上面临一种更隐蔽的失败模式——即使任务描述存在歧义，LLM仍会一致地生成语义错误但表面连贯的代码，而现有依赖不一致性作为模糊性代理指标的技术无法检测此类错误。

Method: 在MBPP、HumanEval、LiveCodeBench三个基准测试上系统测量有害语义坍缩的发生率；进一步通过人为注入模糊性构造实验，测量该比率的变化；对比依赖不一致性作为模糊性代理指标的方法与实际坍缩率之间的差异。

Result: 有害语义坍缩影响MBPP中超过10%的任务、HumanEval中3%、LiveCodeBench中32%；通过故意注入模糊性，该比率可提升至5倍以上；依赖不一致性作为模糊性代理指标的方法存在根本性盲区。

Conclusion: 现有LLM代码生成评估方法中，以输出不一致性作为提示模糊性指标的假设存在缺陷，需要新的技术来检测和缓解"有害语义坍缩"问题，尤其是在看似明确的任务描述场景下。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have become increasingly effective at generating code when task descriptions are clear and precise. Yet, in practice, user-provided task descriptions are often ambiguous, incomplete, or contradictory, leaving critical aspects of the intended program behavior underspecified. In such cases, multiple behaviorally distinct interpretations may satisfy the description equally well, yet semantically differ in ways that matter/affect the user intent. A natural expectation, often assumed by researchers, is that prompt underspecification manifests as incoherence: When asked multiple times, an LLM produces multiple semantically distinct implementations reflecting the ambiguity of the task description. In this paper, we challenge this assumption. We find that LLMs frequently collapse onto a single incorrect interpretation of the task description, consistently generating coherent but behaviorally misaligned code. We term this failure mode detrimental semantic collapse and find that it affects over 10% of tasks in MBPP, 3% in HumanEval, and 32% of LiveCodeBench, all benchmarks assumed to be well-specified. By deliberately injecting underspecification issues in the benchmark prompts, the rate rises to over 5 times, exposing a fundamental blind spot in disambiguation and correctness estimation techniques that rely on incoherence as a proxy for prompt underspecification.

</details>
