*Shahbaz Siddeeq,Mateen Abbasi,Jussi Rasku,Zheying Zhang,François Christophe,Tommi Mikkonen,Pekka Abrahamsson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于史诗组织的LLM流水线（Timeless）用于自动化生成Gherkin BDD验收标准，相比基线方法在结构有效性、语义覆盖率和专家评估质量上表现更优，语义覆盖率94.3% vs 92.9%，专家偏好评分显著更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gherkin BDD验收标准的手动编写是需求工程中的瓶颈，现有方法缺乏自动化且质量不稳定，需要系统性研究如何利用LLM高效生成高质量的Gherkin场景。

Method: 提出Timeless方法（基于史诗组织的LLM流水线），使用JSON约束生成结构有效场景；在PURE数据集107条需求上对比零样本基线；评估包含结构指标、TF-IDF和稠密嵌入的语义覆盖率、四位研究者的盲审专家评估。

Result: Timeless在所有输出中达到100%结构有效性，基线为99%；语义覆盖率94.3% vs 92.9%；TF-IDF覆盖率低于基线（提示词汇度量无法捕捉抽象级别差异）；专家评分：正确性4.61 vs 4.14，可执行性4.61 vs 4.07，完整性4.31 vs 3.50。

Conclusion: 史诗组织的LLM生成策略能在保持可比语义覆盖率的同时提升感知到的Gherkin质量，但结论的泛化需要更广泛的重复验证。

Abstract: Automated authoring of Gherkin Behavior-Driven Development (BDD) acceptance criteria remains a manual bottleneck in requirements engineering. This study investigates whether epic-organized LLM-generated Gherkin produces higher quality and coverage than requirement-aligned generation. We compare our Timeless (an epic-organized LLM pipeline) approach against a naive large language model (LLM) baseline on four requirements documents (107 requirements) from the PURE dataset. Evaluation covers structural metrics, automated requirement coverage via TF-IDF and dense embeddings, and blind expert assessment by four researchers. In our evaluation, the JSON-constrained pipeline produced structurally valid scenarios across all generated outputs, while the zero-shot baseline achieved 99% structural validity. Semantic coverage was comparable to the baseline, with Timeless achieving 94.3% semantic Requirement Coverage Rate compared with 92.9% for the baseline. TF-IDF produced lower coverage scores for the epic-organized output, suggesting that lexical metrics may miss coverage when scenarios paraphrase requirements at a higher level of abstraction. Expert raters prefer the epic-organized strategy on Correctness (4.61 vs 4.14), Executability (4.61 vs 4.07), and Completeness (4.31 vs 3.50). Overall, the results suggest that epic-organized generation can improve perceived Gherkin quality while maintaining comparable semantic coverage, although broader replication is needed before generalizing this finding.

</details>


### [34] [Benchmarking Quantum Software Testing with Scalable Quantum Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02029)
*Yuechen Li,Minqi Shao,Xiyuan Li,Jianjun Zhao,Kai-Yuan Cai*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 Qolumbina，一个面向大规模量子程序的可控软件测试基准基础设施，包含40个经筛选、重构、标准化封装的开源程序，并基于功能、输出行为、开发复杂度与量子执行复杂度四个维度进行表征，通过实证研究验证其在覆盖率多样性、可扩展性以及执行成本与缺陷检测研究中的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子软件测试研究过度依赖小型硬编码或电路级基准，而可用的量子程序散落于各仓库且缺乏明确筛选标准，导致公平比较困难、系统性可复现性不足。亟需一个可测试、反映当前软件开发实践的基准以支撑严格的实证评估。

Method: 从开源仓库中遴选40个量子程序，经过系统性筛选、重构、添加规范说明、测试用例与单元测试以及标准化接口适配，将其转化为可测试用例；同时提出QST导向的四大表征标准（功能、输出行为、开发复杂度、量子执行复杂度），并通过实证研究和两个近期QST方法的对照实验验证基准有效性。

Result: 实证结果表明Qolumbina覆盖了多样化的测试相关属性，并支持超越固定尺寸电路的扩展性分析；通过两个QST方法的受控实验，验证了该基准在执行成本与缺陷检测研究中的可行性，同时揭示了后端依赖效应对QST结果解读的影响。

Conclusion: Qolumbina为量子软件测试提供了首个系统化、可扩展的基准基础设施，有效解决了现有基准碎片化和不可复现的问题，并能支撑对执行成本和缺陷检测能力的深入分析，为QST领域提供了标准化的评估平台。

Abstract: Quantum software testing (QST) checks whether quantum programs behave according to their intended specifications. A key requirement for QST research is a benchmark that supports rigorous empirical evaluation on programs that are testable and better reflect current software development practices. However, existing studies heavily rely on small hard-coded or circuit-level benchmarks, while available quantum programs are scattered across repositories without clear selection criteria, which limits fair comparison and systematic reproducibility. To this end, we present Qolumbina, a benchmark infrastructure for controlled QST experiments on scalable quantum programs. Qolumbina curates 40 programs from open-source repositories, turns them into test-ready subjects through systematic selection, refactoring, specifications, test case examples, unit tests, and standardized interfaces. We also propose QST-oriented criteria to characterize quantum programs along functionality, output behavior, development complexity, and quantum-specific execution complexity. Using these criteria, our empirical study shows that Qolumbina covers diverse testing-relevant properties and supports scalability analysis beyond fixed-size circuit benchmarks. Through controlled experiments with two recent QST approaches, we demonstrate the feasibility of using Qolumbina for execution-cost and fault-detection studies, and highlight backend-dependent effects that can influence QST result interpretation.

</details>


### [35] [Mitigating Package Hallucinations in Large Language Models via Model Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02052)
*Shuhan Liu,Yukai Zhao,Xing Hu,Kui Liu,Xiaohu Yang,Xin Xia*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出BOUND框架，通过轻量级局部模型编辑方法缓解大语言模型中的包幻觉问题。该方法将包幻觉缓解建模为包有效性边界编辑问题，采用风险感知定位策略识别相关模块，并使用边界感知目标函数通过LoRA适配器进行编辑。实验表明，在包推荐任务中，包级幻觉率在编辑提示上降低79.9%，在未见提示上降低65.4%；该边界还能泛化到代码生成和pip安装推荐等其他包相关任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在软件工程任务中可能产生包幻觉（生成不存在或无效的包名），这些幻觉可被攻击者利用进行软件供应链攻击（注册恶意包）。因此，缓解包幻觉对提高LLM辅助软件开发的可靠性和安全性至关重要。

Method: 提出BOUND框架，将包幻觉缓解形式化为包有效性边界编辑问题。方法包括：（1）通过风险感知定位策略识别与包幻觉相关的模型模块；（2）使用边界感知目标函数，通过轻量级LoRA适配器编辑这些模块，强化有效包、抑制幻觉包并保持局部行为不变性。

Result: 在包推荐任务中，包级幻觉率（Package-HR）在编辑提示上降低79.9%，在未见提示上降低65.4%。学习的包有效性边界可泛化到其他任务：代码生成任务降低12.8%，pip安装推荐任务降低34.0%。

Conclusion: BOUND有效减少了包幻觉，同时保留了有效的包推荐能力，通过精炼LLM的包有效性边界提高了包相关输出的可靠性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in software engineering tasks, such as code generation, library recommendation, and dependency configuration. However, recent studies show that LLMs may suffer from package hallucination, where they generate non-existent or invalid package names. These hallucinations can be exploited in software supply chain attacks, as attackers may register malicious packages under hallucinated names. Therefore, mitigating package hallucination is important for improving the reliability and security of LLM-assisted software development. In this paper, we introduce BOUND, a lightweight localized model editing framework for mitigating package hallucinations in LLMs. BOUND formulates package hallucination mitigation as a package-validity boundary editing problem, where the boundary refers to the model's ability to distinguish valid packages from hallucinated package names under a given task context. It first locates modules related to package hallucination through a risk-aware localization strategy, and then edits these modules with lightweight LoRA adapters using a boundary-aware objective that reinforces valid packages, suppresses hallucinated packages, and preserves locality behavior. Experimental results show that BOUND effectively reduces package hallucinations while preserving valid package recommendations. In the package recommendation task, BOUND reduces package-level hallucination rate (Package-HR) by 79.9% on edit prompts and by 65.4% on unseen prompts. The learned package-validity boundary further generalizes to other package-related tasks, reducing Package-HR by 12.8% in code generation and by 34.0% in pip install recommendation. These results show that BOUND refines the package-validity boundary of LLMs and improves the reliability of package-related outputs.

</details>


### [36] [Prompt Coverage Adequacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02057)
*Florian Tambon,Michael Konstantinou,Cedric Richter,Charles Chenouard,Mark Harman,Mike Papadakis*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出提示覆盖充分性（Prompt Coverage Adequacy）——一种针对LLM和智能体编程中提示层面的新测试覆盖准则，通过注意力机制评估测试集对提示需求的满足程度，实验表明其比传统代码覆盖率能多发现30%以上的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM和自主智能体将软件开发从编写精确程序转向表达意图和目标，提示成为主要开发制品，传统代码覆盖准则不再适用，亟需面向提示的新测试度量方法。

Method: 提出Prompt Coverage Adequacy准则，作为传统代码覆盖的类比但操作于提示层面；利用LLM的注意力机制衡量测试套件对提示中需求的满足程度，并基于注意力增强（attention boosting）实现了该准则的简单实例化。

Result: 在两个数据集和多个LLM上的实验表明，Prompt Coverage与故障检测有效性相关；在指导测试生成时，相比传统代码覆盖率能多发现30%以上的缺陷。

Conclusion: Prompt Coverage Adequacy可作为LLM驱动软件开发新范式下测试度量的基础，弥补经典覆盖准则在此新情境中的局限性。

Abstract: In recent years, it has become increasingly evident that large language models (LLMs) and autonomous agents raise the level of abstraction in software development by shifting the focus from writing precise procedures to expressing intents and goals. This paradigm shift introduces new challenges, particularly in how testing should be guided when prompts, rather than code, become primary development artifacts. To address this challenge, we propose Prompt Coverage Adequacy, a novel coverage criterion designed to support the testing of code generated from task descriptions. Prompt Coverage Adequacy serves as an analog to traditional code coverage, but operates at the level of prompts used in LLM and agent-based programming. Specifically, it measures how well a given test suite satisfies the requirements expressed in a prompt by leveraging the attention mechanisms of LLMs. We evaluate a simple instantiation of this criterion, based on attention boosting, across two datasets and multiple LLMs. Our results demonstrate that Prompt Coverage is associated with fault-detection effectiveness and can uncover over 30+% more faults than traditional code coverage when used to guide test generation. These findings suggest that Prompt Coverage Adequacy can serve as a foundation for developing testing metrics better suited to the emerging paradigm of LLM-driven software development, addressing the limitations of classical coverage criteria in this new context.

</details>


### [37] [File-Level Copying Is an Implicit Dependency in Open Source](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02059)
*Runzhi He,Audris Mockus,Wenhao Yang,Minghui Zhou*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 文件级复制在开源生态中广泛存在但缺乏治理，它破坏了包管理器提供的供应链可见性。本研究通过混合方法分析了整个开源生态系统中的文件复制行为，发现13种轴向复制形式，其中依赖注入主要掩盖上游规避意图（70%），而硬件/驱动复制主要为分支维护（78%）。复制源普遍陈旧（中位155天），仅4.3%记录可恢复来源。安全风险集中在依赖注入形式（88%的CVE风险提交），许可风险集中在直接源代码复用（66%的候选违规）。这些风险通过打包软件传播，且对仅依赖声明元数据的依赖扫描器不可见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文件级复制是广泛存在但不受治理的软件复用形式，它破坏了包管理器提供的四个可观察信号（来源、维护、安全、合规），导致供应链可见性丧失。然而，这种无管理复用的规模和后果尚未得到系统性的量化研究。

Method: 使用World of Code（WoC）对全开源生态系统进行混合方法研究。从0.1%提交样本中提取690,500个复制事件，保留3,912个含意图标注的复制提交进行标记。对13种轴向复制形式进行编码分析，并在全WoC图中追踪CVE风险和许可违规。

Result: 1) 13种轴向复制形式是开发者意图的不可靠代理：硬件/驱动复制78%为分支维护，依赖注入70%预示上游规避。2) 复制源陈旧（中位155天，38.5%超过1年），仅4.3%记录可恢复来源，2.0%记录可检查版本。3) 安全风险集中在依赖注入：17,314个CVE风险提交，88%为依赖注入形式，80%评分CVSS≥7.0，上游修复采纳率仅47%-84%。4) 许可风险集中在源代码复用：41,777个预验证候选，66%为源代码形式，39个高星验证违规（kappa=0.752）。5) 两种风险均进入打包软件且对基于声明元数据的依赖扫描器不可见。

Conclusion: 文件级复制造成的可见性缺口具有形式特异性，安全和许可风险集中在互补的复制形式上。当前依赖扫描器仅检查声明元数据，无法检测这些风险，亟需新的供应链可见性恢复机制。

Abstract: File-level copying is a widespread but ungoverned form of software reuse. Copying files across repositories reduces supply-chain visibility: it removes the four observable signals a package manager provides for a declared dependency (provenance, maintenance, security, and compliance) with no mechanism to restore them. To characterize the scale and consequences of this unmanaged reuse, we present a mixed-method study of copying across the entire open-source ecosystem using World of Code (WoC). From a 0.1% commit sample, we extract 690,500 copy events and retain 3,912 rationale-bearing copy commits for intent labeling. We show that the 13 axial copy forms, spanning vendored dependencies, hardware/driver synchronization, scaffolding, UI assets, and direct source-code reuse, are unreliable proxies for developer intent: among rationale-bearing commits, hardware/driver copies are predominantly fork-maintenance work (78%), while dependency-vendoring copies more often signal upstream bypass (70%) than offline availability. These visibility gaps are form-specific: security and license risk concentrate in complementary copy forms. Copied sources are frequently stale (median 155 days; 38.5% over one year old) and seldom record a recoverable origin (4.3% documented), let alone a checkable version (2.0% versioned); even vendored copies record where they came from only 10% of the time. Security risk concentrates in vendored dependencies: 17,314 CVE-risk copy commits in the full-WoC graph, 88% in the dependency-vendoring form; 80% score CVSS >= 7.0 and upstream-fix adoption is only 47%-84%. License risk concentrates in direct source-code reuse: 41,777 pre-validation candidates, 66% in the source-code form, with 39 verified high-star violations (kappa = 0.752). Both risks reach packaged software and are invisible to dependency scanners operating on declared metadata alone.

</details>


### [38] [Coding Agents Are Guessing: Measuring Action-Boundary Violations in Underspecified DevOps Instructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02294)
*Zimo Ji,Zekai Zhang,Congying Xu,Zongjie Li,Yudong Gao,Shuai Wang,Shing-Chi Cheung*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 UnderSpecBench 基准，用于评估编码代理在 DevOps 任务中面对模糊指令时的动作边界违规行为。实验表明，55.8-67.8% 的运行违反了至少一条安全边界，说明以任务完成度为中心的评估会高估代理的安全自主性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代理基准主要关注任务完成度，忽视了在良性但模糊的指令下代理的安全行为。错误的动作可能造成严重后果，因此需要系统评估代理在指令不明确时的边界违规行为。

Method: 构建包含69个任务族的 UnderSpecBench 基准，覆盖四个 DevOps 能力域和九个操作控制面。每个任务保持相同环境和真实安全动作，沿意图清晰度、目标确定性和爆炸半径三个维度变化指令，生成2,208个提示变体。使用基于确定性副作用的神谕评估安全成功、错误目标和越界三类结果。

Result: 在五个代理×模型配置上的评估显示，55.8-67.8% 的运行违反至少一条安全边界。模糊性主要导致代理猜测而非失败。目标不确定性严重降低动作质量，而爆炸半径提示几乎不减少动作倾向。

Conclusion: 以任务完成度为中心的评估会高估代理的安全自主性。需要在模型层、工具层和系统层进行缓解措施来提升编码代理在模糊指令下的安全性。

Abstract: LLM coding agents are increasingly deployed to act autonomously on real production infrastructure. They execute shell commands, modify repositories, and call operational APIs. However, completing a task is not sufficient for safety. A wrong action can cause severe consequences. Existing agent benchmarks largely emphasize task completion, leaving open how agents behave under benign but underspecified instructions.
  We present UnderSpecBench, a benchmark for measuring action-boundary violations in coding agents (i.e., Claude Code, Codex, and OpenCode) on DevOps tasks. UnderSpecBench includes 69 task families grounded in documented incidents, CVEs, or tool behavior and organized across four DevOps capability domains and nine operational control surfaces. To isolate underspecification from task difficulty, each task keeps the same environment and ground-truth safe action while varying the instruction along three axes: intent clarity, target certainty, and blast radius. The resulting 2,208 prompt variants are evaluated with deterministic, side-effect-based oracles that separate Safe Success, Wrong Target, and OverScope outcomes; non-action runs are further classified as clarification, refusal, or deferment.
  Across five agent x model configurations using OpenCode, Claude Code, and Codex, the evaluation results show that underspecification does not mainly make agents fail; it makes them guess. 55.8-67.8% of runs violate at least one boundary. Target underspecification sharply degrades action quality, while blast-radius cues barely reduce action propensity. These findings show that completion-centric evaluation can overstate safe autonomy and motivate mitigations at the model, harness, and system layer.

</details>


### [39] [Guiding Human Validation of LLM-Generated Code via Verifiable Literate Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02333)
*Ziqi Yuan,Wenhao Lu,Hao Wu,Dunhong Jin,Chuan Wu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出VLP（可验证文学编程）框架，通过自然语言文档作为可读中间层，让非专业用户也能参与LLM生成代码的审查与验证，显著提升代码可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的代码常因需求不明确或语义偏差而不可靠，现有验证方法（LLM辅助自动化测试、用户参与提示/测试用例）存在提示歧义、模型易错、遗漏边界情况等问题，亟需让用户能有效参与代码验证的方案。

Method: VLP框架包含三项技术：(i) 具有明确语法的文学编程语言，实现代码到文档的确定性翻译；(ii) 基于LLM的细粒度不匹配检测，通过提示与文档间的追踪链接定位可疑文档行；(iii) 验证模块，利用用户验证过的文档推导API用法检查与形式化属性，通过模型检验验证生成代码。

Result: VLP将代码pass@1从28.7%-73.2%提升至65.4%-93.5%，用户工作量合理。

Conclusion: VLP让各编程水平的用户都能有效审查和验证LLM生成代码，弥合自然语言意图与代码实现之间的语义鸿沟。

Abstract: Vibe coding democratizes software development by allowing users to generate code via natural-language (NL) interaction with large language models (LLMs). However, the code is reliable only when it faithfully implements the user's intent, which is difficult and labor-intensive for users to validate. Existing validation methods either rely on LLM-assisted automated testing, which suffers from prompt ambiguity and model fallibility, or involve users only in partial software artifacts such as prompts and test cases, which may overlook corner cases and program details. Motivated by a bug study of LLM-generated code, we find that detailed human feedback is essential, as failures often stem from underspecified requirements or subtle semantic deviations.
  This paper presents verifiable literate programming (VLP), a human-in-the-loop framework designed to make the review/validation process of LLM-generated code accessible to users at all programming levels. At its core, VLP proposes unambiguous NL-based documentation as a readable intermediate layer between prompts and code. The documentation demonstrates concrete program semantics and enables users to provide feedback on potential intent-code mismatches. It supports human-involved, end-to-end repair and validation via three techniques: (i) an NL-style literate language with unambiguous syntax and mostly deterministic code-to-documentation translation, (ii) LLM-based fine-grained mismatch detection that uses trace links between prompts and documentation to focus users' review effort on suspicious documentation lines, and (iii) a verification module that leverages user-validated documentation to derive API-usage checks and formal properties, which are then verified against the generated code using model checking. Our evaluation shows that VLP improves code pass@1 from 28.7%-73.2% to 65.4%-93.5% with reasonable user effort.

</details>


### [40] [Developers' Experience with Generative AI Beyond Productivity Assessment -- Insights from an Empirical Mixed-Methods Field Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02337)
*Charlotte Brandebusemeyer,Kerim Zunic,Thomas Zimmermann,Tobias Schimmer,Bert Arnrich*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过混合方法实证研究，探讨了专业开发者在真实工作环境中使用生成式AI（GenAI）的情况。研究发现开发者对GenAI整体满意，特别是在单调、重复和结构化任务中；Copilot的交互方式（代码内建议 vs 聊天式提示）偏好因任务类型和复杂度而异，且在同一任务中混合使用两种交互方式会降低收益。开发密集型任务中，认知负荷源于AI交互过程，而生产力感知取决于AI输出质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注AI编码助手的输出质量和生产力提升，而对开发者的幸福感、交互体验以及真实工作环境中的使用行为关注不足。本文旨在填补这一空白，从开发者中心视角出发，研究专业开发者在自然工作环境中与GenAI的实际互动。

Method: 采用以开发者为中心的实证混合方法研究，结合受控数据收集会话与自然工作周期，在专业开发者的真实工作环境中进行调查。

Result: 1) 开发者对GenAI总体满意，尤其适用于单调、重复和结构化任务；2) 代码内建议和聊天式提示各自独立提升任务效率并降低感知工作负荷，但同一任务中混合使用两种交互方式会削弱收益；3) 开发密集型任务中，认知负荷来自AI交互过程，生产力感知取决于AI输出质量；4) 参与研究提升了开发者对GenAI工具的意识和有意使用。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了在真实环境中采用混合方法研究设计的价值，以深入理解GenAI工具及开发者与其交互的体验，并提出了基于任务特征选择交互方式的经验法则。

Abstract: With the growing adoption of AI-powered coding assistants, organizations and developers are increasingly seeking to optimize their interaction with these tools. Prior research has largely focused on output quality and productivity gains, with limited attention paid to developers' well-being and interaction experiences. This paper presents a developer-centered empirical mixed-methods study to investigate how professional developers engage with Generative AI (GenAI) in their natural work environment. Controlled data collection sessions are combined with natural work periods. Results show that developers are generally satisfied with GenAI, particularly for monotonous, repetitive, and structured tasks, and report perceived efficiency and productivity gains. Copilot interaction type preferences differ by task type and complexity: While both in-code suggestions and chat-based prompting independently improve task efficiency and reduce perceived workload, combining these interaction types within a single task diminishes benefits. We propose a rule-of-thumb for selecting an interaction type based on task characteristics. During development-heavy tasks, results indicate that perceived cognitive load arises from AI interaction, while perceived productivity depends on AI output quality. Participation in this study positively influenced developers' awareness and intentional use of GenAI tools. These findings demonstrate the value of real-world, mixed-methods study designs to understand GenAI tools and developers' experiences with them.

</details>


### [41] [SkillFuzz: Fuzzing Skill Composition for Implicit Intents Discovery in Open Skill Marketplaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02345)
*Jinwei Hu,Yi Dong,Youcheng Sun,Xiaowei Huang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillFuzz，一种无需执行的测试方法，通过合约引导的蒙特卡洛树搜索发现LLM agents技能组合中的隐式意图冲突。在代表性技能市场工作负载上，SkillFuzz在固定查询预算下发现1000多个不同的隐式意图，80%以上的高风险组合在执行验证中得到确认。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于LLM agent的技能市场中，单个审核为良性技能在组合使用时可能产生交互效应，将agent导向非预期的目标（隐式意图）。由于效应仅在组合时显现、执行环境在准入时不可用、以及可能的组合空间随市场规模指数增长，检测这类隐式意图极具挑战性。

Method: 将隐式意图发现建模为技能组合的模糊测试问题：以技能组合为被测单元，规划产物在执行前暴露agent意图，技能自由基线作为差分预言。提出SkillFuzz，提取结构化技能合约，并使用合约引导的蒙特卡洛树搜索优先探索潜在冲突组合。

Result: 在固定查询预算下发现1000多个不同的隐式意图；超过80%的最高风险标记组合在执行时验证中得到确认；相比替代搜索策略，在仅探索成对交互空间的一小部分时，识别出更多高严重性隐式意图。

Conclusion: SkillFuzz通过无需执行的合约引导搜索方法，有效发现技能组合中的隐式意图，为LLM agent技能市场的安全性保障提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-based agents increasingly automate software engineering tasks through reusable skills, natural-language instruction documents that guide planning and execution. Open skill marketplaces enable users to assemble agents by co-activating community-contributed skills, but marketplace operators typically audit skills in isolation. As a result, individually benign skills may interact to redirect an agent toward unintended objectives, which we term implicit intents. Detecting such intents is challenging because the effect emerges only through skill composition, execution environments are often unavailable at admission time, and the space of possible co-activations grows exponentially with marketplace size. In this paper, we formulate implicit-intent discovery as a fuzzing problem over skill compositions, where skill compositions are the unit under test, planning artifacts expose agent intent before execution, and deviations from a skill-free baseline serve as a differential oracle. Based on this formulation, we propose skillfuzz, the first execution-free testing approach that extracts structured skill contracts and uses contract-guided Monte Carlo Tree Search to prioritize potentially conflicting compositions. Across representative skill-marketplace workloads, skillfuzz discovers over 1,000 distinct implicit intents under a fixed query budget, confirms more than 80% of the highest-risk flagged compositions during execution-time validation, and identifies substantially more high-severity implicit intents than alternative search strategies while exploring only a fraction of the pairwise interaction space they require.

</details>


### [42] [Understanding Agent-Based Patching of Compiler Missed Optimizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02370)
*Batu Guan,Zirui Wang,Shaohua Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了智能体修复编译器遗漏优化的能力，发现修复需要泛化到相似案例而非仅修复报告案例，构建了LLVM遗漏优化基准，并提出了历史知识增强技术来提升泛化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编译器遗漏优化（compiler missed optimizations）是编译器开发中常见且棘手的问题，修复这类问题需要开发者不仅修复报告的具体案例，还要泛化到类似场景。然而，目前缺乏对智能体（coding agents）在此类任务中表现的系统性研究。

Method: 构建了一个基于真实LLVM遗漏优化问题的基准测试，对比智能体生成的补丁与开发者补丁在优化范围上的差异。提出了历史知识增强技术（historical-knowledge augmentation），通过检索和蒸馏利用历史LLVM优化PR来提升智能体的泛化能力。

Result: 智能体通常能优化给定示例，但生成的补丁要么只覆盖开发者意图范围的一部分，要么部分重叠；有时会超出参考补丁的泛化范围。历史知识增强技术能改善与开发者对齐的泛化能力，并在实际IR上产生实用收益。

Conclusion: 修复编译器遗漏优化需要超越具体案例的泛化能力，而历史知识增强是一种有效的改进方向。

Abstract: Compiler missed optimizations refer to cases in which compilers failed to optimize certain code. It takes many compiler developers' efforts to implement or patch such missed optimizations. In this paper, we present a systematic study of how well agents patch compiler missed optimizations. We identify a significant challenge that patching a missed optimization requires more than just fixing the reported case, and instead requires generalizing to similar cases. We construct a benchmark of real-world LLVM missed optimization issues and compare agent-generated patches with patches from developers in terms of optimization scope. Our results show that coding agents often optimize the given examples, but many generated patches either cover only part of the developer-intended scope or partially overlap with it; in some cases, they further generalize beyond the reference patch. We further introduce historical-knowledge augmentation techniques that leverage prior LLVM optimization pull requests through retrieval and distillation, showing that they improve developer-aligned generalization and yield practical benefits when applied to real-world IR.

</details>


### [43] [Reasoning effort, not tool access, buys first-try reliability in agentic code generation: an observational study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02436)
*Achint Mehta*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统测试了“更多能力=更好软件”的假设，通过90次独立智能体运行构建同一应用并评分。结果表明：能力层级是主导因素（前沿模型接近满分，低成本本地模型仅24-37分）；容器部署是最常见缺陷（44%首次失败）；测试工具增加42-68%成本但未改善功能分数；将推理努力从High提升至xHigh使首次完美运行从28%升至89%；设计导向提示显著提升视觉质量（4.5 vs 3.0）。核心教训：应根据失败根源匹配修复措施——多数首次失败源于推理能力不足，而非可见缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前智能体编码助手被赋予越来越多额外能力（如基于浏览器的测试工具、面向设计的系统提示），但“更多能力是否真的带来更好软件”这一核心假设缺乏直接验证。本研究旨在通过严格控制变量的实验，系统检验这一假设。

Method: 90次独立智能体运行构建同一实时回顾板应用（由一份详细规格说明指导），采用固定14项标准功能评分量表（42分满分）和视觉质量评审。运行覆盖多个模型世代、两种智能体框架、两种推理努力级别、一种测试工具以及两种面向设计的提示。

Result: (1) 能力层级主导：前沿模型接近满分天花板，低成本本地模型仅24-37分；(2) 容器部署是最常见缺陷（44%首次尝试失败），且失败率随模型世代变化剧烈；(3) 测试工具使成本增加42-68%，但未改善功能得分或可靠性；(4) 推理努力从High提升至xHigh使首次完美运行从28%升至89%，纠正提示减少约5倍；(5) 设计导向提示将视觉质量从3.0提升至4.5（5分量表），但不影响功能得分；一段简短改写即复现全部提升。

Conclusion: 实际教训是应根据失败原因匹配修复措施：大多数首次运行失败源于推理能力不足（可通过更强模型或更多推理努力预防），而非测试工具能捕捉的可见缺陷。

Abstract: Agentic coding assistants are increasingly given extra capabilities, such as browser based testing tools and design oriented system prompts, on the assumption that more capability yields better software. This study tested that assumption directly. Ninety independent agent runs built the same application, a real time retrospective board, from one detailed specification, each scored on a fixed 14 criterion functional rubric (42 point maximum) and a visual quality review. The runs spanned several model generations, two agent harnesses, two reasoning effort levels, a testing tool, and two design oriented prompts. Capability tier dominated: frontier models clustered near the ceiling while a low cost local model fell to 24 to 37 points. A criterion level analysis revealed what run totals conceal. Container deployment was the dominant defect, failing first try in 44 percent of runs, with its failure rate shifting sharply across model generations while mean totals moved less than a point. The testing tool raised cost by 42 to 68 percent without improving functional score or reliability, even on interface visible criteria. Raising reasoning effort from High to xHigh lifted first try perfect runs from 28 percent to 89 percent and cut corrective prompts about five fold, for 9 to 29 percent more cost. A design oriented prompt raised visual quality, 4.5 versus 3.0 on a 5 point scale, without lifting function, and a one paragraph paraphrase of its directive reproduced the entire lift. The practical lesson is to match the fix to the failure: most first run failures came from weak reasoning, which a stronger model or more effort prevents, not from visible flaws a checking tool would catch.

</details>


### [44] [HTTP REST API Structure Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02442)
*Ran Dubin,Amit Dvir*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出HRAL方法，通过无监督学习直接从网络流量建模API的结构和行为，在无文档或部分文档场景下实现高效异常检测，平均召回率82.07%，F1-score 87.24%，结合签名规则可达100%检测率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: API在软件开发中广泛应用，但带来了显著安全风险。现有方法依赖预定义规则或完整文档，在文档不完善的真实场景中效果有限。

Method: HRAL通过无监督学习直接分析网络流量，建模API端点的结构和行为特征，不依赖预定义规则或文档，将偏离正常行为的流量标记为潜在威胁。

Result: 平均召回率82.07%，F1-score 87.24%；在API文档有限时显著优于现有方法；结合OWASP ModSecurity CRS签名规则达到100%检测率。

Conclusion: HRAL在部分文档化的真实API环境中表现出色，可作为现代API安全解决方案的基础层，有效弥补文档不完整场景下的安全检测空白。

Abstract: Application Programming Interfaces (APIs) are essential in software development, enabling web services, mobile apps, and microservices. However, their widespread use introduces significant security risks, highlighting the importance of API security. This paper presents HTTP REST API Learning (HRAL), a novel unsupervised anomaly detection approach that models the structure and behavior of API endpoints directly from network traffic, without relying on predefined rules or documentation. HRAL enables robust detection of malicious activity by understanding how APIs behave and flagging deviations as potential threats. We evaluate HRAL across varying levels of OpenAPI documentation detail and compare it with existing techniques. HRAL achieves strong performance, with an average recall of 82.07% and an F1-score of 87.24%, significantly outperforming alternatives when API documentation is limited. Moreover, our results approach the effectiveness of full API document definitions. When combined with signature-based rules such as the OWASP ModSecurity CRS, our system achieves 100% detection. These results highlight HRAL's effectiveness in real-world, partially documented API environments and its potential as a foundational layer for modern API security solutions.

</details>


### [45] [TestEvo-Bench: An Executable and Live Benchmark for Test and Code Co-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02469)
*Jiale Amber Wang,Kaiyuan Wang,Pengyu Nie*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: TestEvo-Bench是一个基于真实仓库的测试与代码协同演化基准，包含746个测试生成和509个测试更新任务，支持执行级指标评估。实验表明最先进智能体在测试生成上达77.5%成功率，测试更新上达74.6%，但在最新任务和有限预算下性能显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试生成和更新基准往往将测试与代码变更隔离，依赖无法验证测试是否可执行或语义关联的静态元数据，难以评估测试自动化智能体是否理解代码变更应如何传播到测试套件中。

Method: 从152个开源Java项目中挖掘59,950个协同演化记录，筛选出746个测试生成任务和509个测试更新任务。每个任务锚定于真实提交历史，打包环境配置以支持通过率、覆盖率和变异得分等可执行指标。采用动态基准设计，记录时间戳并通过自动化流水线定期挖掘新任务，可限制评估模型训练截止日期之后的任务以降低数据泄露风险。

Result: 实验使用四个最先进智能体（Claude Code、Gemini CLI、SWE-Agent结合Claude Opus 4.7和Gemini 3.1 Pro），测试生成成功率达77.5%，测试更新成功率达74.6%。但最新基准任务上的成功率明显较低，且在有限每任务预算下性能大幅下降。

Conclusion: TestEvo-Bench提供了更逼真的测试与代码协同演化评估环境，当前智能体虽能取得不错成绩，但在处理最新任务和资源受限场景下仍有显著提升空间，表明该方向仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: Software tests and code evolve together: a code change should be followed by new or updated tests that record the new software behavior. Yet existing test generation and update benchmarks often isolate the test from the code change, and rely on static metadata that does not verify whether a test is executable or semantically tied to the code change. This makes it difficult to evaluate whether a test automation agent understands how a code change should propagate into the test suite.
  We introduce TestEvo-Bench, a benchmark of test and code co-evolution tasks mined from software repositories, with two tracks: in test generation, the agent shall write new tests to capture the new software behavior; in test update, the agent shall adapt failing existing tests to the changed software behavior. Each task is anchored to a real commit history and packaged with environment configuration to support execution-grounded metrics such as pass rate, coverage, and mutation score. TestEvo-Bench is also a live benchmark: each task records the timestamp of the test and code changes, and new tasks are periodically mined by our automated pipeline, so evaluation can be restricted to tasks postdating a model's training cutoff to reduce data leakage risk. The current snapshot contains 746 test generation and 509 test update tasks, curated from 59,950 candidate co-evolution records across 152 open-source Java projects. We experiment with four state-of-the-art agents that combine strong harnesses (Claude Code, Gemini CLI, and SWE-Agent) with strong foundation models (Claude Opus 4.7 and Gemini 3.1 Pro). Results show that they achieve up to 77.5% success rate on test generation and 74.6% on test update. However, success rate is materially lower on the most recent benchmark tasks and drops significantly under limited per-task cost.

</details>

### [46] [PACE: A Neuro-Symbolic Framework for Plausible and Actionable Counterfactual Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01306)
*Pavel Iakovets,Liyanapathiranage Sudeepika Wajirakumari Samarathunga,Martin Thomas Horsch,Fadi Al Machot*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PACE是一个模块化神经符号框架，通过将神经网络预测与符号推理（ASP规则）分离，在生成反事实解释时显式融入领域知识和干预约束，从而产生既有效又可行的解释。在Adult Income数据集上的案例研究验证了该方法在保持可解释性的同时满足领域特定可行性要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反事实解释方法虽然能生成改变预测结果的输入变化，但由于缺乏融入领域知识和干预约束的显式机制，常常产生不现实或不可行的推荐。

Method: 提出PACE框架，采用模块化神经符号架构，将预测和推理分离为两个组件：神经预测模型（如MLP）执行分类，符号推理层（基于ASP规则）在反事实生成过程中强制执行领域特定约束，显式建模可行的干预操作。

Result: 在Adult Income数据集上的案例研究表明，符号约束生成的反事实解释比无约束方法更好地满足领域特定的可行性要求，同时揭示了反事实有效性与合理性之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 神经符号方法能够有效生成透明、可行性感知的反事实解释，该框架具有模型无关性和领域适应性，适用于需要现实决策支持的实际场景。

Abstract: Counterfactual explanations explain machine learning predictions by identifying minimal input changes that would alter a model's decision. Although many existing methods successfully generate prediction-changing alternatives, they often produce unrealistic or infeasible recommendations due to a lack of explicit mechanisms for incorporating domain knowledge and intervention constraints. Neuro-symbolic AI offers a promising direction by combining data-driven predictive models with symbolic reasoning capable of representing human-understandable rules and feasible actions. This paper presents PACE, a modular neuro-symbolic framework for generating feasibility-aware counterfactual explanations. The framework separates prediction and reasoning into two components: a neural predictive model for classification and a symbolic reasoning layer that enforces domain-specific constraints during counterfactual generation. By explicitly modeling feasible interventions, the framework produces explanations consistent with domain knowledge while remaining interpretable and actionable. The approach is model-agnostic and adaptable to domains requiring realistic decision support. A case study is conducted on the Adult Income dataset, combining a multilayer perceptron classifier with Answer Set Programming (ASP) rules encoding feasible modifications to education, occupation, and working hours while preserving immutable attributes. Results highlight the trade-off between counterfactual validity and plausibility and show that symbolic constraints yield explanations that better satisfy domain-specific feasibility requirements, illustrating the potential of neuro-symbolic methods for transparent, feasibility-aware counterfactual explanation in explainable AI.

</details>


### [47] [Auto-FL-Research: Agentic Search for Federated Learning Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01366)
*Holger R. Roth,Ziyue Xu,Chester Chen,Daguang Xu,Peter Cnudde,Andrew Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Auto-FL-Research (AFR)，一种基于约束编码智能体的联邦学习算法配方搜索工作流，在五个FLamby医疗任务和六个LEAF任务上评估，部分任务取得性能提升，但同时暴露出种子敏感性和搜索选择的失败案例，表明需区分重复的FL机制、固定曲面调优效果和单次运行伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习研究中存在大量微小但重要的算法选择（优化器变体、服务器聚合规则、本地训练策略、正则化、模型架构等），这些选择的手动探索成本高昂且难以公平比较，因为候选变更可能同时改变FL训练或评估路径。

Method: 提出Auto-FL-Research (AFR)，一种约束编码智能体工作流，智能体可提出并实现候选训练算法（包括服务器聚合规则、客户端更新调度、本地目标函数和注册模型变体），任务配置文件固定突变表面、计算预算、通信协议和最终模型评估。每次实验记录候选分数、运行时间、编辑文件、产物和失败状态。

Result: 在五个FLamby跨中心医疗任务和六个LEAF任务（含合成任务）上进行五种子重复评估，四个FLamby任务和五个LEAF任务取得性能提升，但也暴露出种子敏感性和搜索选择的失败案例。与相同预算的对照实验表明，部分提升对应FL配方变化，其他改进可通过固定曲面标量控制复现，或在重复/保留评估下失败。

Conclusion: 混合结果表明智能体生成的候选方案可区分为三类：重复的FL机制、固定曲面调优效果和单次运行伪影，这对理解自动化FL算法搜索的有效性具有重要启示。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) research often depends on many small but consequential algorithmic choices: optimizer variants, server aggregation rules, local training schedules, normalization, regularization, and model architecture. These choices are expensive to explore manually and difficult to compare fairly when candidate changes can also alter the FL training or evaluation path. In this work, we present Auto-FL-Research (AFR), a constrained coding-agent workflow for FL algorithmic recipe search. Agents may propose and implement candidate training algorithms, including server aggregation rules, client update schedules, local objectives, and registered model variants, while task profiles fix the mutation surface, compute budget, communication contract, and final model evaluation. Each campaign records candidate scores, runtime, edited files, artifacts, and failure status. We evaluate AFR on five healthcare cross-silo FLamby tasks and on grouped-client profiles for the five fixed LEAF datasets plus the LEAF synthetic task. Five-seed repeat evaluations support gains on four FLamby tasks and five of six LEAF profiles, while also exposing seed-sensitive and search-selected failure cases. Same-budget controls show that several gains correspond to FL-recipe changes, whereas other improvements are recovered by fixed-surface scalar controls or fail under repeat or held-out evaluation. These mixed outcomes are part of the contribution: they show how agent-generated candidates can be separated into repeated FL mechanisms, fixed-surface tuning effects, and selected single-run artifacts.

</details>


### [48] [The Wiola Architecture for Efficient Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01394)
*Aryuemaan Kumar Chowdhury,Afreen Shaik,Yaparla Bhargavi,Brahma Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Wiola 是一种从头构建的全新小型语言模型架构，包含五种创新组件：螺旋旋转位置编码（SRPE）、门控跨层注意力（GCLA）、自适应Token合并（ATM）、双流前馈网络（DSFF）和WiolaRMSNorm归一化，在四种参数规模（120M至1.5B）上发布并兼容HuggingFace生态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型（如GPT、LLaMA、Mistral等）共享相似的结构谱系，缺乏真正从零开始设计的独特架构。作者旨在提出一种完全原创的SLM架构，通过多个独立创新的组件来探索超越现有范式的新设计空间。

Method: Wiola包含五项创新：1）SRPE：在三维螺旋流形上嵌入位置，融合绝对、相对和层次化位置信号；2）GCLA：每个解码器层可软注意力访问前两层的压缩摘要，增强跨层连贯性；3）ATM：在中间层动态合并语义冗余的相邻token，降低注意力复杂度；4）DSFF：用两个并行流经学习门控融合替代传统MLP；5）WiolaRMSNorm：引入每维度可学习的偏移向量防止表示坍缩。

Result: Wiola发布了四个参数规模（120M、360M、700M、1.5B）的模型，与GPT-2、LLaMA-2、Mistral进行了系统比较，提供了完整的数学推导、架构框图、复杂度分析，所有22个架构单元测试均通过且完全兼容HuggingFace Transformers。

Conclusion: Wiola证明了从头构建完全原创的语言模型架构是可行的，其多项独立创新组件可能为未来SLM设计提供新的方向，且该架构已被实证验证可正常训练和部署于现有生态系统。

Abstract: We present Wiola, a fully original Small Language Model (SLM) architecture built from first principles, sharing no structural lineage with any existing model family including GPT, LLaMA, Mistral, or Falcon. Wiola introduces five independently novel components: (i) Spiral Rotary Positional Encoding (SRPE), which embeds token positions on a three-dimensional helical manifold combining absolute, relative, and hierarchical positional signals; (ii) Gated Cross-Layer Attention (GCLA), providing each decoder layer with soft cross-attention access to compressed summaries of two preceding layers for inter-layer coherence; (iii) Adaptive Token Merging (ATM), which dynamically merges se mantically redundant adjacent tokens in middle network layers to reduce attention complexity without information loss; (iv) Dual Stream Feed-Forward (DSFF), replacing the conventional MLP with two parallel streams fused by a learned per-dimension gate; and (v) WiolaRMSNorm, a modified normalisation introducing a per-dimension learned offset vector that prevents representation collapse. We provide complete mathematical derivations, architectural block diagrams, complexity analyses, and systematic comparisons against GPT-2, LLaMA-2, and Mistral. Wiola is released in four sizes (120M, 360M, 700M, and 1.5B parameters) and is fully compatible with the HuggingFace Transformers ecosystem, with all 22 architectural unit tests passing.

</details>


### [49] [Agent4cs: A Multi-agent System for Code Summarization in Large Hierarchical Codebases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01425)
*Yongjian Tang,Ezgi Sarikayak,Doruk Tuncel,Jie M. Zhang,Thomas Runkler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Agent4cs是一个多智能体框架，采用自底向上方式对大型代码库进行摘要生成，包含摘要生成、关键词提取和质量保证三个智能体，在7个前沿模型上平均提升语义一致性8%，关键词覆盖率提升最高达38%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码摘要方案多依赖单一语言模型或编码助手（如Claude Code），将源代码视为纯文本处理，未能充分利用代码仓库中丰富的相互依赖关系和层次结构信息，导致对复杂、混淆结构且文档不完整的大型代码库理解困难。

Method: 提出Agent4cs多智能体框架，采用自底向上的摘要生成方式：摘要生成智能体负责生成稳健摘要；关键词提取智能体主动识别子文件夹中的关键信息；质量保证智能体迭代优化输出的可读性、连贯性和完整性。

Result: 在7个前沿模型上，Agent4cs相比两个结构化提示基线（含代码段）在所有文件夹层级上平均提升语义一致性8%；在真实数据集上，归一化关键词覆盖率提升最高达38%。

Conclusion: 多智能体协作的自底向上框架能有效利用代码仓库的层次结构和依赖关系，显著优于传统基于单一模型和纯文本处理的代码摘要方法。

Abstract: Understanding large, complex codebases, especially those with obfuscated structures and incomplete documentation, remains a significant challenge. Existing code summarization solutions often rely on a single language model or coding assistant like Claude Code, and treat source code as flat text, underutilizing the rich interdependencies and hierarchical information within a repository. To address these shortcomings, we propose Agent4cs - a multi-agent framework that summarizes large codebases in a bottom-up fashion, where a summarization agent focuses on producing robust summaries; a keyword-extraction agent proactively identifies critical information from subfolders; and a quality-assurance agent iteratively refines the outputs for readability, coherence, and completeness. Evaluated on 7 frontier models, Agent4cs improves semantic consistency across all folder levels by average 8% compared to two structured prompting baselines with code segments. Furthermore, extensive evaluation on real-world datasets demonstrates up to 38% gains in normalized keyword coverage rate over the same baselines.

</details>

*Qian Chen,Chengyuan Liu,Xin Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种难度路由的服务控制架构，通过轻量级路由器将常规会话保持在低成本基线路径，将操作耦合的会话路由至升级工作流，该工作流利用冲突感知通信和写入触发重新考虑来集中安全防护，在零售和航空任务中提升了存在操作冲突的服务请求的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主客服代理从对话交互转向操作执行角色（如退款、取消、改单等），这带来了服务控制问题：企业需要保持常规服务的快速低摩擦，同时防止因客户指令、策略约束、企业记录和后端写入之间交互而导致的操作错误。

Method: 提出难度路由的服务控制架构，包含一个轻量级路由器，将常规会话保持在低成本基线路径，将操作耦合的会话路由至升级工作流；升级路径使用冲突感知通信和写入触发重新考虑，在进行关键后端写入前集中推演和安全防护。

Result: 在零售任务中，该方法在有操作冲突的服务请求上持续提升了可靠性；路由证据表明更强的控制被导向冲突请求而非泛化应用到常规请求；对话和工具使用分析显示增益并非来自无差别交互扩展，而是通过额外轮次和工具调用来支持证据收集、写入分离和写入前重新考虑。

Conclusion: 该架构通过差异化路由实现对不同复杂度请求的自适应控制，在保持常规服务效率的同时增强了对操作冲突场景的安全保障，在零售和航空两个领域均验证了有效性。

Abstract: Autonomous customer-service agents are shifting from conversational interfaces toward operational execution roles: they retrieve firm records, apply service policies, and execute backend writes such as refunds, cancellations, exchanges, order modifications, and reservation changes. This shift creates a service-control problem: firms must keep routine service fast and low-friction while preventing operational errors on requests where customer instructions, policy constraints, firm records, and backend writes interact. We propose a difficulty-routed service-control architecture that asks when service agents should reconsider before acting. A lightweight router keeps routine sessions on a low-cost baseline path and routes operationally coupled sessions to an escalated workflow. The escalated path uses conflict-aware communication and write-triggered reconsideration to concentrate deliberation and safeguards before consequential backend writes, rather than applying additional control uniformly across all service sessions. We evaluate the architecture on human-verified retail and airline tasks from $τ^{2}$-bench. In retail, the method improves reliability consistently on service requests with operational conflict. Routing evidence shows that stronger control is directed toward conflicted requests rather than broadly applied to routine ones. Dialogue and tool-use profiles suggest that gains do not come from indiscriminate interaction expansion or broader tool chains; instead, added turns and tool calls support evidence gathering, write separation, and pre-write reconsideration. Case-level evidence shows that the escalated workflow preserves fallback plans, binds retrieved records to the correct action, sequences writes, and decomposes multi-entity requests. Airline results extend the same service-control logic to reservation operations.

</details>


### [51] [CreativityNeuro: Steering Language Model Weights to Improve Divergent Thinking and Reduce Mode Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01433)
*Samuel Schapiro,Core Francisco Park,Felix Sosa,Lav R. Varshney*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需数据的方法CreativityNeuro，通过对比权重引导增强大语言模型的发散性思维，在多项创造力测试中显著提升性能，减少模式坍塌，且无需重新训练或微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在开放式问题上倾向于生成相似响应（人工 hive mind 效应），缺乏发散性思维和创造力多样性。

Method: CreativityNeuro：一种基于对比权重引导（contrastive weight steering）的无数据方法，通过在权重空间中进行引导来增强发散性思维，无需行为数据、重新训练或梯度微调。

Result: 1) 在发散性关联任务(DAT)上提升多达14个人类百分位点；2) 大规模人工评估(N=720)中，在替代用途测试(AUT)和Task Task上显著提升原创性、惊喜度和创造力；3) 在所有三项任务中减少模式坍塌；4) 激活引导在DAT上表现相当，但无法泛化到AUT和Task Task，而权重空间引导具有更好的泛化性。

Conclusion: CreativityNeuro能有效增强LLM的发散性思维、提升创造力并减少模式坍塌，是一种简单直接、无需数据且可泛化的方法。

Abstract: Divergent thinking is a crucial aspect of creativity, yet large language models (LLMs) tend to consistently generate similar responses to open-ended questions, in what has been termed the artificial hivemind effect. Here, we introduce CreativityNeuro, a data-free method for enhancing divergent thinking in LLMs via contrastive weight steering. We evaluate our method across multiple creativity assessments and report several main findings. On the Divergent Association Task (DAT), a vocabulary-space creativity test, CreativityNeuro improves performance by up to 14 human percentile points. Next, in a large-scale human evaluation (N=720) on the Alternative Uses Test (AUT) and the Task Task, CreativityNeuro achieves significant improvements in originality, surprise, and creativity, transferring to longer-form and more open-ended tasks. Importantly, we find that across all three tasks, CreativityNeuro demonstrably reduces measures of mode collapse. Moreover, activation steering achieves comparable performance to CreativityNeuro on the DAT, but it does not transfer to the AUT and Task Task, demonstrating the effectiveness of weight-space steering in generalizing to unseen tasks. In conclusion, CreativityNeuro improves divergent thinking and reduces mode collapse without requiring behavioral data, re-training, or gradient-based fine-tuning, providing a straightforward way to enhance LLM performance in creative domains.

</details>


### [52] [Discrete Diffusion Language Models for Interactive Radiology Report Drafting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01436)
*Max Van Puyvelde,Halil Ibrahim Gulluk,Wim Van Criekinge,Olivier Gevaert*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次将混合专家扩散语言模型（DiffusionGemma-26B）应用于医学视觉问答，证明其与同规模自回归模型（Gemma-4-26B）性能相当甚至更优，同时解码速度提升3.5-4.4倍，并具备自回归模型缺乏的任意顺序填充能力，更适合医学报告补全场景。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学基础模型目前几乎全是自回归架构，而扩散语言模型在文本生成领域已展现竞争力。作者希望验证扩散模型在医学领域是否也能达到或超越自回归模型的性能，并利用扩散模型的独特优势（双向去噪、任意顺序填充）解决医学报告补全中的实际问题。

Result: 扩散模型在所有医学视觉问答数据集上匹配或超越自回归模型性能；微调后模型（3.8B活跃参数）与前沿视觉-语言模型竞争力相当；解码速度比自回归模型快3.5-4.4倍；具备任意顺序填充能力，可自动补全放射科医生指定的报告片段。

Conclusion: 扩散语言模型在医学视觉问答任务中不仅性能与自回归模型持平或更优，还具有更快的解码速度和独特的任意顺序填充能力，更适合处理医学报告中常见的简洁或不一致的表达，是自回归模型的有力替代方案。

Abstract: Diffusion language models, which generate text by denoising a token canvas bidirectionally instead of emitting tokens left to right, have become competitive with autoregressive (AR) generation. Medical foundation models, however, remain almost entirely autoregressive. We adapt a mixture-of-experts diffusion language model, DiffusionGemma-26B, and benchmark it against its same-size AR sibling Gemma-4-26B under an identical LoRA recipe on medical visual question answering datasets, scored by a verbosity-robust LLM judge. Diffusion matches or exceeds AR on all of them, and the finetuned model (3.8B active) is competitive with frontier vision-language models; its decoding is also 3.5-4.4x faster. Beyond this parity, the diffusion model offers a drafting capability AR lacks: any-order infill. Because the canvas is denoised bidirectionally, a radiologist can fix report fragments and have the model fill the text between them, an operation inherent to diffusion but not to autoregression, which is subpar at it. This suits real reports, which are often terse or inconsistent across clinicians and institutions.

</details>


### [53] [Beyond Next-Token Prediction: An RLVR Proof of Concept for Tool-Use Agents on Atlassian Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01465)
*Karthikeya Aditya Vissa,Sankalp Mane,Ananya Mantravadi,Harshit Rajgarhia,Abhishek Mukherji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究如何用强化学习（RLVR）解决LLM在企业级SaaS API场景中的目标不匹配问题。基于Jira和Confluence API的五种合成环境，用GRPO训练Qwen小模型，在四个非退化场景中将平均奖励从0.35-0.92提升至0.95-1.00，最大提升来自Confluence页面创建（0.35→1.00）。局限性：手工设计验证奖励难以规模化，且一个场景存在奖励饱和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM本质是下一词预测器，在调用特定企业API（如Jira REST v3、Confluence v2）时存在目标不匹配，导致字段缺失、工具幻觉、过早停止等静默失败。作者希望验证直接对目标环境应用RLVR能否弥补这一差距。

Method: 构建五个高保真合成环境模拟Jira和Confluence API的模式；设计完全基于工具调用轨迹的可验证奖励函数（无真实API、无学习判官、无人工标注）；用GRPO算法在Qwen3-1.7B和Qwen3.5-4B上训练，用与训练相同的检查器评估奖励。

Result: 在四个非退化场景中，RL训练策略将平均奖励从4B基线的0.35-0.92提升至0.95-1.00。最大提升：Confluence页面创建从0.35到1.00。一个场景（ticket-transition）奖励饱和，4B基线已最大。

Conclusion: RLVR能有效提升小模型在企业级API调用中的表现，是向面向特定企业API的优化小模型迈出的初步步骤。但手工设计验证奖励难以规模化，且部分场景存在奖励饱和问题，限制了当前方法的适用范围。

Abstract: Large language models are trained to predict the next token, not to act inside a specific API. In niche enterprise SaaS workflows -- where success means hitting the right endpoint with the right nested arguments in the right order -- this objective mismatch shows up as silent failures: dropped required fields, hallucinated tools, or early stops after a single read. We ask whether Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR), applied directly in the target environment, closes the gap. As a proof of concept we build a suite of five synthetic environments emulating the Jira REST v3 and Confluence v2 APIs at schema fidelity; rewards are computed entirely from the tool-call trace, with no live API, no learned judge, and no human label in the loop. Scoring prompted Qwen3-1.7B and Qwen3.5-4B on the same checkers that drive GRPO training, we find that on the four scenarios whose rewards are non-degenerate the RL-trained policy lifts average reward from a 4B-baseline range of 0.35--0.92 to 0.95--1.00, with the largest single gain on Confluence page creation ($0.35 \rightarrow 1.00$). We position this as a preliminary step toward outcome-optimised small models for niche enterprise APIs, and foreground two limitations a workshop reader should weigh: hand-crafting verifiable rewards does not scale beyond the handful of endpoints reported here, and one of our five scenarios (ticket-transition) has a saturating reward shape that the prompted 4B already maxes out.

</details>


### [54] [World Feedback for Clinical Agents: Diagnosing RL in FHIR Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01470)
*Ananya Mantravadi,Harshit Rajgarhia,Prasanna Desikan,Abhishek Mukherji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文审计了MedAgentBench v1/v2发现41.7%的静默完成上限使得不作为成为RL主导策略，构建了MAB-v3（508任务，8.9%上限）。训练Qwen3-8B发现能力上限（10/20任务类型基性能为0）和格式知识障碍（3/20类型需要精确临床代码）两个结构性屏障。纯RL达到18.2% pass@1，基于规则的SFT达到34.1%，15.9个百分点的差距完全归因于这两个障碍。提出决策/格式知识/查找分类法预测RL可学习性并给出解决方案：SFT注入代码，RL学习条件判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床协议执行任务（检查化验值、应用阈值、生成结构化FHIR订单）天然适合从世界反馈中进行强化学习，但应用RL需要合理的反馈渠道和足够的基础能力。作者希望系统性地审计现有基准并识别RL应用于临床任务的结构性障碍。

Method: 审计MedAgentBench v1/v2发现静默完成上限；构建MAB-v3（508任务）；使用Qwen3-8B进行纯RL和基于规则的SFT训练对比；提出决策/格式知识/查找分类法分析RL可学习性。

Result: 纯RL达到18.2% pass@1 vs SFT的34.1%，15.9pp差距完全归因于能力上限和格式知识障碍。

Conclusion: 决策/格式知识/查找分类法可预测RL可学习性，SFT注入代码、RL学习条件判断是有效策略。

Abstract: Clinical protocol-execution tasks -- checking a lab value, applying a threshold, placing a correctly structured FHIR order -- are natural candidates for RL from world feedback: once clinical SMEs encode decision logic into a verifier, that verifier grades unlimited rollouts without per-episode annotation. But applying RL requires a sound feedback channel and sufficient base capability. We audit MedAgentBench v1/v2, find a 41.7\% silent-finish ceiling that makes inaction the RL dominant strategy, and construct \textbf{MedAgentBench-v3 (MAB-v3)} (508 tasks, 8.9\% ceiling). Training Qwen3-8B exposes two structural barriers: a \emph{capability ceiling} (10/20 task types have 0\% base performance, zero gradient) and a \emph{format-knowledge barrier} (3/20 types require exact clinical codes undiscoverable by exploration). Pure RL reaches 18.2\% pass@1 vs.\ 34.1\% for rule-based SFT; the 15.9~pp gap is attributable entirely to these barriers. A decision/format-knowledge/lookup taxonomy predicts RL learnability and prescribes the fix: SFT to inject codes, RL to learn conditionals.

</details>


### [55] [Procedural Memory Distillation: Online Reflection for Self-Improving Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01480)
*Ye Liu,Srijan Bansal,Bo Pang,Yang Li,Zeyu Leo Liu,Yifei Ming,Zixuan Ke,Shafiq Joty,Semih Yavuz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出过程性记忆蒸馏（PMD），通过将跨轮次/跨轨迹的过程性信号（原始轨迹、反思策略、重复行为模式）在线收集为可复用的过程性记忆，并蒸馏到策略权重中，在SCIence & Knowledge Benchmark（SCIKNOWEVAL）和LIVECODEBENCH上分别比SDPO提升3.8-5.5%和7.9-13.6%，且共进化设计（记忆与策略互相更新）是关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR及其自蒸馏变体（如SDPO）仅利用episode级别的验证信号更新策略，忽略了rollout中丰富的过程性信息，也无法捕捉跨episode和跨epoch的一致性策略模式、持续性失败模式等重复性信号。

Method: PMD构建三层抽象的过程性记忆：原始轨迹、自我反思策略与教训、跨问题的高层行为模式。通过在线方式从模型自身轨迹中提取，并用记忆条件化的自教师模型监督学生模型的rollout，实现策略与记忆的共进化：策略生成rollout更新记忆，记忆塑造监督信号更新策略。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B和OLMo3-Instruct-7B上，PMD相比SDPO在SCIKNOWEVAL提升3.8-5.5%，在LIVECODEBENCH提升7.9-13.6%。消融实验表明，冻结记忆或冻结策略均导致在SCIKNOWEVAL各域下降超过10%，验证了共进化的关键作用。

Conclusion: PMD通过将跨轨迹的过程性记忆蒸馏到策略参数中，显著优于仅用episode级信号的SDPO，且共进化设计是性能提升的核心。推理时无需额外记忆模块，模型完全自包含。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), along with recent selfdistillation variants such as SDPO, evaluates each rollout against a verifier and updates the policy from that episode-level signal. However, the richer procedural information in the rollout is rarely retained or reused. Across episodes and epochs, the model repeatedly encounters related problems under a changing policy, producing cross-episode signals that episode-local updates cannot capture: which strategies consistently pass verification, which failure modes persist, which patterns recur. We propose Procedural Memory Distillation (PMD), which converts these crossepisode signals into reusable procedural memory and distills it into the policy's weights during training. This memory functions as a training scaffold, absorbed into the policy itself, yielding a memory-free model at inference. PMD organizes the memory at three levels of abstraction: raw trajectories, self-reflected strategies and lessons, and higher-level behavioral patterns that recur across problems, all extracted online from the model's own trajectories. A memory-conditioned self-teacher draws on the accumulated experience to supervise the student on its own rollouts, enabling student to progressively internalize procedural knowledge within its parameters. The central design principle is co-evolution: the policy generates rollouts that update the memory, and memory shapes the supervision that updates the policy. Empirically, across Qwen3-8B and OLMo3-Instruct-7B, PMD improves over SDPO by 3.8-5.5% on SCIKNOWEVAL and 7.9-13.6% on LIVECODEBENCH. Co-evolution powers these gains: freezing either the memory or the policy trails PMD by more than 10% across SCIKNOWEVAL domains.

</details>


### [56] [The Agentic Garden of Forking Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01507)
*Jiacheng Miao,Jonathan K Pritchard,James Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现AI智能体能够复现人类研究者在数据分析中的主观变异，并通过引入m值（多重宇宙值）和Agentic Bootstrap方法，将选择性分析路径问题显式化，从而为评估科学证据的可信度提供新标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实证研究中不同分析选择会导致不同结论，但这些隐藏的"分叉路径"难以观察。作者希望利用AI智能体将这些路径显式化，并量化单一分析结果在合理分析空间中的极端程度。

Method: （1）让AI智能体扮演不同角色（personas），对同一数据进行分析，观察结论的分歧程度；（2）在42个人类研究团队分析同一移民数据集的对照实验中，比较AI与人类在效应量估计上的意识形态差距；（3）提出m值（multiverse value），定义为随机选出的分析路径产生比报告结果更极端结论的概率；（4）提出Agentic Bootstrap方法，利用AI智能体对合理分析路径进行采样，估计m值。

Result: AI智能体复现了人类研究者72%的意识形态效应量差距；86%的AI报告通过独立AI审核，78%通过多数人类专家审核；在人类移民研究中，13.5%的报告分析落在分析空间最极端的5%区间内（m<0.05）。

Conclusion: 科学证据不应仅由单一报告分析来评估，而应考察该分析在合理分析空间中的位置。Agentic Bootstrap使这一分布可观察，并将其转化为科学可信度的判断标准。AI智能体虽能放大选择性分析问题，但也提供了解决该问题的工具。

Abstract: Empirical research rarely admits a unique analysis. Different analytical choices can lead to different conclusions from the same data, yet these hidden forking paths are difficult to observe. We show that AI agents capture much of the analytical variation among human researchers while making these paths explicit. Across four high-stakes domains, assigning different personas is sufficient for AI agents to report divergent, often opposing, conclusions from the same data and question, with findings systematically aligned with those beliefs. In a study in which 42 human research teams analyzed the same immigration dataset, AI agents reproduced 72% of the human ideological gap in reported effect estimates. Despite reaching opposing conclusions, it is difficult to identify clear issues in each analysis based on the final AI reports: 86% passed independent AI review and 78% passed majority human expert review. These findings suggest that the central challenge is often not flawed analyses, but selective exploration and reporting from a large space of methodologically defensible analyses. AI agents may amplify this longstanding problem by making such exploration inexpensive and scalable. To address this, we introduce the m-value (multiverse value), the probability that an analysis path would produce a claim at least as extreme as the reported one. We further introduce Agentic Bootstrap, which estimates the m-value by using AI agents to sample plausible analysis paths. Applied to the human immigration study, 13.5% of reported human analyses fell in the most extreme 5% of the analysis space (m<0.05). Scientific evidence should therefore be evaluated not only by a single reported analysis but also by its position within the distribution of analyses that could reasonably have been reported. Agentic Bootstrap makes this distribution observable and turns it into a criterion for scientific credibility.

</details>


### [57] [Janus: a Playground for User-Involved Agentic Permission Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01510)
*Natalie Grace Brigham,Eugene Bagdasarian,Tadayoshi Kohno,Franziska Roesner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Janus，一个用于实现和评估用户参与式代理权限管理的游乐场系统。通过构建模块化系统Janus-Core和自动化评估框架Janus-Harness，作者在三个场景下评估了六种权限助手，发现用户输入对隐私安全至关重要，AI增强可降低认知负荷，但需要考虑用户权限疲劳等真实行为，且没有单一设计在所有场景下表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管存在多种代理权限管理方法，但用户在这一过程中的角色尚未得到充分探索。研究者希望系统性地理解用户应如何以及在何种程度上参与代理系统的权限管理过程。

Method: 构建了Janus双组件系统：Janus-Core（模块化代理系统，支持多种权限管理设计）和Janus-Harness（自动化评估框架）。基于概念模型识别关键设计轴，实现了六种覆盖设计空间的权限助手，并在三个场景和三个合成响应者上进行评估。

Result: 用户输入对加强隐私和安全至关重要；AI增强用户决策有助于降低认知负荷；真实用户行为（如权限疲劳）必须在系统设计中考虑；没有单一设计在所有上下文中表现最优。

Conclusion: 需要更原则性和上下文敏感的方法来在代理系统中部署权限助手。Janus已公开可用，以支持未来在这一代理系统设计维度上的研究。

Abstract: AI agents that autonomously execute tool calls on a user's behalf raise pressing questions about permission management: what role could users play, and what role should they play? Despite many proposed approaches, the user's role in agentic permission management remains under explored. We introduce Janus, a playground system for implementing and evaluating user-involved agentic permission management designs. Janus consists of two components: Janus-Core, a modular agentic system supporting a diverse spectrum of permission management designs, and Janus-Harness, an automated evaluation framework. Grounded in a conceptual model that identifies key design axes for user involvement, we implement six permission assistants spanning the design space and evaluate them across three scenarios and three synthetic responders. We demonstrate that user input is critical and can significantly strengthen privacy and security, that AI augmentation of user decisions can help reduce cognitive load, and that realistic user behavior including permission fatigue must be accounted for in system design. No single design performs optimally across all contexts, motivating a more principled and context-sensitive approach to deploying permission assistants in agentic systems. Janus is publicly available to support future investigation into this dimension of agentic system design.

</details>


### [58] [Revisiting Chain-of-Thought Reasoning under Limited Supervision: Semi-supervised Chain-of-Thought Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01511)
*Hongyang He,Jiuming Liu,Victor Sanchez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出半监督思维链学习框架Semi-CoT，利用未标注问题通过语义熵筛选低熵伪思维链作为训练信号，实验显示伪答案精度高但在部分数据集上效果有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CoT方法主要将推理链用作推理时的提示，很少将生成的推理轨迹作为半监督学习信号重复利用，缺乏利用未标注数据来增强CoT推理能力的机制。

Method: 提出Semi-CoT框架：对每个未标注问题采样多条伪CoT，估计答案级语义熵，选择低熵推理链作为可靠伪CoT演示，从而实现从推理时优化到半监督伪监督的扩展。

Result: 在AQuA、SVAMP、GSM8K和MultiArith上的初步实验显示，熵门控机制选择的高精度伪CoT的伪答案精度在91.36%到100%之间。SVAMP和GSM8K有轻微提升，AQuA出现负迁移，MultiArith达到性能天花板。

Conclusion: 未标注问题可以提供可靠的伪推理信号，但有效利用仍需更强的演示选择策略或学生训练机制。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning has emerged as an effective approach for activating latent reasoning capabilities in large language models. However, most existing CoT methods use reasoning chains mainly as inference-time prompts, while the generated reasoning traces are rarely reused as semi-supervised learning signals. In this report, we define \textbf{Semi-supervised Chain-of-Thought Learning} and propose \textbf{Semi-CoT}, a simple framework that uses unlabeled questions to construct pseudo reasoning supervision. Semi-CoT samples multiple pseudo-CoTs for each unlabeled question, estimates answer-level semantic entropy, and selects low-entropy reasoning chains as reliable pseudo-CoT demonstrations. This extends the self-training view of CoT from inference-time refinement to semi-supervised pseudo-supervision. Pilot experiments on AQuA, SVAMP, GSM8K, and MultiArith show that the entropy gate selects high-precision pseudo-CoTs, with pseudo-answer precision ranging from $91.36\%$ to $100\%$. Semi-CoT also gives small gains on SVAMP and GSM8K, while AQuA shows negative transfer and MultiArith reaches a ceiling. These results suggest that unlabeled questions can provide reliable pseudo reasoning signals, but their effective use still requires stronger demonstration selection or student training.

</details>


### [59] [OPINE-World: Programmatic World Modeling with Ontology-error-Prioritized Interactive Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01531)
*David Courtis,Wenhao Li,Scott Sanner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OPINE-World是一种基于LLM的智能体，通过在线交互学习面向对象的程序化世界模型。它将环境交互与代码合成相结合，使用贝叶斯本体误差度量引导探索，在ARC-AGI-3基准测试中无需逐游戏训练即解决了20/25个游戏，动作效率得分为78.4。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度网络世界模型数据效率低且分布外泛化差，而程序合成的世界模型在结构化状态世界中表现良好，但无法扩展到像素渲染环境，因为需要灵活假设对象结构且单一程序搜索不可行。

Method: OPINE-World采用两个协作智能体的假设-测试循环：一个在环境中执行动作，另一个通过重放验证和基于模型的规划以代码形式合成模型。同时引入贝叶斯度量的本体误差（ontology error）来引导探索，评估对象类型充分性。

Result: 在ARC-AGI-3基准测试中，OPINE-World无需每游戏训练即解决了20/25个游戏，动作效率得分为78.4（人类基线水平）。

Conclusion: 将LLM驱动的程序合成与在线交互探索相结合，能够高效学习面向对象的世界模型，在技能获取效率方面达到接近人类水平的表现。

Abstract: Learning how an environment behaves from interaction is central to building agents that adapt to unfamiliar tasks. World models learned with deep networks are flexible but data-hungry and transfer poorly beyond their training distribution. Program-synthesized world models, written as source code by LLMs and refined through counterexample-guided inductive synthesis (CEGIS), are instead data-efficient and reusable, yet they have been demonstrated mainly on structured-state worlds with a given object vocabulary, and a single program search does not scale to pixel-rendered environments whose object structure must be hypothesized flexibly. We introduce OPINE-World, an LLM agent that learns an object-centric programmatic world model online from interaction. OPINE-World couples two cooperating agents in a loop of hypothesis and test, one acting in the environment and one synthesizing the model in code with replay verification and model-based planning, and it steers exploration with a Bayesian measure of object-type adequacy we call ontology error. We evaluate OPINE-World on ARC-AGI-3, a benchmark for skill-acquisition efficiency in which the object vocabulary, the goal, and the action semantics are withheld. OPINE-World solves 20 of 25 games without per-game training and reaches an action-efficiency score of 78.4 against the human baseline.

</details>


### [60] [Scaling Trends for Lie Detector Oversight in Preference Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01567)
*Oskar J. Hollinsworth,Ann-Kathrin Dombrowski,Sam Adam-Day,Adam Gleave,Chris Cundy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文扩展了SOLiD（通过测谎仪的可扩展监督）方法到更大模型和更真实的偏好学习场景，发现有利的扩展性（未检测欺骗从34%降至14%），且可完全移除人工标注者而不显著增加欺骗，但该方法对分布偏移敏感，会导致虚警率过高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型中的欺骗行为难以监控和防范，现有方法如SOLiD通过测谎仪识别需高成本标注者审查的响应，但该方法此前仅在较小模型和有限场景中验证，亟需评估其在更大模型和更真实偏好学习环境中的可扩展性与有效性。

Method: 将SOLiD扩展到更大规模（1B至405B参数）的模型，在更多样化和更真实的偏好学习场景中评估，包括在检测器真阳性率为99%条件下测量未检测欺骗率，以及测试完全移除人工标注者的影响。

Result: 未检测欺骗率从1B模型的34%下降到405B模型的14%（检测器真阳性率99%）；可从微调阶段完全移除昂贵的人工标注者，而欺骗率无统计显著增加；但SOLiD对分布偏移敏感，导致检测器虚警率升至不实用水平。

Conclusion: SOLiD在大规模模型上展现出有利的扩展性，且有望实现无人工标注的微调，但其实际部署受限于对分布偏移的敏感性，需要进一步解决这一问题。

Abstract: Deceptive behavior in LLMs is costly to monitor and prevent, motivating approaches such as Scalable Oversight via Lie Detectors (SOLiD) (Cundy & Gleave, 2025), which uses lie detectors to identify responses for review by high-cost labelers. In this paper, we scale SOLiD to larger models and evaluate it in more diverse and realistic preference-learning settings. We find favorable scaling: undetected deception drops from 34% for 1B-parameter models to 14% for 405B-parameter models at a detector true positive rate of 99%, and expensive human labelers can be removed entirely from the fine-tuning phase without a statistically significant increase in deception. However, SOLiD is sensitive to distribution shift between detector training and preference-training data, which can drive detector false positive rates to impractical levels.

</details>


### [61] [EO-Agents: A Three-Agent LLM Pipeline for Earth Observation Hypothesis Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01584)
*Mahyar Ghazanfari,Amin Tabrizian,Armin Mehrabian,Peng Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于NASA地球观测知识图谱的假设生成管道，利用异构图表征学习和大语言模型协作，从1,475个数据集中生成了160个跨学科的地球科学假设，并揭示了LLM评估中单一审判者的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学假设生成方法大多依赖非结构化文献和自由文本，缺乏对结构化知识图谱的利用，难以系统性地挖掘大规模数据集之间的潜在关联。

Method: 首先使用异构图表征神经网络学习历史共现关系以排序候选数据集组合，然后通过一个三智能体LLM管道（生成、筛选、评估）产出结构化研究假设。

Result: 在1,475个NASA数据集上生成了160个跨域假设；模型预测的新颖数据对组合与文献中真实共现组合的合理性评分相当；2×2×2析因实验显示假设排序稳定但绝对评分受审判者身份影响显著。

Conclusion: 该管道能够产生科学连贯且未被探索的数据集组合，但LLM作为假设评估者存在单一审判者偏差问题，未来需引入更稳健的评估机制。

Abstract: Large language models have recently been explored for scientific hypothesis generation, but most prior work relies on unstructured literature and free-form textual claims. We present a pipeline for Earth observation that grounds hypothesis generation directly in the NASA Earth Observation Knowledge Graph. A heterogeneous graph neural network trained on historical co-usage relations ranks candidate dataset pairings, and a three-agent LLM pipeline filters, generates, and evaluates structured research hypotheses. Applied to 1,475 NASA datasets, the system produces 160 hypotheses spanning multiple Earth-science domains, including ecohydrology, glaciology, aerosol--cloud interactions, vegetation phenology, and stratospheric chemistry. Model-predicted novel dataset pairings are rated nearly as plausible as held-out real co-usages from the literature, indicating that the pipeline surfaces scientifically coherent yet unexplored combinations. A 2*2*2 factorial experiment across GPT-5.2 and Claude Sonnet 4.6 shows that hypothesis rankings remain stable, while absolute scores depend strongly on judge identity, highlighting limitations of single-judge LLM evaluation.

</details>


### [62] [Hawk: Harnessing Hardware-Aware Knowledge for High-Performance NPU Kernel Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01590)
*Junyi Wen,Ruiyan Zhuang,Yongjia Xu,Pengtu Li,Rui Zou,Hongyi Chen,Chingman Wan,Puxu Yang,Wuhui Chen,Yanlin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Hawk是一个无需训练、利用硬件感知知识提升NPU内核生成质量的框架，通过运行时知识合成、瓶颈感知检索和效果驱动蒸馏三大模块，将生成准确率从49.4%提升至80.0%，执行速度最高提升2.2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在NPU内核生成任务中因缺乏硬件先验知识而表现不佳，直接移植代码片段虽可通过编译器但常引发运行时崩溃和性能下降，亟需一种能融合硬件约束知识的自动化框架。

Method: Hawk包含三个核心模块：(1) 运行时知识合成模块，采用三重可执行知识表示将错误上下文与可执行语义固有耦合；(2) 瓶颈感知知识检索模块，实现2D检索范式将查询投影到正交的句法空间和硬件对齐的语义空间；(3) 效果驱动知识蒸馏模块，利用LLM驱动的语义仲裁，基于执行反馈不断裁剪错误、合并冗余以持续蒸馏知识。

Result: 在真实NPU工作负载上的评估表明，Hawk将生成准确率从49.4%提升至80.0%，执行速度相较于最先进基线最高提升2.2倍。

Conclusion: Hawk通过无需训练的硬件感知知识融合框架，有效解决了LLM在NPU内核开发中的局限性，显著提升了生成代码的正确性和执行效率。

Abstract: Developing high-performance kernels for Neural Processing Units (NPUs) is a critical industry bottleneck, requiring developers to manually navigate implicit hardware constraints and strict memory hierarchies. While large language models offer immense automation potential, they fail catastrophically on NPUs due to a fundamental lack of hardware-specific priors. Naively transplanting code snippets from similar NPU kernels may pass the compiler, but it consistently triggers runtime crashes and performance degradation by blindly violating underlying hardware constraints. To overcome this, we introduce Hawk, a training-free framework that harnesses hardware-aware knowledge through three core modules: (1) Run-Time Knowledge Synthesis Module, which employs a Triple-Part Executable Knowledge Representation to inherently couple the error context with executable semantics; (2) Bottleneck-Aware Knowledge Retrieval Module, which implements a 2D-Retrieval paradigm to project queries into orthogonal syntactic and hardware-aligned semantic spaces; and (3) Effect-Driven Knowledge Distillation Module, which leverages LLM-driven semantic arbitration to continuously distill the knowledge by pruning errors and consolidating redundancies based on the empirical execution feedback. Extensive evaluations on real-world NPU workloads demonstrate that Hawk elevates generation accuracy from 49.4% to 80.0%, while achieving up to a 2.2x execution speedup over state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [63] [Safe and Adaptive Cloud Healing: Verifying LLM-Generated Recovery Plans with a Neural-Symbolic World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01595)
*Junyan Tan,Haoran Lin,Siyuan Guo,Yichen Fang,Xinyue Luo,Tianyu Shen,Zeyu Qiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PASE是一个将故障自愈重新构思为神经符号程序合成任务的新型框架，通过LLM规划引擎、神经符号世界模型和DRL训练的元提示优化器三者紧密协同，实现动态、上下文感知的恢复策略生成，在真实云故障数据集上将平均恢复时间降低40%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法虽结合了LLM的语义理解与DRL的策略优化，但采用顺序、松散耦合的架构，未能充分利用LLM的生成和推理能力，难以应对日益复杂的云AI系统故障检测与自适应恢复挑战。

Method: 提出PASE框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) LLM作为核心的规划合成引擎，从语义原语库生成结构化恢复计划；(2) 神经符号世界模型通过仿真验证计划可行性；(3) 元提示优化器通过DRL训练学习生成最优提示以引导LLM规划过程。形成紧密的推理-规划-验证-自适应循环。

Result: 在真实云故障注入数据集上，PASE相比最先进方法将平均系统恢复时间降低超过40%，并在未知故障场景下提升了故障检测准确率。

Conclusion: PASE通过统一LLM推理、模型辅助验证和元学习引导，推进了自主系统管理的发展，实现了超越预定义动作空间的动态、上下文感知恢复策略生成。

Abstract: As the scale and complexity of cloud-based AI systems continue to escalate, ensuring service reliability through rapid fault detection and adaptive recovery has become a critical challenge. While existing approaches integrate Large Language Models (LLMs) for semantic understanding and Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) for policy optimization, they often rely on sequential, loosely coupled architectures that underutilize the generative and reasoning capabilities of LLMs. In this paper, we propose a paradigm shift with PASE, a Planning-Aware Semantic self-healing engine, a novel fault self-healing framework that reconceptualizes recovery as a neuro-symbolic program synthesis task. PASE employs an LLM as a core Plan Synthesis Engine to generate structured recovery plans from a library of semantic primitives. A Neural-Symbolic World Model verifies plan feasibility through simulation, while a Meta-Prompt Optimizer, trained via DRL, learns to generate optimal prompts that guide the LLM's planning process. This tight reason-plan-verify-adapt loop enables dynamic, context-aware recovery strategy generation beyond predefined action spaces. Experiments on a real-world cloud fault injection dataset demonstrate that PASE significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods, reducing average system recovery time by over 40% and improving fault detection accuracy in unknown fault scenarios. Our framework advances autonomous system management by unifying LLM-based reasoning with model-assisted verification and meta-learned guidance.

</details>


### [64] [SemHash-LLM: A Multi-Granularity Semantic Hashing Framework for Document Deduplication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01601)
*Xinyi Fang,Kejian Tong,Jiabei Liu,Tao Ning,Yuhang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SemHash LLM框架，通过多粒度语义投影哈希、注意力加权MinHash、对比边界学习和选择性LLM裁决，在大型语料库中实现高效且语义等价的文档去重，神经验证成本低于1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模文档去重需在保持语义等价性的同时，在超大规模语料上保持高效，现有方法在模板污染、短文本扰动、包含关系和病毒式片段等场景下鲁棒性不足。

Method: 提出SemHash LLM框架，包含：(1) 语义投影哈希——在蒸馏LLM嵌入空间学习紧凑二进制编码；(2) 注意力加权MinHash——抑制模板噪声，突出信息性内容；(3) 对比边界学习——自适应决策边界与不确定性估计；(4) 选择性LLM裁决——级联过滤流水线，仅对候选集调用LLM，验证成本低于1%。多粒度信号（字符、词元、文档级）通过门控融合机制整合。

Result: 实验表明SemHash LLM在多种去重场景下达到强检测质量，且神经验证成本（LLM调用）低于全部数据的1%。

Conclusion: 多粒度语义哈希结合级联过滤与选择性LLM裁决方案，能在极低计算开销下实现高质量的大规模语义级文档去重。

Abstract: Large scale document deduplication must preserve semantic equivalence while remaining efficient over massive corpora. We present SemHash LLM, a multi granularity framework that unifies semantic projection hashing, attention weighted MinHash, contrastive boundary learning, and selective LLM based adjudication. The method combines character, token, and document level signals through gated fusion, then applies a cascaded filtering pipeline for efficient candidate reduction. Semantic projection hashing learns compact binary codes in distilled LLM embedding space, while attention weighted Min- Hash suppresses boilerplate and emphasizes informative content. Adaptive decision boundaries and uncertainty estimation further improve robustness across template pollution, short text perturbation, containment, and viral fragments. Experiments show that SemHash LLM achieves strong duplicate detection quality with less than one percent neural verification cost.

</details>


### [65] [Profit-Based Counterfactual Explanations for Product Improvement: A Case Study of Manga Sales in Japan](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01610)
*Keita Kinjo,Takeshi Ebina*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于利润的反事实解释（PBCE）框架，将反事实解释重新定义为利润最大化问题，消除了传统方法中需要外生指定目标值和距离函数的局限，并在管理和营销场景中赋予了距离项以成本的经济学解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反事实解释方法在回归任务中存在两个关键问题：一是需要外生指定目标输出值，但其有效性未得到充分验证；二是距离度量的实际解释性不足。此外，大多数方法仅关注改变预测结果，而非优化实际决策目标，无法满足真实业务场景中的目标最大化需求。

Method: 将反事实解释形式化为利润最大化问题，以利润作为直接优化目标替代外生目标值，同时将距离项重新解释为修改产品属性的成本，从而赋予其明确的经济学含义。

Result: PBCE框架消除了对外生目标值的依赖，并将距离度量转化为具有经济可解释性的成本项，使反事实解释更贴合管理营销中的实际决策场景。

Conclusion: 通过将反事实解释与利润最大化目标对齐，PBCE在保持可解释性的同时提升了方法在实际决策中的实用性和经济合理性。

Abstract: Counterfactual explanation (CE) is widely used to enhance the interpretability of machine learning models and support data-driven decision-making based on model predictions. However, existing CE methods typically require two exogenously specified inputs: a desired output value (target) and a distance function that quantifies changes in explanatory variables. In regression settings, neither the validity of target specification nor the practical interpretation of the distance metric has been sufficiently addressed. Furthermore, most existing CE methods focus on altering predictions rather than optimizing a decision objective, even though real-world decision-making often requires explicit objective maximization. To address these limitations, we formulate CE as a profit maximization problem in management and marketing contexts and propose a framework termed profit-based counterfactual explanation (PBCE). PBCE eliminates the need for exogenous target specification by directly maximizing profit as the primary optimization objective. Concurrently, the distance term is reinterpreted as the cost of modifying product attributes, providing a clear and economically grounded interpretation.

</details>


### [66] [Scaling with Confidence: Calibrating Confidence of LLMs for Adaptive Test Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01612)
*Xuqing Yang,Yi Yuan,Shanzhe Lei,Xuhong Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出C3RL算法，将正确性、置信度校准和数据集参考准确率纳入强化学习奖励设计，提升大语言模型的置信度校准能力；并在此基础上提出CAS自适应推理策略，基于置信度动态分配计算资源，在降低推理成本的同时超越多数投票方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习奖励设计仅关注回答正确性，忽略了置信度校准，导致模型在不确定时过度自信地产生幻觉，准确性与置信度之间校准不良。

Method: 提出C3RL（Correctness and Confidence Calibration Reinforcement Learning），将正确性奖励、置信度校准奖励和基于数据集的参考准确率奖励三者融合。基于C3RL校准后的语言化置信度，进一步提出CAS（Confidence-based Adaptive Test Time Scaling），根据响应置信度动态调整推理计算资源分配。

Result: 在8个文本和多模态数据集上，C3RL在不牺牲准确率的前提下提升了校准性能，优于当前最先进方法。CAS在域内和域外数据集上超越多数投票，同时推理预算最多降低12.33倍。

Conclusion: C3RL与CAS的协同为部署更可靠、更资源高效的大语言模型铺平了道路。

Abstract: Training large language models (LLMs) with reinforcement learning (RL) has significantly advanced their performance on reasoning and question-answering tasks. However, prevailing RL reward designs typically prioritize response correctness, neglecting to incentivize models to express their confidence accurately. This leads to a critical problem: performance gains are often accompanied by poor calibration between confidence and accuracy, misleading models to overconfidently hallucinate when uncertain. To address this limitation, we propose $\textbf{C}$orrectness and $\textbf{C}$onfidence $\textbf{C}$alibration $\textbf{R}$einforcement $\textbf{L}$earning ($\textbf{C3RL}$), a novel RL algorithm integrating correctness, calibration and dataset-informed reference accuracy rewards together. Comprehensive evaluation across 8 text and multimodal datasets demonstrates that C3RL enhances calibration without sacrificing accuracy, outperforming the current state-of-the-art method in both performance and calibration metrics. Utilizing the well-calibrated verbalized confidence from C3RL, we further introduce $\textbf{C}$onfidence-based $\textbf{A}$daptive Test Time $\textbf{S}$caling ($\textbf{CAS}$), an adjustable inference-time strategy that allocates computational resources based on response confidence. Experiments show that CAS surpasses majority voting on both in-domain and out-of-domain datasets while reducing the inference budget by up to 12.33 times. We believe the synergy of C3RL and CAS paves the way for deploying more reliable and resource-efficient LLMs. The code, data and models will be released.

</details>


### [67] [Spatial Support Matters: Geometry-Aware Graph Fusion for Rainfall Field Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01621)
*Low Jun Yu,Niramay Kachhadiya,Herath Mudiyanselage Viraj Vidura Herath,Sanka Rasnayaka,Lucy Amanda Marshall*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种几何感知的多支持异构图神经网络，通过将雨量计（0D点）、微波链路（1D线）和雷达/卫星产品（2D网格）的不同测量支持表示为独立节点层，并利用跨支持消息传递进行融合，实现了精细尺度降雨场重建。在新加坡数据上相比IDW基线降低RMSE 23.2%，并揭示了多支持融合在观测稀疏时增益最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实降雨感知系统存在测量支持不兼容问题（点/线/面），现有异构图方法仅从特征空间融合而忽略了感知几何结构，导致空间约束信息丢失。

Method: 构建几何感知多支持异构图神经网络：将0D点、1D线、2D网格观测分别建模为独立节点层，通过跨支持消息传递融合信息，最终映射到点支持预测层进行降雨场重建。采用归纳式掩码节点机制解耦预测分辨率与感知分辨率。

Result: 新加坡数据上RMSE比IDW降低23.2%，优于卷积融合和支持无关异构图基线。悉尼泛化实验表明，当雨量计间距大于空间相关长度（即欠采样）时融合增益最大，反之增益有限。

Conclusion: 显式建模测量支持的几何结构能有效融合多源降雨观测，尤其在观测稀疏时显著提升重建精度，为城市洪涝建模提供更精细的降雨场输入。
