Method: 从最优控制理论中提取结构化的验证时诊断指标，在LunarLander v3环境中训练RSSM世界模型，以CEM-MPC回报作为闭环性能的oracle，评估40个指标与oracle的相关性，最终组合ROF与三个结构正则化器形成CROF分数。

Result: 奖励可观测性分数（ROF）是闭环性能的最强单指标预测器；CROF选出的世界模型训练的基于模型的A2C策略超越无模型A2C基线约24.5回报点，同时减少约65倍真实环境交互；该世界模型也能驱动强大的零样本CEM-MPC策略。

Conclusion: 基于最优控制理论的结构化验证时诊断指标（特别是ROF和CROF）能有效预测潜在世界模型的闭环性能，为选择高质量世界模型检查点提供了可靠方法。

Abstract: We study how to predict the downstream closed-loop performance of a learned latent world model from validation-time diagnostics alone. Choosing the right checkpoint from a world-model training run is difficult: validation loss and multi-step prediction RMSE keep improving long after closed-loop performance has collapsed. We present a suite of structural validation-time diagnostics drawn from optimal-control theory and apply them to Gymnasium's LunarLander v3, which features shaped rewards. We train an RSSM [5, 4] world model on it and treat per checkpoint CEM-MPC return as the oracle for closed-loop quality. By evaluating 40 metrics against this oracle, we find that the strongest single predictor is the Reward Observability Fraction (ROF), which measures the reward predictor's dependence on the observable subspace. We combine ROF with three structural regularizers into a single-number offline checkpoint selection score, the Composite Reward Observability Fraction (CROF). The CROF-selected world model trains a model-based A2C policy that beats a fairly evaluated model-free A2C baseline by ~24.5 return points while using ~65x fewer real-environment interactions, and the same world model also drives a strong zero-shot CEM-MPC policy. Code and data: https://github.com/nsmoly/LunarLander_RSSM.

</details>


### [177] [Finite-Lag Operator Geometry of Recurrent Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01746)
*Kanishka Reddy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了有限滞后算子几何框架，用于从观测到的源-后继对中分析循环隐状态的有向滞后流，并推导出源中心传输张量、反反对称坐标环流等量，证明了仿射协方差和有限滞后分离结果，在重复复制网络中揭示了架构依赖的传输差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环表征本质上是轨迹，但现有表示几何方法仅从静态快照测量，无法捕捉有向时间滞后动态。本文旨在开发一种能捕捉循环隐状态中有限滞后有向传输结构的几何框架。

Method: 从源-后继对(X_t, X_{t+Δ})出发，通过条件传输律Q_Δ(dy|x)和稠密高斯源平滑算子估计，导出源中心传输张量G_Δ（分解为条件扩散和相干位移）和反反对称坐标环流W_Δ^ρ；证明了仿射协方差、有界轨迹云的稠密集估计稳定性，以及有限滞后分离结果（源中心传输可检测确定性循环运动而无穷小几何无法检测）。

Result: 线性高斯闭式解校准了各量（更新矩阵A_Δ、源协方差、创新协方差）。控制实验验证了分解、环流、协方差和稳定性预测。在性能匹配的重复复制网络中，该框架揭示了架构依赖的总传输尺度和相干位移迹差异，而相干位移分数依赖于度量与分辨率。

Conclusion: 有限滞后算子几何能有效捕捉循环隐状态的有向动态结构，补充了传统静态几何方法，为分析循环网络表示提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: Recurrent representations are trajectories, but representation geometry is often measured from static snapshots. We develop finite-lag operator geometry for recurrent hidden states from observed source-successor pairs $(X_t,X_{t+Δ})$. The primitive is the conditional transport law $Q_Δ(dy\mid x)$, estimated by a dense Gaussian source-smoothing operator. From this directed finite-lag law we derive a source-centered transport tensor $G_Δ$, which decomposes exactly into conditional spread and coherent displacement, and an antisymmetric coordinate circulation $W_Δ^ρ$, which summarizes directed lagged flow. We prove affine covariance with explicit metric dependence of scalar summaries, dense estimator stability on bounded trajectory clouds, and a finite-lag separation result showing that source-centered transport detects deterministic recurrent motion not recorded by infinitesimal carre-du-champ geometry. A linear-Gaussian closed form calibrates the quantities in terms of the update $A_Δ$, source covariance, and innovation covariance. Controlled experiments validate the decomposition, circulation, covariance, and stability predictions. In performance matched repeat-copy networks, the framework reveals architecture dependent differences in total transport scale and coherent displacement trace, while coherent displacement fraction is metric and resolution dependent.

</details>


### [178] [Efficient Temporal Point Processes via Monotone Alternating Splines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01752)
*Cheng Wan,Quyu Kong,Feng Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出单调交替样条（MAS）框架，通过插值与外推分离的设计，解决了单调神经网络（MNN）在建模累积条件强度函数（CCIF）时存在的凸性限制、饱和极限和建模需求违背三大结构性缺陷，在合成与真实数据集上均取得更优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CCIF参数化方法统一依赖单调神经网络（MNN），但MNN存在凸性约束、饱和极限以及违反CCIF建模需求等结构性缺陷，严重限制了其对复杂时序动态的表达能力。

Method: 提出单调交替样条（MAS）框架，将插值（interpolation）与外推（extrapolation）组件分离设计，以灵活高效的方式建模CCIF。

Result: 理论分析表明MAS的插值组件提供强拟合精度，外推组件支持鲁棒泛化，减少了MNN的不可约逼近误差；大量实验在合成与真实数据集上均取得优越性能。

Conclusion: MAS有效克服了现有MNN方法的三个结构性瓶颈，为时序点过程（TPP）中的CCIF建模提供了一种更灵活、高效且表达能力更强的方案。

Abstract: Temporal point processes (TPPs) have widespread applications across various domains. Compared to modeling the conditional intensity of a TPP, modeling its cumulative conditional intensity function (CCIF) improves computational efficiency and eliminates numerical approximation errors. However, current CCIF parameterizations uniformly rely on Monotone Neural Networks (MNNs), which we identify as suffering from three structural deadlocks--convexity restrictions, saturation limits, and violations of CCIF modeling requirements--that fundamentally restrict their representational capacity for complex temporal dynamics. To resolve these bottlenecks, this paper proposes a novel framework called Monotone Alternating Splines (MAS). By leveraging distinct interpolation and extrapolation components, MAS provides a flexible and efficient framework for modeling CCIFs. Theoretically, MAS's interpolation provides strong fitting accuracy, while its extrapolation supports robust generalization, reducing the irreducible approximation gaps of MNNs. Extensive experiments show that MAS achieves superior performance on both synthetic and real-world datasets.

</details>


### [179] [Role-Aware Neural Convex Divergence Heads for Asymmetric Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01762)
*He Huang,Lu Shen,Yunfeng Huang,Li Qi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种角色感知的神经凸散度头（role-aware neural convex divergence head），用于非对称表示学习。该方法通过源/目标角色投影和输入凸神经Bregman散度，在角色投影空间中生成非负结构化得分。实验表明，该方法在词汇、句子、本体等有向关系基准上优于非对称基线，但在大规模固定特征引用预测中仍弱于对称或双曲基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表示学习中的有向关系（如词汇蕴含、句子蕴含、本体层级、引用链接）需要非对称建模，但标准欧氏、余弦和马氏距离头是对称的，而通用神经评分器虽能建模方向性但缺乏几何结构。本文旨在提出一种兼具方向建模能力和几何可解释性的结构化距离模块。

Method: 方法包括：(1) 源角色投影和目标角色投影，将输入映射到角色特定空间；(2) 输入凸神经Bregman散度（ICNN-Bregman），在角色投影空间中计算非负结构化得分。理论上刻画了投影空间恒等性、源角色凸性、方向间隙分解和基于Hessian的局部曲率。

Result: 在词汇、句子、本体和有向图基准上的实验表明，角色感知投影在主要语义和本体基准上一致提升了纯ICNN-Bregman头的方向准确率，并保持零负散度率。但在大规模固定特征引用预测中，专用对称或双曲基线在排序准确率上更强。

Conclusion: 该头可被理解为一种结构化的、可解释的即插即用距离模块，适用于有向关系重要的任务，但在某些边界场景（如大规模固定特征引用预测）中仍存在局限。

Abstract: Many representation learning problems involve directed relations, such as lexical entailment, sentence entailment, ontology hierarchy, and citation links. Standard Euclidean, cosine, and Mahalanobis heads are symmetric, while generic neural scorers can model directionality but provide limited geometric structure. This paper proposes a role-aware neural convex divergence head for asymmetric representation learning. The head applies source- and target-role projections before evaluating an input-convex neural Bregman divergence, yielding a nonnegative structured score in the role-projected space. We characterize its projected-space identity, source-role convexity, directional-gap decomposition, and Hessian-based local curvature. Experiments on lexical, sentence, ontology, and directed graph benchmarks compare symmetric distances, unstructured asymmetric scorers, order/hyperbolic baselines, plain ICNN-Bregman heads, and the proposed role-aware variant. Across ten random seeds on the main semantic and ontology benchmarks, role-aware projections consistently improve directional accuracy over plain ICNN-Bregman heads while preserving zero observed negative divergence rate. The results also identify a boundary case: on large fixed-feature citation prediction, specialized symmetric or hyperbolic baselines remain stronger in ranking accuracy. Overall, the proposed head is best understood as a structured and interpretable plug-in distance module for tasks where directional relations matter.

</details>


### [180] [Denser $\neq$ Better: Limits of On-Policy Self-Distillation for Continual Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01763)
*Meng Wang,Haohan Zhao,Wenzhuo Liu,Lu Yang,Geng Liu,Haiyang Guo,Guo-Sen Xie,Gaofeng Meng,Hongbin Liu,Fei Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文通过自蒸馏策略优化（SDPO）实验揭示：在教师信号稳定且对齐良好的情况下，SDPO能加速领域内特化，但在持续后训练中会表现出更强的遗忘甚至崩溃，而GRPO等在线强化学习方法能更保守地适应并保持先前能力。研究发现密集自蒸馏会导致参数空间和响应空间的更大漂移，并通过自我强化的师生循环放大高频格式化伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新审视“在线策略学习可以缓解遗忘”这一乐观观点，特别是针对在线策略自蒸馏方法在持续后训练中的真实表现进行系统性评估。

Method: 采用自蒸馏策略优化（SDPO）进行实验，对比在线策略强化学习方法（如GRPO），分析教师信号稳定性、分布偏移场景下的表现，并从参数空间和响应空间漂移角度进行机制分析。

Result: SDPO在教师信号稳定时能加速领域内特化，但在分布外场景泛化能力差；在持续后训练中表现出更强遗忘甚至崩溃；密集自蒸馏导致参数/响应空间漂移增大，并通过自我强化循环放大格式化伪影。

Conclusion: 在线策略数据本身不足以实现持续学习。密集自蒸馏在教师目标稳定且token级监督可靠时是有效的特化加速器，但不应作为持续后训练的默认稳定策略。

Abstract: Continual post-training enables foundation models to acquire new knowledge while preserving existing capabilities. Recent work suggests that on-policy learning can mitigate forgetting, with on-policy self-distillation emerging as a particularly attractive approach. In this work, we revisit this optimistic view through self-distillation policy optimization (SDPO). Our experiments show that SDPO can accelerate in-domain specialization when teacher signals are stable and well aligned, but it struggles to generalize to out-of-distribution scenarios. In continual post-training, SDPO exhibits stronger forgetting and can even collapse, whereas on-policy reinforcement learning methods such as GRPO adapt more conservatively and better preserve prior capabilities. Further analyses reveal that denser self-distillation induces larger drift in both parameter space and response space, and can amplify high-frequency formatting artifacts through a self-reinforcing teacher--student loop. These findings suggest that on-policy data alone is insufficient for continual learning. Dense self-distillation can accelerate specialization when teacher targets are stable and token-level supervision is reliable, but it should not be treated as a default stabilizer for continual post-training. Our code is available at https://github.com/Moenupa/SDPO-CL.

</details>


### [181] [Set Diffusion: Interpolating Token Orderings Between Autoregression and Diffusion for Fast and Flexible Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01775)
*Marianne Arriola,Volodymyr Kuleshov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Set Diffusion，一种通过灵活位置和长度的token集合分解似然函数的新型扩散语言模型，结合集合因果扩散架构支持KV缓存，在数学推理、摘要生成和无条件生成中实现更优的速度-质量权衡，并在填充任务上超越块扩散模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散扩散模型局限于固定长度生成且不支持KV缓存；块扩散虽部分桥接了扩散与自回归模型，但其固定大小的顺序块限制了解码灵活性和并行性。本文旨在解决这些局限性。

Method: 提出Set Diffusion：(i) 一种基于灵活位置和灵活长度token集合分解的似然参数化方法；(ii) 一种集合因果扩散架构，支持每一步推理后更新KV缓存。通过将分解对象从固定大小的块改为token集合，token可以以任意顺序的集合（包括滑动窗口集合）进行解码。

Result: 在数学推理、摘要生成和无条件生成任务上，Set Diffusion比先前的扩散语言模型取得了更好的速度-质量权衡，同时填充性能优于块扩散模型。

Conclusion: Set Diffusion通过集合级别的灵活解码和KV缓存支持，有效提升了扩散语言模型的推理效率和生成质量，为扩散模型在语言生成领域的应用提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models have steadily improved in quality relative to autoregressive (AR) models. However, these models are normally constrained to fixed-length generation and do not support key-value (KV) caching. Block diffusion partially bridges diffusion and AR by generating token blocks left-to-right, but its fixed-size sequential blocks limit decoding flexibility and parallelism. Here, we present a new class of language models, set diffusion, comprised of (i) a likelihood parameterization that factorizes over flexible-position, flexible-length token sets and (ii) a set-causal diffusion architecture that supports KV cache updates after every inference step. By factorizing over token sets instead of fixed-size blocks, tokens can be decoded in arbitrarily-ordered sets, including sliding-window sets, enabling faster inference and support for any-order decoding. Set diffusion achieves better speed-quality tradeoffs on mathematical reasoning, summarization, and unconditional generation compared to prior diffusion language models while offering stronger infilling performance than block diffusion. We provide the code, along with the model weights and blog post on the project page: https://m-arriola.com/setdlms/

</details>


### [182] [EHHN: An Event-driven Heterogeneous Hypergraph Network for Object-Centric Next Activity Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01785)
*Jiaxing Wang,Kaitao Chen,Zhubin Han,Chenyu Hou,Bin Cao,Jing Fan,Ji Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EHHN，一种事件驱动异质超图网络，用于面向对象中心的下一个活动预测。EHHN将预测前缀表示为异质超图，通过双流架构（微观空间流+宏观演化流）融合对象状态变化、时间动态和全局模式。在四个公开OCEL基准上，EHHN在所有数据集上取得最优准确率和宏F1分数，相比最强基线分别提升最高8.1和12.4个百分点，GPU内存峰值降低最高24倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有下一个活动预测方法主要面向经典单案例事件日志，而现实服务流程中事件常由多个类型业务对象共享。基于对象中心事件日志（OCEL）的预测方法存在局限：扁平化方法丢失跨对象上下文，原生OCEL图方法通过成对关系编码多对象事件。现有模型无法联合捕获事件驱动的对象状态变化、事件间时序和全局执行模式。

Method: 提出EHHN（事件驱动异质超图网络）。首先将每个预测前缀表示为异质超图，其中事件-对象超边绑定共同参与的对象，生命周期超边聚合主对象的已观测生命周期事件。采用双流架构：微观空间流建模事件驱动的对象状态演化，宏观演化流利用检索的全局原型捕获时间动态。两流融合后预测下一个活动。

Result: 在四个公开OCEL基准上对比9个基线方法，EHHN在所有数据集上取得最优准确率和宏F1分数，相比最强基线提升最高8.1（准确率）和12.4（宏F1）个百分点。与最强OCEL原生图基线相比，GPU峰值内存降低最高24倍。

Conclusion: EHHN通过异质超图表示和双流架构，有效解决了对象中心流程中跨对象上下文建模、事件驱动状态变化与全局时序模式联合捕获的难题，在预测性能和计算效率上均显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Next activity prediction helps service-oriented processes anticipate upcoming steps before delays, exceptions, or service-level risks occur. Most existing methods assume classical single-case event logs, whereas real service processes often involve events shared by multiple typed business objects. Object-centric event logs (OCELs) capture such interactions, but current predictors remain limited. Flattening-based approaches lose cross-object context, and native OCEL graph-based approaches encode multi-object events through pairwise relations. Existing models also do not jointly capture event-driven object state changes, inter-event timing, and global execution patterns. We propose EHHN, an Event-driven Heterogeneous Hypergraph Network for object-centric next activity prediction. EHHN represents each prediction prefix as a heterogeneous hypergraph, where event--object hyperedges bind retained co-participating objects and a lifecycle hyperedge groups the primary object's observed lifecycle events. Based on this representation, EHHN uses a dual-stream architecture in which a micro-spatial stream models event-driven object-state evolution and a macro-evolution stream captures temporal dynamics using retrieved global prototypes. The two streams are fused to predict the next activity. Experiments on four public OCEL benchmarks against nine baselines show that EHHN achieves the best accuracy and macro F1-score on all datasets, with improvements of up to 8.1 and 12.4 percentage points over the strongest baselines. Compared with the strongest OCEL-native graph baseline, EHHN also reduces peak GPU memory by up to 24 times. Code is available at https://github.com/chenkaitao1112/EHHN.

</details>


### [183] [EPnG: Adaptive Expert Prune-and-Grow for Parameter-Efficient MoE Fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01789)
*Ahin Lee,Sehyun Yun,Taesik Gong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EPnG框架，基于路由器门控概率评估专家重要性，动态剪枝低效专家并扩展高重要性专家的LoRA秩，在固定参数预算下实现比LoRA更优的性能，仅更新0.55%-0.72%参数即达到全参数微调效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE模型存在冗余专家和均匀参数分配问题，而LoRA等PEFT方法忽略了MoE路由动态，导致资源利用次优。

Method: EPnG框架：根据路由器门控概率计算专家重要性，剪枝低重要性专家，通过正交初始化扩展高重要性专家的LoRA秩，维持总参数预算不变。

Result: 在OLMoE和Qwen1.5-MoE上，EPnG在相同预算下一致优于LoRA，仅更新0.55%-0.72%参数（减少140x-180x）即可与全微调性能相当。

Conclusion: 将PEFT与MoE路由对齐能产生更有效且可扩展的微调策略。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models scale efficiently but remain costly to adapt due to redundant experts and uniform parameter allocation. Existing parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods such as LoRA ignore MoE routing dynamics, leading to suboptimal resource use. We propose EPnG, an adaptive prune-and-grow framework that reallocates LoRA capacity based on expert importance derived from router gate probabilities. EPnG prunes under-utilized experts and expands high-importance experts via rank growth with orthogonal initialization, while maintaining a fixed parameter budget. Across OLMoE and Qwen1.5-MoE, EPnG consistently outperforms LoRA under the same budget and achieves performance comparable to full fine-tuning while updating only 0.55%-0.72% of parameters (up to 140x-180x fewer). These results demonstrate that aligning PEFT with MoE routing yields a more effective and scalable fine-tuning strategy.

</details>


### [184] [Single-Channel EEG-Based Cognitive Load Assessment in Online Learning: A Hybrid Deep Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01795)
*Rowan Hussein,Mohamed Ouf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了使用单通道消费级EEG设备（NeuroSky MindWave Mobile 2）区分在线学习视频的难易程度。采用混合CNN+LSTM+Attention模型，在9名受试者的被试内设置中达到78.5%的准确率，但被试间评估更具挑战性。研究强调被试独立评估应成为标准，并发布了可复现的评估流程和可视化工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线学习中监控认知负荷有助于教师识别学生困难内容，但远程环境缺少课堂上的视觉线索。需要一种能自动检测认知负荷的方法来替代缺失的视觉反馈。

Method: 使用NeuroSky MindWave Mobile 2单通道EEG设备采集数据，采用混合CNN+LSTM+Attention模型，结合原始波形和频带功率特征。通过dropout和L2正则化缓解过拟合，并进行被试内和被试独立两种评估。

Result: 被试内设置下混合模型准确率达78.5%，远超传统特征分类器的55%；正则化后验证准确率稳定在68-73%。但仅9名受试者，被试内评估结果存在乐观偏差。

Conclusion: 该工作作为可行性研究，表明消费级EEG有区分认知负荷的潜力，但被试独立评估应成为标准。研究还发布了可复现的评估流程和面向教育者的可视化工具。

Abstract: Monitoring cognitive load during online learning could help instructors identify content that learners find difficult, but remote settings remove the visual cues that support this judgement in a classroom. We study whether a single-channel, consumer-grade EEG device (the NeuroSky MindWave Mobile 2) can distinguish easy from difficult educational-video content, using the publicly available dataset of Wang et al. [24] (ten learners, one excluded for excessive noise, leaving nine). We implement a hybrid CNN+LSTM+Attention model that combines the raw waveform with band-power features. In a within-subject setting, the model reaches up to 78.5% accuracy, compared with 55% for conventional feature-based classifiers; regularization (dropout and L2) closes the large gap between training and validation accuracy that we observe without it, keeping validation accuracy stable at roughly 68-73%. We are deliberately cautious about these numbers: with only nine subjects, within-subject evaluation is optimistic, and we argue that subject-independent evaluation -- in which no learner appears in both training and test data -- should be the standard for this task. To that end we release a reproducible evaluation pipeline. We frame the work as a feasibility study rather than a deployable system, and pair it with an open, notebook-based tool that records EEG, runs inference, and visualizes estimated cognitive load as a heatmap over the video timeline to help educators locate potentially challenging segments.

</details>


### [185] [Expander Sparse Autoencoders: Parameter-Efficient Dictionaries for Mechanistic Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01799)
*Rodrigo Mendoza-Smith*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Expander SAE，通过将自编码器的解码器权重限制在左d-正则扩展器掩码上（仅学习dn个值而非O(mn)），在大幅减少存储和计算的同时，在语言模型激活分解中保持了与密集解码器相当的性能，并在理论上证明了扩展性和列平坦性对稀疏码可识别性的充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准稀疏自编码器（SAE）的解码器为密集矩阵，需要学习O(mn)个参数，当特征数量n增大时存储和计算成本高昂，限制了SAE在大规模语言模型可解释性中的可扩展性。

Method: 提出Expander SAE：将TopK SAE的解码器和绑定编码器限制在左d-正则扩展器二值掩码上，仅学习dn个非零值；同时将OMP中的匹配追踪相关步骤W^T r优化为O(dn)的gather-and-reduce操作。

Result: 在Pythia-70M/160M、Qwen2.5-3B、Llama-3.2-1B的残差流激活上，不同d值形成一致的存储- fidelity前沿；Qwen2.5-3B中d=7时，仅用密集解码器1/293的学习参数即恢复84%的密集交叉熵损失。控制实验表明稀疏、多样化的解码器支撑结构而非更少的参数是改进的关键。

Conclusion: Expander SAE在大幅降低SAE存储和计算开销的同时保持了良好性能，为SAE在大规模语言模型中的可扩展应用提供了有效方案；理论证明了扩展性和列平坦性对无噪声k-稀疏码可识别性的充分条件，以及OMP精确恢复支撑的互补充分条件。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) decompose internal activations of neural networks into sparse linear combinations of learned features by fitting an overcomplete dictionary $\mathbf{W}\in\mathbb{R}^{m\times n}$ with $m<n$, and inferring a sparse code $\mathbf{x}\in\mathbb{R}^n$ from $\mathbf{h}\approx\mathbf{W}\mathbf{x}$. This inference problem closely resembles the canonical setup of compressed sensing, but dense decoders requires $O(mn)$ learned values, which becomes costly at large feature counts. We introduce Expander SAEs: TopK SAEs whose decoder and tied encoder are supported on a left-$d$-regular expander mask with $d\ll m$, learning only $dn$ decoder values while keeping the sparse-coding problem $(m,n,k)$ fixed. The same structure reduces storage and turns the matching-pursuit correlation step $\mathbf{W}^\top \mathbf{r}$ in OMP into an $O(dn)$ gather-and-reduce operation. Our experiments show that across Pythia-70M/160M, Qwen2.5-3B, and Llama-3.2-1B residual-stream activations, varying $d$ traces a consistent storage--fidelity frontier, and that at the most compressed modern-LM setting, Qwen2.5-3B with $d=7$ uses $293\times$ fewer learned decoder values than the full dense decoder while retaining $84$% of dense CE-loss recovered. Control experiments show that the improved storage--fidelity tradeoff is driven by sparse, diverse decoder support structure rather than by fewer learned decoder values, and that when sparse and dense decoders are compared at matched parameter count, part of the remaining gap comes from encoder amortisation. On the theoretical side, we show that expansion and column flatness are sufficient for identifiability of noiseless $k$-sparse codes, and we derive complementary sufficient conditions under which OMP recovers the support exactly.

</details>


### [186] [Do LLMs Truly Generalize in the Molecular Domain? A Perturbation-Based Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01800)
*Jiatong Li,Weida Wang,Changmeng Zheng,Shufei Zhang,Yatao Bian,Xiao-yong Wei,Qing Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出分子扰动框架研究分子LLM对结构变化的泛化能力，发现即使单次编辑也会导致性能大幅下降，而基于结构相似分子的上下文调优可部分缓解此问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子LLM在离散序列概率建模与化学空间刚性拓扑约束之间存在差距，现有研究缺乏对分子LLM能否超越序列表示诱导的局部邻域进行泛化的系统理解。

Method: 提出分子扰动框架，通过受控图编辑距离生成句法有效的结构变体，系统探测分子LLM的流形正则性；引入上下文调优（ICT）利用结构相似分子锚定预测来缓解结构敏感性。

Result: 单次结构编辑即可导致常见分子任务性能显著下降，揭示狭窄局部信任区域和脆弱的结构敏感性；ICT可部分扩展局部信任区域，增强模型对结构扰动的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 分子LLM对结构变化高度敏感，局部信任区域极其狭窄；基于结构相似性的上下文调优是稳定分子LLM应对结构变异的有前景方向。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently shown promise in molecular discovery, yet a gap remains between their probabilistic nature over discrete sequential tokens and the rigid topological constraints of chemical space. This raises the question of whether molecular LLMs can generalize beyond the local neighborhoods induced by their sequence-based representations. To systematically investigate this question, we introduce a Molecular Perturbation framework that generates syntax-valid structural variants of training molecules under controlled Graph Edit Distance (GED) to probe the manifold regularity of molecular LLMs. Our analysis shows that even a single edit can cause substantial performance drops on common molecular tasks, revealing a narrow local trust region and fragile sensitivity to structural changes. Since similar molecules tend to exhibit similar properties, In-Context Tuning (ICT), which anchors predictions on structurally similar molecules, offers a natural way to mitigate such fragility. Our experiments also examine whether ICT confers robustness under controlled structural perturbations, and the results suggest that it can partially expand the local trust region and offer a promising direction for stabilizing molecular LLMs against structural variation.

</details>


### [187] [Gaming Consensus: Coordinated Manipulation in Crowdsourced Fact-Checking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01824)
*Nikil Roashan Selvam,Jay Baxter,Sophie Hilgard,Brad Miller,Keith Coleman,Ellen Vitercik,Sanmi Koyejo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于矩阵分解的众包事实核查系统（如X的Community Notes）中的桥接机制，发现通过策略性投票（包括反向评级）可在少于10次评分内操纵10.7%的低质量笔记达到共识阈值，并提出了缓解措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 众包事实核查系统（如X、Meta、TikTok、Google采用的）依赖桥接机制来识别跨视角获得支持的笔记，但该机制可能被恶意用户利用潜在表征进行策略性投票操纵。

Method: 使用历史生产数据实证评估低质量笔记被操纵至共识阈值以上的比例；进行理论分析揭示反向评级（"Not Helpful"）反而可能提高帮助度分数的反直觉现象；建立量化操纵努力的成本模型。

Result: 发现最多10.7%的低质量笔记可通过不到10次评分被操纵至共识阈值以上；反向评级可能提高帮助度分数；已在X的Community Notes算法中部署缓解合成共识的防御措施。

Conclusion: 矩阵分解桥接机制存在可被利用的战略性投票漏洞，但通过针对性缓解措施可防御合成共识攻击。

Abstract: Crowdsourced fact-checking systems have been adopted by major social media companies such as X, Meta, TikTok and Google with the aim of combating misleading information at scale without relying on centralized editorial control. These systems have been developed around a common underlying concept: a bridging mechanism that identifies notes flagging misleading information when they receive support from people with different perspectives rather than simple majority support. To our knowledge the only publicly disclosed bridging algorithms deployed for fact-checking are based on matrix factorization, as deployed by both X and Meta, augmented with additional components addressing abuse, targeted manipulation, and contributor brigades. This work examines the core matrix factorization portion of these systems, presenting theoretical and empirical evaluations of the degree to which coordinated users could vote strategically by leveraging the latent representations to fabricate the appearance of synthetic consensus within the bridging mechanism. Using historic production data, we find that up to 10.7% of lower quality notes could be manipulated above consensus thresholds using less than 10 ratings. We complement these findings with a theoretical analysis, revealing counterintuitively that rating a note as "Not Helpful" can increase its helpfulness score, as well as a cost model quantifying manipulation effort. We have developed and deployed mitigations within X's Community Notes algorithm to address synthetic consensus.

</details>


### [188] [Many Voices, One Reward: Multi-Role Rubric Generation for LLM Judging and Reward Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01830)
*Dazhi Fu,Jiuding Yang,Yiwen Guo,Jicong Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出多角色评分准则生成框架(MRRG)，通过多个互补角色生成评估标准并整合为可审计的评分器，用于偏好验证和强化学习奖励信号，实验表明优于单角色基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于评分准则的判定器通常依赖单一通用评估器，可能忽略人类偏好的重要维度（即维度盲区），需要更全面的评估框架。

Method: 提出MRRG框架，无需训练和参考，从多个互补角色角度生成评估准则，整合成可审计的基于评分准则的评分器，用于偏好验证和GRPO风格强化学习。

Result: 在偏好验证基准上，MRRG在多个骨干模型上持续优于单角色评分准则生成基线；在RLVR实验中，MRRG为改进开放式生成提供了更强的奖励信号。

Conclusion: MRRG通过多角色互补生成评估准则，有效克服了单一评估器的维度盲区问题，在偏好验证和强化学习奖励信号方面均取得更优效果。

Abstract: Reliable reward and preference signals are critical for evaluating and optimizing large language models on open-ended tasks. Rubric-based judges offer a transparent way to decompose such judgments into explicit evaluation criteria, but existing annotation-free rubric generators typically rely on a single generic evaluator. As a result, they may overlook important dimensions of human preference, a failure mode we term dimensional blind spots. To address this limitation, we propose Multi-Role Rubric Generation (MRRG), a training-free and reference-free framework that elicits evaluation criteria from multiple complementary roles and consolidates them into an auditable rubric-based scorer. This scorer can be used both to validate pairwise preferences and to provide rewards for GRPO-style Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR). Experiments on preference validation benchmarks show that MRRG consistently outperforms single-role rubric generation baselines across multiple backbone models. Further RLVR experiments demonstrate that MRRG yields a stronger reward signal for improving open-ended generation.

</details>


### [189] [Adaptive Group-Based Counterfactual Explanations for Time-Series Rehabilitation Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01838)
*Emmanuel C. Chukwu,Rianne M. Schouten,Monique Tabak,Mykola Pechenizkiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多传感器IMU康复运动分析中现有反事实解释方法在通道级别操作导致解释零散且生物力学不连贯的问题，本文提出两阶段框架：先证实Shapley-Adaptive组排序保持反事实有效性但缺乏组级稀疏性，再引入可学习门控（LG）方法，通过可训练的每组相关性门控与扰动掩码联合优化，在KneE-PAD数据集上显著提升模态组稀疏性，同时保持或改善有效性、时间平滑性和生成效率，产生符合临床推理的肌肉级纠正指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列分类器的反事实解释通常在单个通道级别生成，而康复医学专家以语义特征组（如肌群、关节段）进行推理，导致通道级解释零散、生物力学不连贯，难以提供临床可操作的指导。

Method: 提出两阶段框架：第一阶段使用Shapley-Adaptive组排序保持反事实有效性；第二阶段引入可学习门控（LG）方法，为每组分配可训练的 relevance 门控，与扰动掩码联合优化，实现显式的组级稀疏性选择。

Result: 在KneE-PAD康复数据集上，LG相比通道级M-CELS基线显著提升了模态组稀疏性，同时维持或改善了反事实有效性、时间平滑性和生成效率；运动特定分析表明组结构化反事实产生符合临床推理的简洁肌肉级纠正指导。

Conclusion: 该框架在不牺牲反事实质量的前提下增强了可解释性，为康复运动分析提供了更具可操作性的组级反事实解释。

Abstract: Counterfactual explanations (CEs) for multivariate time-series classifiers are often difficult to interpret in domains where experts reason in terms of semantic feature groups rather than individual channels. In rehabilitation movement analysis with multi-sensor inertial measurement units (IMUs), clinicians interpret motion through muscle-group and joint-segment abstractions; yet, most existing counterfactual methods operate at the channel level, producing scattered and biomechanically incoherent explanations. We propose a two-stage framework for group-based counterfactual generation in high-dimensional IMU data. We first show that Shapley-Adaptive (SA) group ranking preserves counterfactual validity but fails to enforce group-level sparsity, motivating the need for explicit group selection. We then introduce Learnable Gate (LG) methods, which incorporate trainable per-group relevance gates jointly optimized with perturbation masks. Experiments on the KneE-PAD rehabilitation dataset demonstrate that LG substantially improves modality-group sparsity compared to the channel-level M-CELS baseline while maintaining or improving validity, temporal smoothness, and generation efficiency. Exercise-specific analyses further show that group-structured counterfactuals yield concise, muscle-level corrective guidance aligned with clinical reasoning. Overall, the proposed framework enhances interpretability without sacrificing counterfactual quality, enabling more actionable explanations for rehabilitation movement analysis.

</details>


### [190] [Decomposer: Learning to Decompile Symbolic Music to Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01849)
*Yewon Kim,Apurva Gandhi,David Chung,Graham Neubig,Chris Donahue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Decomposer提出了一种符号音乐反编译的后训练框架，通过两阶段方法（合成数据监督微调+强化学习）将MIDI音乐转换为可执行、可编辑的Strudel程序，在保证重构保真度的同时提升代码可读性和多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音乐演奏包含了高层级的音乐指令，但从中恢复这些指令是一个具有挑战性的逆问题。现有方法缺乏将符号音乐（MIDI）反编译为可编辑音乐程序的有效手段。

Method: 两阶段后训练框架：(1) 构建Strudel-Synth合成语料库进行监督微调；(2) 使用强化学习在无配对MIDI数据上优化MIDI重构保真度和代码可读性奖励。

Result: 在合成和真实MIDI基准测试中，Decomposer在MIDI重构保真度上显著优于闭源大语言模型，同时生成比启发式转换器更可读且更多样化的代码。

Conclusion: Decomposer有效解决了符号音乐反编译中低资源语言数据匮乏和过度优化保真度导致代码不可读的双重挑战，为音乐程序合成提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Musical performance involves executing a set of high-level musical instructions, yet recovering those instructions from the performance is a challenging inverse problem. We present Decomposer, a post-training framework for symbolic music decompilation: the task of recovering executable, editable music programs from symbolic music. We instantiate the task as MIDI-to-Strudel decompilation, where the model takes symbolic MIDI as input and produces a program in Strudel, a music programming language, that reconstructs the input when executed. The task poses two challenges: Strudel is a low-resource language with little naturally paired MIDI-code data, and optimizing faithful reconstruction of MIDI alone can collapse to unreadable note-by-note transliteration. We address these challenges in two stages. First, we construct Strudel-Synth, a synthetic corpus of paired Strudel programs and rendered MIDI, and use it for supervised fine-tuning. Second, we refine the model with reinforcement learning on unpaired MIDI, optimizing rewards for both MIDI reconstruction faithfulness and code readability. Our evaluation across synthetic and real-world MIDI benchmarks shows that Decomposer achieves substantially higher MIDI reconstruction faithfulness than closed-source LLMs while producing more readable and diverse code than the heuristic converter.

</details>


### [191] [Learning the Supports for Categorical Critic in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01880)
*Jen-Yen Chang,Takayuki Osa,Tatsuya Harada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种动态学习支撑区间边界的直方图损失方法（HL-Gauss变体），无需预定义固定支撑区间，解决了分布强化学习中值函数估计的分类建模问题，并在理论上证明了其比固定支撑方法更紧的MSE上界，实验在连续控制任务上匹配或优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统直方图损失（HL-Gauss）需要预定义固定的支撑区间，但强化学习中目标值具有非平稳性和随机性，导致固定区间难以适配动态变化的回报分布，限制了其实际应用效果。

Method: 提出动态学习支撑区间上下界的联合优化目标，该目标同时学习分类表示和支撑边界；从理论上证明该目标构成均方贝尔曼误差的上界，且比非学习支撑（HL-Gauss）的上界更紧。

Result: 在连续控制任务中，该方法无需预指定支撑区间即可匹配基于HL-Gauss的Actor-Critic算法性能，并在部分任务上取得改进。

Conclusion: 动态学习支撑区间是一种有效解决直方图损失中固定支撑局限性的方案，在保持性能的同时提升了实用性和适应性。

Abstract: Value functions are an essential component in actor-critic based deep reinforcement learning (RL). Conventionally, these functions are trained as a regression task by minimising the mean squared error (MSE) relative to bootstrapped target values. Meanwhile, in distributional RL, a distribution of returns is modelled based on the distributional Bellman operator. This work investigates the Gaussian Histogram Loss (HL-Gauss), a recent approach that reframes value estimation as classification by encoding each scalar Bellman target as a Gaussian-smoothed categorical target. Despite its potential, applying histogram-based losses to RL presents inherent challenges, most notably the requirement to pre-define a fixed support interval, which is often complicated by the non-stationary and stochastic nature of target values typically found in RL tasks. In this work, we propose an approach that dynamically learns the lower and upper bounds of the support instead of assigning them beforehand. We derive an objective that jointly learns these bounds whilst learning the categorical representation of the scalar values, and we show that this objective forms an upper bound on the mean-squared Bellman error. Our theoretical analysis further shows that this bound is tighter than that of non-learned supports of HL-Gauss. Empirically, the proposed objective enables stable adaptation of the support interval and matches HL-Gauss-based actor-critic algorithms on most continuous-control tasks whilst improving on a subset, without requiring a pre-specified support interval.

</details>


### [192] [Regularized Variational and Spectral Log-Density-Ratio Estimation in the Gaussian Location Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01895)
*Francis Bach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在高斯位置模型中研究岭正则化对数密度比估计，推导了变分估计和谱估计在高维渐近下的确定性等价，并通过实验比较了两种估计器的风险表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推导岭正则化对数密度比估计在高维渐近框架下的确定性等价，并系统比较变分估计与谱估计的种群风险，以确定在不同观测数量下的最优估计策略。

Method: 利用仿射不变性将模型标准化为q~N(0,I)、p~N(Δ,I)；变分估计采用经验KL对数归一化拟合加L2惩罚，谱估计采用连续岭正则化最小二乘问题；通过凸-高斯-最小-最大定理（CGMT）推导变分极限，通过两个独立高斯样本协方差矩阵预解式的确定性等价推导谱极限。

Result: 观测较多时，良好设定的变分估计风险更小；观测较少时，谱估计因协方差基构造方差更低而更优；核惩罚可用于特征学习但仅能部分分析。

Conclusion: 在高斯位置模型下，变分估计与谱估计各有适用场景，选择取决于观测数量与维度之比；核惩罚为特征学习提供可能但仍需进一步分析。

Abstract: We study ridge-regularized log-density-ratio estimation in the Gaussian location model with a common covariance matrix. By affine invariance, the model is written as q $\sim$ N(0, I), p $\sim$ N($Δ$, I), with linear features, where $Δ$ is a mean vector. The variational estimator is the empirical Kullback-Leibler (KL) log-normalized fit with a squared L2-penalty on its nonconstant coefficient, and the spectral estimator recently introduced in [1] replaces a single variational problem by a continuum of ridge-regularized least-squares problems. We derive high-dimensional deterministic asymptotic equivalents when the numbers of observations and dimension tend to infinity with fixed ratios. The regularized variational limit is characterized by a scalar entropy minimization problem derived from the convex-Gaussian-min-max theorem (CGMT), while the regularized spectral limit follows from deterministic equivalents for resolvents of weighted sums of two independent Gaussian sample covariance matrices. We use these formulas to compare population risks, with experiments focused on fixed-signal aspect-ratio sweeps and optimized regularization. Our conclusion is that with many observations, under the criteria and asymptotic regimes analyzed here, the well-specified variational estimator has the smaller risk, while with fewer observations, the spectral estimator is favored because its covariance-based construction has lower variance. We also study how a nuclear penalty can be used and partially analyzed to perform feature learning.

</details>


### [193] [Rank-Then-Act: Reward-Free Control from Frame-Order Progress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01897)
*Yuriy Maksyuta,George Bredis,Ruslan Rakhimov,Daniil Gavrilov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Rank-Then-Act (RTA)框架，通过视频演示学习控制策略，无需环境奖励。利用VLM对视频帧进行进度排序，基于斯皮尔曼等级相关设计奖励函数，实现跨任务策略学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视频的奖励学习方法需要精确的标量奖励或绝对校准，难以跨任务迁移；缺乏一种不依赖环境奖励、仅从视频演示中提取有序监督信号进行策略学习的可扩展方案。

Method: 1) 使用GRPO目标对打乱的帧序列训练VLM作为进度排序器；2) 计算预测进度排名与真实时间索引之间的斯皮尔曼等级相关作为奖励信号；3) 将此有界、尺度不变的奖励用于强化学习策略训练。

Result: 在离散控制（PyBoy: Catrap, Kirby）和连续控制（PointMaze, MetaWorld）任务上，RTA一致达到或优于先前基于视频的奖励学习方法，且单个预训练进度排序器可跨任务复用。

Conclusion: 基于视频的排序监督信号足以用于策略学习，相关性结构化的奖励设计是显式奖励设计的可扩展替代方案。

Abstract: We introduce Rank-Then-Act (RTA), a framework for learning control policies from expert video demonstrations without environment rewards. RTA trains a Vision-Language Model (VLM) offline as a progress-based ordinal scorer, using a Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) objective over shuffled frame sequences, which forces the model to recover temporal ordering from visual semantics rather than trivial time cues. Importantly, instead of using the scorer directly as a scalar reward model, we propose a correlation-based reward function for reinforcement learning: at each interaction window, we compute the Spearman rank correlation between predicted progress rankings and true temporal indices, yielding a bounded, scale-invariant learning signal. This design decouples reward learning from absolute calibration and enables stable transfer across tasks and environments. We evaluate RTA on discrete control benchmarks (PyBoy: Catrap, Kirby) and continuous control tasks (PointMaze, MetaWorld). RTA consistently matches or outperforms prior video-based reward learning methods and rank-based baselines, while demonstrating strong cross-task reuse of a single pretrained progress scorer. Our results suggest that correlation-structured supervision over video-derived ordinal signals is sufficient for policy learning, offering a scalable alternative to explicit reward design.

</details>


### [194] [SABER: A Semantic-Aligned Brain Network Analysis Framework via Multi-scale Hypergraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01901)
*Yidan Xu,Xiangmin Han,Rundong Xue,Huihui Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出语义对齐脑网络框架，通过整合LLM语义（ROI级自注意力、多尺度超图、决策级语义对齐）提升脑疾病诊断性能，在ABIDE和ADHD-200上达到SOTA，尤其在少样本场景下表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将LLM语义仅作为辅助特征或监督信号，未能使其直接参与决策，限制了分类的稳定性和鲁棒性。

Method: 1) ROI级语义通过全局自注意力融入节点表示；2) 构建多尺度超图建模功能子网络和多ROI交互，捕获高阶依赖；3) 决策级语义对齐机制将患者特定文本嵌入注入图表示，使语义直接指导预测而不扰动网络结构。

Result: 在ABIDE和ADHD-200数据集上达到SOTA性能，具有更好的稳定性和可解释性，尤其在少样本场景下。

Conclusion: 将LLM语义主动融入预测过程而非作为辅助特征，能显著提升脑疾病诊断的准确性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Effective brain disease diagnosis requires the synergy of brain connectivity patterns and high-level semantic knowledge. Existing methods, however, largely treat semantics from large language models (LLMs) as auxiliary features or supervision, limiting their direct role in decision-making and constraining classification stability and robustness. To overcome this, we propose a semantic-aligned brain network framework that actively integrates LLM-derived semantics into the prediction process. Specifically, ROI-level semantics are first incorporated via global self-attention to enrich node representations and provide whole-brain context. Multi-scale hypergraphs are then constructed to explicitly model functional subnetworks and multi-ROI interactions, addressing the locality limitations of traditional GNNs and capturing high-order dependencies. Finally, a decision-level semantic alignment mechanism selectively injects patient-specific textual embeddings into graph representations, enabling semantics to directly guide predictions without perturbing the underlying network structure. Experiments on public brain network datasets ABIDE and ADHD-200 demonstrate state-of-the-art performance, enhanced stability, and improved interpretability, particularly in small-sample settings.

</details>


### [195] [Population-Based Multi-Objective Training of Discriminators for Semi-Supervised GANs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01907)
*Francisco Sedeño,Francisco Chicano,Jamal Toutouh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于种群进化的多目标优化训练策略，将半监督GAN中判别器的监督与无监督损失作为两个独立目标，通过帕累托支配排序维护判别器种群，在MNIST小标签场景下提升了训练稳定性和分类精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半监督GAN（SSL-GAN）虽能利用大量无标签数据，但训练过程常不稳定，原因在于将监督损失和无监督损失合并为单一标量损失难以平衡两者之间的冲突。

Method: 将判别器学习建模为多目标优化问题，维护一个基于帕累托支配排序的判别器种群，探索分类准确率与真实/虚假判别之间的不同权衡；分析了精英策略和单目标消融实验等变体。

Result: 在MNIST有限标签实验上，相比SSL-GAN和CE-SSL-GAN基线，该方法提升了训练鲁棒性，其中精英变体始终取得最高分类准确率。

Conclusion: 多目标进化训练策略能有效改善半监督GAN中判别器的两个角色——准确分类与真实/虚假判别，提升训练稳定性与性能。

Abstract: Semi-supervised generative adversarial networks (SSL-GANs) can exploit large unlabeled datasets while retaining a classifier in the discriminator, but their training is often unstable. This paper proposes a population-based evolutionary training strategy in which discriminator learning is formulated as a multi-objective optimization problem. Instead of aggregating the supervised and unsupervised components of the SSL objective into a single scalar loss, the method maintains a population of discriminators ranked by Pareto dominance, enabling the exploration of different trade-offs between classification accuracy and real/fake discrimination. This formulation aims to improve both roles of SSL-GANs: learning accurate classifiers and training generators capable of producing realistic samples. We analyze several variants, including an elitist strategy and a mono-objective ablation, to assess the role of multi-objective selection. Experiments on MNIST with limited labels show improved training robustness compared to SSL-GAN and CE-SSL-GAN state-of-the-art baselines, while the elitist variant consistently achieves the highest classification accuracy.

</details>


### [196] [Zeus: Towards Tuning-Free Foundation Model for Time Series Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01918)
*Yisong Fu,Zezhi Shao,Chengqing Yu,Yujie Li,Yongjun Xu,Xueqi Cheng,Fei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Zeus是一个统一的无调优时间序列基础模型，通过多尺度Transformer和多目标时间掩码策略，在零样本设置下实现预测、插值和全局抽象等多种任务的竞争性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列基础模型主要关注零样本预测，但其他任务仍需任务特定微调，缺乏真正的多任务泛化能力。Zeus旨在弥合这一差距，实现无需任何微调即可处理多种时间序列分析任务。

Method: 提出两个关键技术：1) 多尺度Transformer，采用逐点令牌化和U形层次结构，兼顾细粒度保真度和计算效率；2) 多目标时间掩码（MOTM），作为统一策略支持外推、插值和全局抽象等异构任务。

Result: 在五种代表性任务上的大量实验表明，Zeus在无调优设置下一致取得具有竞争力的结果。

Conclusion: Zeus展示了作为通用时间序列基础模型的潜力，无需任务特定微调即可在多种时间序列分析任务上取得优异性能。

Abstract: We present Zeus, a unified tuning-free Time Series Foundation Model (TSFM) that delivers superior performance across diverse analysis tasks without any task-specific fine-tuning. Unlike prior studies that primarily focus on zero-shot forecasting but require task-specific tuning for other tasks, Zeus bridges this gap by addressing two fundamental challenges in multi-task generalization. First, to reconcile point-level granularity with long-sequence scalability, Zeus incorporates a multi-scale Transformer featuring point-wise tokenization and a U-shaped hierarchy, effectively balancing fine-grained fidelity with computational efficiency. Second, to accommodate varying inductive biases across different tasks, Zeus introduces Multi-Objective Temporal Masking (MOTM), a unified strategy that supports heterogeneous tasks (e.g., extrapolation, interpolation, and global abstraction) within a single framework. Extensive experiments across five representative tasks demonstrate that Zeus consistently achieves competitive results in tuning-free settings, underscoring its potential as a general-purpose TSFM.

</details>


### [197] [Conditional Co-Ablation: Recovering Self-Repair Backups in Transformer Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01940)
*Zhiren Gong,Zihao Zeng,Chau Yuen,Wei Yang Bryan Lim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对Transformer自修复现象导致单单元消融评分失效的问题，提出条件共消融（CoAx）方法，通过测量主组件移除后剩余单元消融效应的增长来发现隐藏的备份组件，在IOI电路上将备份头恢复从0.33提升至0.91 ROC-AUC，并应用于归因校正、能力击穿和结构化剪枝，跨越1.24亿到70亿参数规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统可解释性依赖组件级干预（如消融）来评估每个单元的重要性，当组件影响可加时有效；但Transformer存在自修复现象：移除主组件后，原本不活跃的备份组件会接管功能，导致主组件的消融效应被低估，而备份组件在完整模型上看似无关。这种一阶评分失效需要新的二阶交互度量方法。

Method: 提出条件共消融（Conditional Co-Ablation, CoAx）：在移除一组主要组件后，测量每个剩余单元的消融效应增长程度，以此量化二阶交互。这是一个无标签、基于输出的评分方法，通过暴露主组件缺失时的备份组件贡献来发现自修复回路。

Result: 在GPT-2-small的IOI电路上，CoAx将备份头恢复从0.33提升至0.91 ROC-AUC，优于所有基线包括自修复感知的梯度评分（最佳0.82）；反事实补丁验证恢复的头确实因果承载修复。该方法无需标签即可迁移至8个模型的归纳头发现；恢复的备份可校正自修复掩盖的归因、识别能力击穿所需组件，并实现从1.24亿到70亿参数的修复感知结构化剪枝。

Conclusion: 组件重要性不是孤立的单元属性：在鲁棒电路中，真正重要的组件只有在使其成为必要的干预条件下才会显现。CoAx通过条件化消融暴露了传统一阶评分遗漏的二阶交互，为可解释性中的自修复问题提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability often relies on component-level interventions to discover how a model produces a behavior. This guides attribution, capability knockout, and model pruning downstream to operate by scoring each unit by the effect of ablation in isolation. Such first-order scoring is natural when component importance is additive, but becomes misleading when a transformer self-repairs: after a primary component is removed, a dormant backup can take over, muting the primary's measured effect while the backup itself appears irrelevant on the intact model. We recast this failure as a recovery task, conditional circuit completion, and introduce Conditional Co-Ablation (CoAx), a label-free, output-grounded score that asks how much each remaining unit's ablation effect grows once a primary set has been removed. This conditional growth exposes the second-order interaction that single-unit scores discard. On the GPT-2-small IOI circuit, CoAx raises backup-head recovery from 0.33 to 0.91 ROC-AUC, outperforming all baselines, including self-repair-aware gradient scores (best 0.82); counterfactual patching verifies that the recovered heads causally carry the repair. The same label-free procedure transfers to induction across eight models. Beyond discovery, the recovered backups correct self-repair-masked attribution, identify the components required for capability knockout, and yield repair-aware structured pruning scaling from 124M to 7B. Component importance is therefore not merely an isolated-unit property: in robust circuits, the components that matter can become visible only under the interventions that make them necessary.

</details>


### [198] [Hybrid quantum-classical neural network for sentiment analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01943)
*Giacomo Cappiello,Filippo Caruso,Xing Liang,Dimitrios Makris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了混合量子-经典神经网络在情感分析任务中的应用，使用TF-IDF向量化的COVID-19推文数据集。混合模型在准确率上与经典基线相当，但展现出更丰富的表示能力；在迁移学习到SMS垃圾邮件分类时，混合模型在垃圾邮件类别上准确率从66%提升至81%，显著超越经典模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子机器学习（QML）在自然语言处理中的情感分析任务上的可行性，验证混合量子-经典架构是否能在实际NLP任务中带来性能提升或更优的表示能力。

Method: 使用TF-IDF对COVID-19推文文本进行向量化，分别构建经典前馈神经网络和混合架构（集成参数化量子电路），并在SMS垃圾邮件分类上应用迁移学习进行评估。

Result: 混合模型在COVID-19推文情感分析任务上准确率与经典基线相当，但验证损失和准确率曲线表现出不同的学习动态。在迁移学习到SMS垃圾邮件分类时，混合模型在垃圾邮件类别上准确率从66%提升至81%，提升15个百分点。

Conclusion: 混合量子-经典模型在NLP任务中是可行的，展现出更强的泛化能力和表示能力，随着量子硬件的发展具有潜在优势。

Abstract: Quantum machine learning has recently emerged as a promising paradigm that leverages the expressive power of quantum circuits to address complex learning tasks. In this work, we investigate the applicability of hybrid quantum-classical neural networks to sentiment analysis, a central problem in natural language processing. We focus on a dataset of tweets related to COVID-19, where the textual content is vectorized using TF-IDF and fed into both classical feedforward networks and hybrid architectures incorporating parameterized quantum circuits. Our results show that hybrid models can achieve accuracy comparable to the classical baseline, while exhibiting distinct learning dynamics, especially in terms of validation loss and accuracy, that suggest a richer representational capacity. Moreover, when applying transfer learning to an SMS spam classification task, the hybrid models consistently outperform the classical counterpart, achieving an accuracy increase of 15 percentage points (from 66% to 81%) on the spam class, demonstrating enhanced generalization. These findings highlight the feasibility of employing QML for natural language processing and point toward the potential advantages of hybrid models as quantum hardware continues to advance.

</details>


### [199] [A More Accurate Algorithm Comparison through A/B Testing using Offline Evaluation Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01958)
*Koki Konishi,Masataka Ushiku,Yuta Saito*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现A/B测试在算法选择中可能比离线评估产生更高的选择错误率，并提出一种通过引入中间算法来诱导正相关、从而提升A/B测试选择精度的新估计方法，实验表明该方法可使用一半数据达到同等选择错误率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为离线评估精度低，最终算法选择依赖A/B测试。然而A/B测试代价高昂且存在风险，本文试图揭示A/B测试在算法选择中可能存在的反直觉缺陷，并探索改进方法。

Method: 提出一种引入假设中间算法的估计器，通过分步使用共享数据估计算法A、M、B之间性能差异，在每一步中应用离线评估技术以诱导正相关，从而减少关键选择错误。并通过偏差-方差分析推导最优中间算法。

Result: 实验表明，所提估计器在使用仅一半A/B测试数据的情况下，即可达到与现有方法相同的选择错误率。

Conclusion: A/B测试的样本均值估计器因无法诱导正相关而可能产生比离线评估更高的选择错误率；通过引入中间算法诱导正相关可显著提升A/B测试的算法选择效率。

Abstract: A/B testing is the gold standard for selecting the better algorithm in online services. While offline evaluation has attracted attention as a safer alternative due to the high experimental costs and the potential risk of degrading user experience and revenue in A/B testing, it is widely recognized that the estimation accuracy of offline evaluation is substantially lower. As a result, final selection decisions are typically made through A/B testing. Contrary to this conventional view, we reveal a counterintuitive phenomenon in which A/B testing can produce a higher algorithm selection error rate than offline evaluation. This occurs because the sample mean estimator used in A/B testing does not induce positive correlation, which is crucial for reducing critical selection errors, namely underestimating the truly superior algorithm and overestimating the truly inferior one. In contrast, offline evaluation methods unintentionally generate this beneficial correlation by relying on shared offline data when estimating and comparing the performance of multiple algorithms. Building on this insight, we propose an estimator that intentionally induces positive correlation to improve algorithm selection in A/B testing. The key idea is to introduce a hypothetical middle algorithm and to estimate the performance difference between algorithms A, M, and B in a stepwise manner using shared data at each step. This approach enables the application of offline evaluation techniques in each step, thereby inducing positive correlation and reducing critical selection errors. Furthermore, we derive the optimal middle algorithm regarding the resulting variance and analyze its advantages over existing methods through bias-variance analysis. Experiments on real-world data demonstrate that our estimator achieves the same selection error rate as existing approaches while using only one half of the A/B testing data.

</details>


### [200] [Probabilistic Low-Voltage Peak Load Forecasting with Time Series Foundation Models Evaluated on Application-Oriented Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01966)
*Benedikt Kaas,Manuel Treutlein,Hannes Benedikt Gerber,Oliver Neumann,Cheewan Phatthanakhuha,Oliver Resch,Ralf Mikut,Veit Hagenmeyer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对200个真实低压配电网馈线的短期净负荷预测进行了全面评估，重点比较了Chronos-Bolt、Chronos-2和TabPFN-TS等时序基础模型与六个基线模型，结果显示Chronos-2表现最优。消融实验表明时序基础模型能适应天气协变量缺失时的不确定性增加，并提出了一个连接峰值预测与电网资产规划中成本-风险权衡的应用导向指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低压负荷预测对高电气化和分布式发电的能源系统至关重要，但现有方法存在人工成本高、缺乏不确定性估计和峰值预测能力不足、未充分考虑电网需求评估等问题。

Method: 基于200个真实低压馈线数据，对Chronos-Bolt、Chronos-2、TabPFN-TS等时序基础模型与六个基线模型进行短期净负荷预测比较；通过去除天气协变量的消融实验研究模型对不确定性的适应能力；设计应用导向指标评估峰值预测与电网资产规划中成本-风险权衡的关系。

Result: Chronos-2在所有模型中表现最优；时序基础模型在缺少天气信息时仍能适应不确定性增加；提出的新指标有效关联了峰值预测能力与电网资产规划的权衡。

Conclusion: 时序基础模型在低压负荷预测中优于传统基线模型，能更好地处理不确定性，且新提出的应用导向指标为电网规划决策提供了有价值的评估工具。

Abstract: Low-voltage load forecasting is an important component in current and future energy systems with a high degree of electrification and decentralized generation. However, current forecasting methods require significant manual effort, often lack uncertainty estimation and proper peak prediction, and they are often not adequately evaluated in terms of grid requirements. In the present study, we provide an extensive evaluation of short-term net load forecasts of 200 real-world low-voltage feeders with a focus on the rapidly evolving time series foundation models. Our study compares Chronos-Bolt, Chronos-2 and TabPFN-TS to six baseline models and demonstrates superior performance, in particular for Chronos-2. An ablation study, in which weather covariates are omitted, shows that time series foundation models adapt to increased uncertainty, despite the importance of weather information. A novel application-oriented metric links the model's forecasting capabilities in peak prediction to the trade-off in grid asset planning and operation between cost reduction and minimizing the risk of failure.

</details>


### [201] [Do Newer Lightweight CNNs Perform Better Under Resource Constraints? A Controlled Multigenerational Study of Architecture, Initialization, Training Budget, and Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01984)
*Tasnim Shahriar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究在统一协议下系统比较了九种轻量级CNN模型，发现EfficientNet-B0在精度与资源消耗之间取得了最佳平衡，出现在所有评估的Pareto前沿上；而较新的模型设计仅提供选择性而非普遍性收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新型轻量级CNN常声称提升了预测性能和部署效率，但这些主张缺乏受控评估。现有研究常在不同协议下进行，难以进行公平比较，因此需要一项在统一下游协议下的系统性基准测试。

Method: 在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和Tiny ImageNet三个数据集上，采用共享的下游协议比较九种轻量级CNN模型包。评估指标包括top-1准确率、宏F1、top-5准确率、参数量、FP32存储、GMACs、NVIDIA L4 GPU和AMD Ryzen 5 5500U CPU上的batch-size-1延迟、PyTorch CUDA峰值分配张量内存，以及点估计Pareto前沿。

Result: EfficientNetV2-S在CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100上取得最高top-1准确率（97.57%和86.98%），RepViT-M1.0在Tiny ImageNet上领先（79.87%）。EfficientNet-B0与最佳结果差距极小（0.22/0.85/1.79个百分点），同时参数量减少约79%，GMACs减少约86%，并出现在所有Pareto前沿上。MobileNetV3-Small具有最低GMACs且CPU延迟最快，在所有数据集上准确率均高于MobileNetV4-Conv-S。SqueezeNet1.1参数量最少但精度明显较弱。GPU和CPU上的延迟排名差异显著，表明GMACs单独无法预测实际推理性能。

Conclusion: 较新的模型设计提供的是选择性而非普遍性收益；EfficientNet-B0作为中间预算选项最具竞争力；模型评估应结合多种资源指标和不同硬件环境，而非仅依赖GMACs；预训练对性能有重要影响，EfficientNet-B0从头训练100个epoch后与预训练版本差距显著。

Abstract: Newer lightweight convolutional neural networks are often presented as improving predictive performance and deployment efficiency, but such claims require controlled evaluation. This study compares nine lightweight CNN model packages across CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and Tiny ImageNet under a shared downstream protocol. We report top-1 accuracy, macro F1, top-5 accuracy, parameter count, FP32 storage, GMACs, batch-size-1 latency on an NVIDIA L4 and AMD Ryzen 5 5500U CPU, peak PyTorch CUDA allocated tensor memory, and point estimate Pareto frontiers. EfficientNetV2-S achieves the highest observed top-1 accuracy on CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100 at 97.57% and 86.98%, while RepViT-M1.0 leads Tiny ImageNet at 79.87%. EfficientNet-B0 remains within 0.22, 0.85, and 1.79 percentage points of the best result on the three datasets while using approximately 79% fewer parameters and 86% fewer GMACs than EfficientNetV2-S. It also appears on every evaluated accuracy and resource Pareto frontier, making it the most consistently competitive intermediate-budget option. MobileNetV3-Small has the lowest GMAC count, is the fastest model under both CPU thread settings, and records higher observed accuracy than MobileNetV4-Conv-S on all three datasets. Under random initialization, it leads MobileNetV4-Conv-S by 2.55, 1.76, and 0.99 points, with paired test-set intervals excluding zero for the fixed trained models. EfficientNet-B0 remains 3.29, 10.10, and 17.54 points below its pretrained counterpart after 100 epochs of scratch training, despite requiring about five times the recorded training time. SqueezeNet1.1 has the fewest parameters and lowest peak CUDA allocation, but substantially weaker accuracy. Latency rankings differ sharply between the L4 and CPU environments, showing that GMACs alone do not predict measured inference performance. Overall, newer designs provide selective rather than universal gains

</details>


### [202] [Liquid Latent State Dynamics for Interpretable Turbofan Degradation Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01986)
*Weizhi Nie,Weijie Wang,Yuting Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于液态神经网络（LNN）的潜变量动力学模型，用于航空发动机健康监测。通过将潜状态分解为退化分量和工况分量，并引入剩余寿命、单调风险、潜一致性等损失函数，实现了退化过程的解耦与可解释建模。在C-MAPSS四个子集上，传感器预测RMSE从GRU基线的0.2438降至0.2266，退化状态的速度Spearman相关性平均达0.5960。但直接RUL回归仍不及GRU基线，表明该模型更适合作为可解释的退化世界模型而非精确寿命回归器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多元时间序列预测模型多关注点预测精度，但其内部状态缺乏对退化过程的一致性与可解释性表征，难以分离健康退化与工况变化的影响。

Method: 使用液态神经网络作为潜动力学模型，编码历史窗口为潜状态，通过液态转移模型演化状态并解码未来传感器观测。潜状态分解为退化分量和工况分量；训练损失包括RUL回归、单调风险、潜一致性（退化分量），以及工况预测与去相关损失（工况分量）。

Result: 在C-MAPSS FD001-FD004上，全解耦模型的传感器预测RMSE为0.2266（GRU基线0.2438），多工况子集FD002/FD004提升最大；退化状态速度Spearman相关性平均0.5960；但直接RUL回归性能仍弱于GRU基线。

Conclusion: 液态潜动力学模型能够在预测性维护中桥接传感器预测与可解释的健康状态建模，其解耦表征更适合作为可解释的退化世界模型，而非高精度的剩余寿命回归器。

Abstract: Multivariate time-series models for prognostics are often evaluated by point prediction accuracy, yet their internal states rarely expose a coherent degradation process. We study liquid neural networks as latent dynamics models for aircraft engine health monitoring on the C-MAPSS benchmark. The proposed model encodes a history window into a latent state, evolves that state with a liquid transition model, and decodes future sensor observations. To separate health evolution from operating-condition variation, the latent state is factorized into degradation and condition components. Remaining useful life, monotonic risk, and latent-consistency losses supervise the degradation component, while condition prediction and decorrelation losses discourage operating-condition leakage. Across FD001--FD004, the full disentangled model improves overall sensor forecasting RMSE from 0.2438 for a GRU baseline to 0.2266, with the largest gains on the multi-condition subsets FD002 and FD004. The learned degradation state also forms a clearer temporal degradation axis, reaching an average state-speed Spearman correlation of 0.5960. Direct remaining-useful-life regression remains stronger for the GRU baseline, indicating that the proposed representation is currently more effective as an interpretable world model for degradation dynamics than as a calibrated lifetime regressor. These results suggest that liquid latent dynamics can bridge predictive maintenance forecasting and inspectable health-state modeling.

</details>


### [203] [Fast and Accurate Anomaly Detection in Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02046)
*Emanuele Mele,Massimo Cafaro,Angelo Coluccia,Italo Epicoco*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Haar离散小波和t检验的无监督时间序列异常检测算法，在343个数据集上优于现有无监督和自监督方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统异常检测方法面临两大挑战：有监督方法需要大量标注数据（成本高、耗时长），无监督方法则容易产生高误报率。现有的无监督算法在时间序列异常检测中性能不足，亟需一种既无需标注又能保持低误报率的高效算法。

Method: 提出一种基于Haar离散小波变换和专门设计的t检验的无监督异常检测算法。利用Haar小波对时间序列进行多尺度分解，结合t检验进行统计显著性检验，从而识别异常点。文中给出了所提t检验的理论基础证明。

Result: 在343个数据集上进行了广泛实验，结果表明该算法在性能上优于现有的无监督和自监督基准方法。

Conclusion: 基于Haar小波和t检验的无监督方法能够有效解决异常检测中的类不平衡和标注稀缺问题，同时降低误报率，适用于安全关键应用场景。

Abstract: Anomaly detection is a critical and evolving field in Machine Learning, with applications targeting different domains such as cybersecurity, finance, healthcare, manufacturing and IoT (Internet of Things) systems. Traditionally, anomaly detection algorithms have been designed using both supervised and unsupervised learning paradigms. The fundamental challenge in real-world anomaly detection scenarios is related to the inherent class imbalance (anomalies are typically rare) and, for supervised methods, to the scarcity of labelled anomalous data. Indeed, labelling is both expensive and time-consuming. Conversely unsupervised methods do not require labelling, but may suffer from high false positive rates when deployed in safety-critical applications. In this work we introduce a novel unsupervised algorithm for anomaly detection in time series based on the Haar discrete wavelet and a suitably designed $t$-test. We establish the theoretical foundation of the proposed $t$-test and, through extensive experimentation across 343 datasets, demonstrate that our algorithm outperforms state-of-the-art unsupervised and self-supervised benchmarks.

</details>


### [204] [A Memory Efficient Unified Algorithm for Online Learning of Linear Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02050)
*Yuval Ran-Milo,Angelos Assos,Elad Hazan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对一般未知线性动力系统（LDS）的在线预测问题，提出了一种统一算法，其可学习参数数量为$\widetilde{O}(k)$，其中$k$表示系统的不稳定复杂度（实稳定区间外的特征值个数及非半单模态数）。研究表明，仅当不稳定复杂度较小时预测才有意义，否则系统无法在不使用指数级大控制的情况下被稳定。实验表明，在相同参数预算下，该算法显著优于先前方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机源于从观测数据中稳定一般未知线性动力系统的挑战，其自然前提是实现在线预测。目标是实现次线性遗憾，同时内存占用适应动力学的内在复杂度，而非完整的隐藏状态维度。

Method: 提出一种统一的在线算法，可处理所有LDS（包括非对角化系统、复杂或爆炸模态），可学习参数数量为$\widetilde{O}(k)$。此外，证明了下界：任何基于滤波器的预测器至少需要$k$个滤波器。

Result: 理论结果表明，不稳定复杂度高的系统无法在不使用指数级大控制的情况下被稳定；提出的算法在低不稳定复杂度系统中有效；实验证明在相同参数预算下，该方法显著优于先前方法。

Conclusion: 预测对于稳定化有意义的前提是不稳定复杂度较小；$k$是有效的复杂度度量指标；本文算法实现了与内在复杂度匹配的参数效率。

Abstract: Motivated by the challenge of stabilizing a general unknown linear dynamical system (LDS) from observations, we study the natural prerequisite of online prediction. Our goal is to achieve sublinear regret with a memory footprint that adapts to the intrinsic complexity of the dynamics rather than the full hidden -- state dimension. We focus on the practically central regime of systems with low instability complexity -- eigenvalues outside the real stable interval that do not decay rapidly, together with non-semisimple modes-potentially embedded in an otherwise stable real spectrum of much higher dimension; we write $k$ for this count. This regime is the primary setting in which stabilization is plausible: we show that many systems with high instability complexity cannot be stabilized without exponentially large controls. Thus, prediction is meaningful for stabilization precisely when the instability complexity is small. Within this regime, we introduce a unified online algorithm that handles every LDS (including non-diagonalizable systems with complex or exploding modes) with a learnable parameter count of $\widetilde{O}(k)$. Finally, we prove a lower bound showing that $k$ is a valid complexity measure: any filter-based predictor needs at least $k$ filters. Experiments corroborate our theory: on a high-dimensional system, our predictor sharply outperforms prior methods at an equal parameter budget.

</details>


### [205] [Beyond the Performance Illusion: Structure-Aware Stratified Partitioning and Curriculum Distributionally Robust Optimization for Spatially Correlated Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02055)
*Prathamesh Patil,Arpit Jain,Aswanth Krishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出在时空相关领域（如航拍、精准农业、医学影像）中，随机数据集划分会破坏i.i.d.假设，导致数据泄露和隐藏分层问题。为此提出统一框架：SASP（结构感知分层划分）减少时空泄露并保持类别平衡，CDRO（课程分布鲁棒优化）稳定优化。实验表明该方法改善了泛化能力、置信度校准，并暴露了传统评估隐藏的失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI系统性能评估普遍假设随机数据集划分产生独立同分布子集，但这一假设在时空相关领域（如航拍、精准农业、医学影像）往往不成立，导致数据泄露和隐藏分层两种系统性失败，因此需要更可靠的评估与训练框架。

Method: 提出统一评估与训练框架：1) SASP（Structure-Aware Stratified Partitioning）——构建减少时空泄露同时保持有意义类别平衡的验证划分；2) CDRO（Curriculum Distributionally Robust Optimization）——基于课程的分布鲁棒训练松弛方法，在更严格的划分下稳定优化。

Result: 在多个基准测试上，该组合方法持续提升泛化性能，实现更可靠的置信度校准，并暴露了传统随机划分评估下隐藏的失败模式。

Conclusion: 时空相关数据下的随机划分假设不可靠，所提出的SASP+CDRO框架能有效改善评估可靠性和模型鲁棒性，应成为此类场景下的标准实践。

Abstract: Performance evaluation in AI systems commonly assumes that random dataset splits produce independent and identically distributed (i.i.d.) subsets. We show that this assumption often breaks down in spatiotemporally correlated domains such as aerial surveillance, precision agriculture, and medical imaging, leading to two systematic failures: data leakage, where correlated samples span training and validation splits and inflate performance estimates, and hidden stratification, where errors on minority subpopulations are obscured by aggregate metrics. To address these issues, we propose a unified evaluation and training framework for spatially correlated data. We introduce Structure-Aware Stratified Partitioning (SASP), which constructs validation splits that reduce spatiotemporal leakage while preserving meaningful class balance, and Curriculum Distributionally Robust Optimization (CDRO), a curriculum-based relaxation of distributionally robust training that stabilizes optimization under these stricter splits. Across multiple benchmarks, this combination yields consistently improved generalization, more reliable confidence calibration, and exposes failure modes that remain hidden under conventional random-split evaluation.

</details>


### [206] [SA-HGNN: Sample-Adaptive Hyperbolic Graph Neural Network for EEG-Based Depression Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02063)
*Yang Li,Pan Hu,Yan Zhang,Wenfan Yang,Tao Wu,Lianbo Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出样本自适应双曲图神经网络（SA-HGNN），通过动态构建个性化脑网络拓扑、双曲图卷积捕获层次结构以及注意力池化去噪，在EEG抑郁症识别中取得优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抑郁症患者脑网络的功能连接具有内在的层次结构，现有GNN方法难以准确捕获这种复杂连接模式。

Method: 提出SA-HGNN模型，包含三个核心模块：1）样本自适应图构建模块动态生成个性化脑网络拓扑；2）双曲图卷积利用双曲几何精确捕获脑网络中的潜在层次关系；3）注意力池化模块自适应过滤EEG信号中的高冗余噪声通道。

Result: 在公开EEG数据集上的实验表明，该方法在静息态和任务态范式下均表现出优越性能，验证了对噪声的鲁棒性和捕获异常功能连接模式的有效性。

Conclusion: SA-HGNN通过双曲空间图表示学习有效克服了欧氏空间的表示瓶颈，能够精确提取抑郁症脑网络的真实层次结构，为基于EEG的抑郁症识别提供了新方法。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have been widely used to capture spatial functional connectivity patterns to improve electroencephalography (EEG)-based depression recognition performance. However, the functional connectivity of brain networks in patients with depression exhibits an inherent hierarchical structure, making it difficult to capture accurate connection patterns. To address these issues, this paper proposes a novel model named Sample-Adaptive Hyperbolic Graph Neural Network (SA-HGNN), which aims to accurately extract the authentic hierarchical structure of depression-affected brain networks. Specifically, the proposed model comprises three core modules. First, a Sample-Adaptive Graph Construction module dynamically constructs personalized brain network topologies to capture more complex spatial relationships within the brain network. Second, hyperbolic graph convolution is employed to overcome the representation bottlenecks of Euclidean space, leveraging hyperbolic geometry to precisely capture latent hierarchical relationships within the brain network. Finally, an Attention Pooling module adaptively filters out highly redundant noise channels in EEG signals, effectively mitigating the interference of inherent noise on the authentic hierarchical topology. Extensive experiments on public EEG datasets demonstrate the superior performance of our method across resting-state and task-related paradigms, validating its robustness to noise and efficacy in capturing abnormal functional connectivity patterns in brain networks of patients with depression.

</details>


### [207] [kNNGuard: Turning LLM Hidden Activations into a Training-Free Configurable Guardrail](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02072)
*Mahmoud Abdelfattah,Hamid Nasiri,Peter Garraghan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: kNNGuard是一种无需训练的防护栏方法，利用现成LLM的激活空间，通过多层kNN融合激活空间和嵌入空间分数进行分类，在六个领域取得与微调方法相当或更优的F1值，运行速度快2.7-10倍，域适应仅需不到10秒构建标注样本库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防护栏主要依赖微调构建分类器，存在泛化能力差、推理延迟高的问题。

Method: 利用现成LLM的激活空间，构建50个安全/不安全提示的小样本库，提取隐层激活并进行多层kNN融合激活空间和嵌入空间分数进行分类。

Result: 在六个领域（涵盖主题和安全提示）中取得与微调最先进防护栏相当或更优的F1值，运行速度比最佳可比防护栏快2.7倍，比微调安全分类器快10倍；域适应可在10秒内完成。

Conclusion: kNNGuard作为一种无需训练、低延迟、可配置的防护栏方法，能够高效集成到生产LLM管线中，域适应成本极低。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in domains requiring guardrails to detect unsafe, off-topic, or adversarial prompts. Existing guardrails predominately rely on fine-tuning to build classifiers, which often suffer from low generalization and high inference latency. We present kNNGuard, a training-free guardrail that utilizes the activation space of an off-the-shelf LLM. Given a small bank of 50 safe and unsafe prompts, kNNGuard extracts hidden activations and performs multi-layer kNN fusing activation-space and embedding-space scores for classification. Across six domains spanning topical and security prompts, kNNGuard achieves competitive or superior F1 compared to fine-tuned state-of-the-art guardrails while running 2.7x faster than the best comparable guardrail, and 10x faster than a fine-tuned safety classifier without gradient updates or fine-tuning. Domain adaptation requires only updating the labeled bank, which can be constructed in under 10 seconds and several orders of magnitude faster than established guardrails. We also analyze the impact of system prompts, layer selection, and integration into production LLM pipelines as a configurable, low-latency guardrail.

</details>


### [208] [Fourier Neural Operators for Rayleigh-Bénard Convection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02088)
*Chelsea Maria John,Thibaut Lunet,Sebastian Götschel,Andreas Herten,Stefan Kesselheim,Daniel Ruprecht*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种改进的傅里叶神经算子（FNO），通过预测时间增量而非完整解来建模二维瑞利-贝纳德对流，在保持紧凑模型尺寸（314k参数，1.26 MB）和快速推理（7 ms）的同时，实现了比标准FNO基线更高的精度。研究表明FNO可泛化到更精细网格，但精度受限于训练数据分辨率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有标准FNO在建模二维瑞利-贝纳德对流时精度有限，需要更高效、更精确的神经算子方法来提升流体动力学模拟的预测性能。

Method: 改进FNO架构，采用预测时间增量而非完整解的策略进行训练和推理。

Result: 模型仅314k参数（1.26 MB），推理速度7 ms，精度优于标准FNO基线；FNO可泛化到更精细网格，但精度受训练数据分辨率限制。

Conclusion: 改进FNO通过预测时间增量有效提升了对流建模的精度与效率，但训练数据分辨率是进一步提升精度的关键瓶颈。

Abstract: We propose an improved Fourier Neural Operator (FNO) for modeling two-dimensional Rayleigh-Bénard convection by predicting time increments instead of full solutions, achieving higher accuracy than a standard FNO baseline. The resulting model is compact (314k parameters, 1.26 MB) and fast (7 ms inference), while maintaining similar accuracy as demonstrated in previous benchmarks. We show that although FNOs generalize to finer meshes, accuracy remains limited by the resolution of the training data.

</details>


### [209] [Ask the Right Comparison:Bias-Aware Bayesian Active Top-$k$ Ranking with LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02104)
*Jian Xu,Delu Zeng,John Paisley,Qibin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种贝叶斯推断框架来校正LLM作为评判者时的系统性偏差（如冗长偏好、位置效应），并引入top-k感知的主动获取策略，在有限比较预算下高效识别top-k项目。实验表明，该方法能显著提升廉价/中端模型的召回率（从0.5-0.6提升至0.84-1.0），而对前沿模型影响不大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为评判者存在噪声和系统性偏差（偏好冗长/格式好的回答、位置效应），简单投票聚合无法恢复真实质量排序。现有方法未能在有限比较预算下同时处理偏差校正和高效采样的问题。

Method: 1) 将评判建模为潜在质量上的贝叶斯推断，引入显式的评判者特定偏差协变量（冗长、位置），通过收缩先验正则化让数据决定偏差强度；2) 提出top-k感知的主动获取规则，选择能最大程度减少top-k成员不确定性的下一次比较。

Result: 在16个真实LLM（Llama、Qwen、GPT-4o-mini/5.1/5.5、Gemini、DeepSeek、Claude系列）的基准上，偏置感知模型能从错误top-k中恢复，top-k感知获取策略比轮询和D-optimal规则更快达到天花板。廉价/中端模型召回率从~0.5-0.6提升至0.84-1.0，前沿模型本身偏差小，校正效果有限。

Conclusion: 偏差是真实存在且异质的，取决于模型能力：偏差感知建模对廉价/中端模型至关重要，而前沿模型已足够准确；结合偏差校正和top-k感知采样是有限预算下高效可靠评判的有效方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as cheap, scalable judges that compare candidate outputs pairwise -- to rank responses, select models, or triage papers. Yet LLM judges are both noisy and systematically biased: they favor verbose or well-formatted answers and exhibit position effects, so simply aggregating their votes recovers a ranking of presentation, not of true quality. We study the practical goal of identifying the \topk{} items under a fixed comparison budget, and make two contributions. First, we cast judging as Bayesian inference over latent quality with explicit, judge-specific bias covariates (verbosity, position), regularized by a shrinkage prior so that the data decide which biases a given judge actually exhibits. Second, we introduce a \topk-aware active acquisition rule that chooses the next comparison to maximally reduce uncertainty about \topk{} \emph{membership}, rather than about the full ranking. On a controlled benchmark with known ground-truth quality, judged by sixteen real LLMs spanning open and proprietary families (Llama, Qwen, Phi-4, GPT-4o-mini/5.1/5.5, Gemini, DeepSeek, and Claude Haiku/Sonnet/Opus), naive aggregation plateaus at a wrong \topk{} on biased judges regardless of budget, while our bias-aware model recovers it; \topk-aware acquisition reaches this ceiling with far fewer comparisons than round-robin or a global-uncertainty (D-optimal) rule. Bias is real but heterogeneous and capability-dependent: cheap and mid-tier judges carry a strong verbosity bias that our model corrects (lifting recall from $\sim$$0.5$--$0.6$ to $0.84$--$1.0$), whereas the frontier judges we tested show little bias and already rank accurately, so bias-aware modeling changes little there.

</details>


### [210] [Predictive Conformal Slip Monitoring: An Empirical Evaluation of Rolling Split Conformal Prediction for Pre-Incident Traction Loss Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02124)
*Varshith Roy Kotla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探究了基于滚动分裂共形预测的牵引力失控预警方法，通过监测驾驶员滑移行为随机森林模型残差的波动性，试图在轮胎附着力极限被突破前提供统计预警信号。然而，在19名车手、55,563个遥测样本上的实验结果表明，该方法对14个真实事故标签的精确率和召回率均为0.0，虚警率达15.3%，静态95分位数基线也无优势，且分裂共形交换性假设在所有车手上均被违反（Ljung-Box p < 0.001）。研究最终报告了这一方法严格的负面发现，诊断了原因并展望了真正可预测版本所需条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统牵引力控制架构只能在轮胎附着力极限被突破后进行干预，缺乏基于统计的事前预警信号。本文尝试探究滚动分裂共形预测能否通过监测残差波动性，提供早于牵引力完全丧失的预警信号。

Method: 使用基于每位车手随机森林滑移行为模型的滚动分裂共形预测，监测非一致性残差的波动性。相比于早期内部草稿，修正了滑移代理变量中的混杂（车速作为显式模型特征），使用了每位车手的每一圈而非仅最快圈，并基于FIA赛事控制信息和赛道限制圈数删除的真实时间戳事故标签进行评分。

Result: 在19名车手、55,563个测试阶段遥测样本上，滚动波动性检测器对14个真实事故标签的平均精确率为0.0，平均召回率为0.0，却将平均15.3%的样本标记为异常。静态95百分位数基线也未显示任何能够证明共形波动性公式复杂性的优势。残差自相关诊断显示，所有19名车手的分裂共形交换性假设均被违反（Ljung-Box p < 0.001）。

Conclusion: 该方法在当前设定下不可行，虚警率过高而无法用于任何早期预警用途。论文报告了这一严格的负面发现，诊断了高虚警率的一个可能原因是交换性假设被违反，并概述了构建真正可预测版本所需的条件。

Abstract: Conventional traction control architectures intervene only after the adhesion limit of a tire has already been breached. This paper investigates whether Rolling Split Conformal Prediction , monitoring the volatility of non-conformity residuals from a per-driver Random Forest model of expected slip behavior , can serve as a statistically grounded pre-incident warning signal, ahead of gross traction loss. Unlike an earlier internal draft of this work, the evaluation reported here corrects a confound in the slip proxy (vehicle speed is included as an explicit model feature, not left implicit in the target's denominator), uses every racing lap for each driver rather than only the fastest lap, and is scored against real, timestamped incident labels extracted from FIA Race Control Messages and track-limits lap deletions rather than narrated post-hoc. The result is negative: across 19 drivers and 55,563 test-phase telemetry samples, the rolling-volatility detector achieves a mean precision of essentially 0.0 and mean recall of 0.0 against 14 ground-truth incidents, while flagging on average 15.3% of all samples as anomalous , too high a false-alarm rate for any early-warning use. A static 95th-percentile threshold baseline performs no better in any way that would justify the added complexity of the conformal-volatility formulation. Residual autocorrelation diagnostics show the split-conformal exchangeability assumption is violated for every driver (Ljung-Box p < 0.001, n = 19/19), which is one plausible driver of the high false-alarm rate. We report this as a methodologically rigorous negative finding, diagnose its likely causes, and outline what a genuinely predictive version of this approach would require.

</details>


### [211] [ART for Diffusion Sampling: Continuous-Time Control and Actor-Critic Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02137)
*Yilie Huang,Wenpin Tang,Xun Yu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ART-RL，一种通过强化学习为扩散模型采样自适应分配时间步的方法，能在保持采样管道不变的情况下持续提升样本质量，且学到的调度策略具有良好的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型采样中的时间步分配采用均匀或手工设计的固定调度方案，这些方案缺乏自适应性，可能导致次优的采样效果。

Method: 提出ART（自适应重参数化时间）连续时间控制框架，通过将采样时钟速度作为控制变量来学习时间变换；进一步引入ART-RL，通过高斯策略将调度学习转化为连续时间强化学习问题，并推导出可实现的Actor-Critic更新规则。

Result: 在低维控制实验到图像生成任务中，ART-RL仅通过改变时间步网格即可提升现有扩散采样器的样本质量，优于强基线调度方案；学到的调度策略无需重新训练即可泛化到不同采样预算、数据集、求解器、管道和表示空间。

Conclusion: ART-RL提供了一种即插即用的自适应时间步分配方案，在保持采样管道其余部分不变的情况下持续提升质量，且调度策略具有良好的跨场景泛化能力。

Abstract: We study timestep allocation for score-based diffusion sampling, where a learned reverse-time dynamics is discretized on a finite grid. Uniform and hand-crafted schedules are standard choices, but they rely on fixed prescriptions and can therefore be suboptimal. To address this limitation, we propose Adaptive Reparameterized Time (ART), a continuous-time control formulation that learns a time change by treating the speed of the sampling clock as the control, so that a uniform grid on the learned clock induces adaptive timesteps in the original diffusion time. Based on a leading-order Euler error surrogate, ART provides a principled objective for allocating timesteps along the sampling trajectory. To solve this deterministic control problem, we introduce ART-RL, an auxiliary randomized formulation with Gaussian policies that turns schedule learning into a continuous-time reinforcement learning problem. We prove that the randomized ART-RL formulation is equivalent to ART at the optimizer level, in the sense that its optimal Gaussian policy recovers the optimal ART time-warping rate through its mean. We further establish policy evaluation and policy improvement characterizations and derive trajectory-based moment identities that yield implementable actor--critic updates for learning the schedule. Across experiments ranging from controlled low-dimensional settings to image generation, ART-RL can be plugged into existing diffusion samplers by changing only the timestep grid, consistently improving sample quality over strong baseline schedules at matched budgets while leaving the rest of the sampling pipeline unchanged. The learned schedules also exhibit broad generalization, transferring without retraining across sampling budgets, datasets, solvers, pipelines, and representation spaces.

</details>


### [212] [Probing Chemical Language Models: Effects of Pre-training and Fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02140)
*Anna Karnysheva,Dietrich Klakow,Ji-Ung Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了化学语言模型（CLMs）对78种分子子结构的编码能力，发现预训练能提升模型对分子结构的感知（尤其是上层），随机初始化模型在第一层就能很好编码环状结构，而微调会更有针对性地影响与任务相关的子结构，符合化学理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学语言模型使用SMILES等线性化表示进行训练，但学界对其内部编码了哪些具有化学意义的子结构仍不清楚。为增进对CLMs的理解，有必要进行系统性的探测研究。

Method: 对8个预训练和6个随机初始化的化学语言模型进行系统探测，针对78种分子子结构进行探针分析；同时研究微调对两种化学下游任务中分子子结构表示的影响。

Result: 预训练普遍提升了CLMs的分子结构感知能力，尤其在上层表现明显；随机初始化模型在第一层已能很好地编码环状结构；微调对任务相关子结构的影响大于非相关子结构。

Conclusion: 预训练增强了化学语言模型对分子子结构的编码能力，且微调过程中表示的变化遵循化学理论，即模型会针对性地调整与任务相关的子结构表示。

Abstract: Chemical language models (CLMs) are trained with linearized representations such as SMILES, yet it remains unclear which chemically meaningful substructures they encode. To foster a better understanding of CLMs, we conduct a systematic study and probe for 78 molecular substructures across eight pre-trained and six randomly initialized models.
